FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/AppScanner.kt

package com.example

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.content.pm.ResolveInfo
import android.graphics.drawable.Drawable
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

data class AppInfo(
    val name: String,
    val packageName: String,
    val icon: Drawable,
    val isEmulator: Boolean = false
)

class AppScanner(private val context: Context) {
    
    // Known emulator package keywords for categorization
    private val emulatorKeywords = listOf(
        "retroarch", "emulator", "drastic", "citra", "azahar", "dolphin", 
        "ppsspp", "yuzu", "suyu", "sudachi", "skyline", "eden", "aethersx2",
        "nethersx2", "duckstation", "mupen64", "m64plus", "fzurita", "lime3ds",
        "mandarine", "pizzaboy", "myboy", "myoldboy", "snes9x", "superretro",
        "flycast", "redream", "vita3k", "melonds", "yaba", "epsxe", "fpse",
        "lemuroid", "classicboy", "nes.emu", "gba.emu", "gbc.emu", "md.emu",
        "snes9xex", "mame4droid", "neo.emu", "pce.emu"
    )

    suspend fun getInstalledApps(): List<AppInfo> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val pm = context.packageManager
        val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_MAIN, null).apply {
            addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_LAUNCHER)
        }
        
        val resolveInfos: List<ResolveInfo> = pm.queryIntentActivities(intent, 0)
        
        resolveInfos.mapNotNull { resolveInfo ->
            try {
                val packageName = resolveInfo.activityInfo.packageName
                // Skip our own app
                if (packageName == context.packageName) return@mapNotNull null
                
                val name = resolveInfo.loadLabel(pm).toString()
                val icon = resolveInfo.loadIcon(pm)
                
                val isEmulator = emulatorKeywords.any { 
                    packageName.lowercase().contains(it) || name.lowercase().contains(it)
                }
                
                AppInfo(
                    name = name,
                    packageName = packageName,
                    icon = icon,
                    isEmulator = isEmulator
                )
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                null
            }
        }.sortedBy { it.name }
    }
    
    fun launchApp(packageName: String) {
        try {
            val launchIntent = context.packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(packageName)
            if (launchIntent != null) {
                launchIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                context.startActivity(launchIntent)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
        }
    }
}

package com.example

import android.net.Uri
import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.activity.compose.rememberLauncherForActivityResult

import androidx.compose.animation.slideOutHorizontally
import androidx.compose.animation.core.animateFloatAsState

import androidx.compose.animation.core.RepeatMode
import androidx.compose.animation.core.LinearEasing

fun getPlatformImageUrl(context: android.content.Context, platform: String, assetRootUriStr: String? = null): String {
    val fallback = com.example.ui.theme.PlatformVisuals.getFastFallbackIconModel(context, platform)
    if (fallback is String) {
        return fallback
    }
    val style = com.example.ui.theme.PlatformVisuals.getStyle(platform)
    return style.bannerUrl.ifBlank { "https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/14/Video_Game_Controller_Icon.svg/1200px-Video_Game_Controller_Icon.svg.png" }
}

@Composable
fun GameLauncherScreen(viewModel: GameViewModel = viewModel()) {
    val currentThemeFamilyIndex by viewModel.currentThemeFamilyIndex.collectAsState()
    val isDarkTheme by viewModel.isDarkTheme.collectAsState()
    val customWallpaper by viewModel.customWallpaperUri.collectAsState()
    val selectedGame by viewModel.selectedGame.collectAsState()
    val glassOpacity by viewModel.glassOpacity.collectAsState()

    val currentThemeFamily = ThemeFamilies.getOrElse(currentThemeFamilyIndex) { ThemeFamilies.first() }
    val currentTheme = if (isDarkTheme) currentThemeFamily.dark else currentThemeFamily.light

    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalPegasusTheme provides currentTheme) {
        androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme(colorScheme = currentTheme.toMaterialColorScheme()) {
            var showSplashScreen by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }

            if (showSplashScreen) {
                com.example.ui.screens.SylphoraSplashScreen(
                    onFinished = { showSplashScreen = false }
                )
            } else {
            val context = LocalContext.current
        val appScanner = remember { AppScanner(context) }
        var installedApps by remember { mutableStateOf<List<AppInfo>>(emptyList()) }
        var isLoadingApps by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }

        val folderPickerLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
            contract = ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocumentTree()
        ) { uri: Uri? ->
            uri?.let { viewModel.startRomScan(it) }
        }

        var showGameDetail by remember { mutableStateOf<com.example.database.GameEntity?>(null) }
        var showProfileDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

        val navController = rememberNavController()
        val navBackStackEntry by navController.currentBackStackEntryAsState()
        val currentRoute = navBackStackEntry?.destination?.route ?: "home"
        val currentRouteRef by rememberUpdatedState(currentRoute)

        var dockInteractionToken by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }

        BackHandler(enabled = currentRoute != "home") {
            navController.navigate("home") {
                popUpTo("home") { inclusive = false }
            }
        }

        val sfxEnabled by viewModel.isSfxEnabled.collectAsState()
        val bgmEnabled by viewModel.isBgmEnabled.collectAsState()
        val bgmVolume by viewModel.bgmVolume.collectAsState()

        LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
            installedApps = appScanner.getInstalledApps()
            isLoadingApps = false
        }

        LaunchedEffect(sfxEnabled, bgmEnabled, bgmVolume) {
            SoundManager.init(sfxEnabled, bgmEnabled, bgmVolume)
        }

        // Hardware Gamepad Navigation Event Listener
        LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
            GlobalGamepadEvents.collect { keyCode ->
                val route = currentRouteRef
                when (keyCode) {
                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MENU, KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BUTTON_START -> {
                        dockInteractionToken++
                    }
                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BUTTON_B -> {
                        if (route != "home") {
                            dockInteractionToken++
                            SoundManager.playBackSound(context)
                            navController.navigate("home") {
                                popUpTo("home") { inclusive = false }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BUTTON_X -> {
                        dockInteractionToken++
                        SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                        navController.navigate("library") {
                            popUpTo("home") { saveState = true }
                            launchSingleTop = true
                        }
                    }
                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BUTTON_Y -> {
                        dockInteractionToken++
                        SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                        navController.navigate("ai") {
                            popUpTo("home") { saveState = true }
                            launchSingleTop = true
                        }
                    }
                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BUTTON_L1 -> {
                        dockInteractionToken++
                        SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                        val routes = listOf("home", "library", "ai", "research", "tools")
                        val currentIdx = routes.indexOf(route)
                        val prevIdx = if (currentIdx > 0) currentIdx - 1 else routes.size - 1
                        navController.navigate(routes[prevIdx]) {
                            popUpTo("home") { saveState = true }
                            launchSingleTop = true
                        }
                    }
                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BUTTON_R1 -> {
                        dockInteractionToken++
                        SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                        val routes = listOf("home", "library", "ai", "research", "tools")
                        val currentIdx = routes.indexOf(route)
                        val nextIdx = (currentIdx + 1) % routes.size
                        navController.navigate(routes[nextIdx]) {
                            popUpTo("home") { saveState = true }
                            launchSingleTop = true
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(currentTheme.background)
        ) {
            // Background Wallpaper: Selected Game Background > Custom Wallpaper > Fallback
            val activeBackground = if (currentRoute == "home") {
                selectedGame?.backgroundPath ?: selectedGame?.coverPath ?: customWallpaper
            } else {
                customWallpaper
            }
            
            if (!activeBackground.isNullOrBlank()) {
                androidx.compose.animation.Crossfade(targetState = activeBackground, label = "bg_crossfade") { bg ->
                    AsyncImage(
                        model = bg,
                        contentDescription = "Background",
                        contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                    )
                }
                // Frosted Glass Tint Overlay
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .background(currentTheme.background.copy(alpha = glassOpacity))
                )
            } else {
                // Keep the existing Sylphora background composition, but add a
                // very restrained atmospheric layer so the scene feels alive
                // without changing the established layout. Other themes keep
                // their own existing background treatment.
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .background(
                            Brush.radialGradient(
                                colors = listOf(
                                    currentTheme.accent.copy(alpha = if (currentTheme.isDark) 0.15f else 0.08f),
                                    currentTheme.background
                                ),
                                radius = 1000f
                            )
                        )
                )
                if (currentThemeFamilyIndex == 0) {
                    SylphoraAmbientAtmosphere(
                        isDark = currentTheme.isDark,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                    )
                }
            }

            // Foreground Main App Shell: Router Navigation fills the entire container
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .windowInsetsPadding(WindowInsets.safeDrawing)
            ) {
                NavHost(navController = navController, startDestination = "home") {
                    composable(
                        "home",
                        enterTransition = { fadeIn(tween(200)) + slideInHorizontally(initialOffsetX = { it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(200)) },
                        exitTransition = { fadeOut(tween(150)) + slideOutHorizontally(targetOffsetX = { -it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(150)) }
                    ) {
                        HomeBody(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            currentTheme = currentTheme,
                            currentThemeFamilyIndex = currentThemeFamilyIndex,
                            folderPickerLauncher = folderPickerLauncher,
                            onOpenProfile = { showProfileDialog = true },
                            onOpenSettings = {
                                navController.navigate("tools") { launchSingleTop = true }
                            },
                            onShowGameDetail = { game -> showGameDetail = game }
                        )
                    }
                    composable(
                        "library",
                        enterTransition = { fadeIn(tween(200)) + slideInHorizontally(initialOffsetX = { it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(200)) },
                        exitTransition = { fadeOut(tween(150)) + slideOutHorizontally(targetOffsetX = { -it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(150)) }
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxSize()
                        ) {
                            if (isLoadingApps) {
                                Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                    CircularProgressIndicator(color = currentTheme.accent)
                                }
                            } else {
                                LibraryScreen(
                                    viewModel = viewModel,
                                    installedApps = installedApps,
                                    onLaunchApp = { pkg -> appScanner.launchApp(pkg) }
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    composable(
                        "ai",
                        enterTransition = { fadeIn(tween(200)) + slideInHorizontally(initialOffsetX = { it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(200)) },
                        exitTransition = { fadeOut(tween(150)) + slideOutHorizontally(targetOffsetX = { -it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(150)) }
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxSize()
                        ) {
                            if (currentThemeFamilyIndex == 5) {
                                com.example.ui.theme.CyberpunkRobotCurator(viewModel = viewModel, theme = currentTheme)
                            } else {
                                CuratorScreen(viewModel = viewModel)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    composable(
                        "research",
                        enterTransition = { fadeIn(tween(200)) + slideInHorizontally(initialOffsetX = { it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(200)) },
                        exitTransition = { fadeOut(tween(150)) + slideOutHorizontally(targetOffsetX = { -it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(150)) }
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxSize()
                        ) {
                            com.example.ui.screens.ResearchScreen(viewModel = viewModel)
                        }
                    }
                    composable(
                        "tools",
                        enterTransition = { fadeIn(tween(200)) + slideInHorizontally(initialOffsetX = { it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(200)) },
                        exitTransition = { fadeOut(tween(150)) + slideOutHorizontally(targetOffsetX = { -it / 4 }, animationSpec = tween(150)) }
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxSize()
                        ) {
                            SettingsScreen(viewModel = viewModel, onClose = { navController.navigate("home") { popUpTo("home") { inclusive = false }; launchSingleTop = true } })
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Floating navigation dock & swipe trigger
            DockAndNav(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                currentTheme = currentTheme,
                themeIndex = currentThemeFamilyIndex,
                currentRoute = currentRoute,
                dockInteractionToken = dockInteractionToken,
                onNavigate = { route ->
                    navController.navigate(route) {
                        popUpTo("home") { saveState = true }
                        launchSingleTop = true
                        restoreState = true
                    }
                },
                onTriggerDock = { dockInteractionToken++ },
                modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
            )
            
            showGameDetail?.let { game ->
                com.example.ui.screens.GameMuseumDetailDialog(
                    game = game,
                    onDismiss = { showGameDetail = null },
                    viewModel = viewModel
                )
            }

            if (showProfileDialog) {
                PlayerProfileDialog(
                    viewModel = viewModel,
                    onDismiss = { showProfileDialog = false }
                )
            }

            val activeEmulatorPicker by viewModel.activeEmulatorPicker.collectAsState()
            val activeNoEmulatorDialog by viewModel.activeNoEmulatorDialog.collectAsState()

            if (activeEmulatorPicker != null) {
                com.example.ui.screens.EmulatorSelectionDialog(
                    state = activeEmulatorPicker!!,
                    onDismiss = { viewModel.activeEmulatorPicker.value = null },
                    onSelectLauncher = { launcherId, setAsDefault ->
                        viewModel.launchGameWithLauncher(context, activeEmulatorPicker!!.game, launcherId, setAsDefault)
                    }
                )
            }

            if (activeNoEmulatorDialog != null) {
                com.example.ui.screens.NoEmulatorDialog(
                    game = activeNoEmulatorDialog!!,
                    onDismiss = { viewModel.activeNoEmulatorDialog.value = null }
                )
            }
        }
    }
}
}
}

@Composable
private fun HomeBody(
    viewModel: GameViewModel,
    currentTheme: PegasusTheme,
    currentThemeFamilyIndex: Int,
    folderPickerLauncher: androidx.activity.result.ActivityResultLauncher<Uri?>,
    onOpenProfile: () -> Unit,
    onOpenSettings: () -> Unit,
    onShowGameDetail: (com.example.database.GameEntity) -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val games by viewModel.allGames.collectAsState()
    val isScanning by viewModel.isScanning.collectAsState()
    val scanProgressText by viewModel.scanProgressText.collectAsState()
    val assetRootUri by viewModel.assetRootUri.collectAsState()
    val userProfile by viewModel.userProfile.collectAsState()
    val fontScale by viewModel.fontSize.collectAsState()

    var selectedIndex by rememberSaveable { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    var selectedPlatform by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(
                horizontal = if (currentThemeFamilyIndex == 0 || currentThemeFamilyIndex == 10) 0.dp else 18.dp,
                vertical = if (currentThemeFamilyIndex == 0 || currentThemeFamilyIndex == 10) 0.dp else 2.dp
            )
    ) {
        // Top Rail (Header) on Home (Omitted in Sylphora & Switch 2 themes as they have their own integrated capsule/top bar)
        if (currentThemeFamilyIndex != 0 && currentThemeFamilyIndex != 10) {
            TopRail(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                themeIndex = currentThemeFamilyIndex,
                onOpenProfile = onOpenProfile
            )
        }

        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f)) {
            if (isScanning) {
                Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                    Column(
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                            .background(currentTheme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.85f))
                            .border(1.dp, currentTheme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.3f), RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                            .padding(36.dp)
                    ) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = currentTheme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp))
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(20.dp))
                        Text("AI 索引引擎運作中", color = currentTheme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 18.sp)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                        Text(scanProgressText, color = currentTheme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    }
                }
            } else if (games.isEmpty()) {
                if (currentThemeFamilyIndex == 0) {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        Column(
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
                                .background(currentTheme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                                .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f), RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
                                .clickable { folderPickerLauncher.launch(null) }
                                .padding(40.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.FolderOpen, contentDescription = null, tint = currentTheme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(64.dp))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                            Text("歡迎使用 Sylphora", color = currentTheme.textMain, fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                            Text("選擇 ROM 根目錄開始建立遊戲索引", color = currentTheme.textSecondary, fontSize = 14.sp)
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    ThemeEmptyState(currentTheme, currentThemeFamilyIndex) { folderPickerLauncher.launch(null) }
                }
            } else {
                val platformGroups = remember(games) {
                    games.groupBy { it.platform.trim().ifBlank { "UNKNOWN" } }.toSortedMap(String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER)
                }

                val isPlatformView = selectedPlatform == null
                val displayedGames = if (isPlatformView) {
                    // Generate fake GameEntity objects to represent platforms
                    platformGroups.keys.toList().mapIndexed { i, plat ->
                        GameEntity(
                            id = i,
                            title = plat,
                            platform = "PLATFORM_SELECTOR",
                            romPath = plat,
                            coverPath = getPlatformImageUrl(context, plat, assetRootUri),
                            description = "點擊進入 ${plat} 遊戲庫，瀏覽與啟動您的收藏。",
                            publisher = "${platformGroups[plat]?.size ?: 0} 遊戲",
                            developer = "System"
                        )
                    }
                } else {
                    platformGroups[selectedPlatform].orEmpty()
                }

                val safeIndex = selectedIndex.coerceIn(0, (displayedGames.size - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))

                Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    // Header or Back Button if viewing games (themes 0 and 10 have integrated back buttons)
                    if (!isPlatformView && currentThemeFamilyIndex != 0 && currentThemeFamilyIndex != 10) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                .clickable {
                                    selectedPlatform = null
                                    selectedIndex = 0
                                }
                                .padding(vertical = 8.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                Icons.Filled.ArrowBack,
                                contentDescription = "Back to Platforms",
                                tint = currentTheme.textMain,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                "回到平台列表 (${selectedPlatform})",
                                color = currentTheme.textMain,
                                fontSize = 14.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        ThemeExperienceHome(
                            theme = currentTheme,
                            themeIndex = currentThemeFamilyIndex,
                            fontScale = fontScale.scale,
                            games = displayedGames,
                            selectedIndex = safeIndex,
                            userProfile = userProfile,
                            onGameSelected = { index -> 
                                val clicked = displayedGames.getOrNull(index)
                                if (selectedIndex == index && clicked != null) {
                                    if (clicked.platform == "PLATFORM_SELECTOR") {
                                        selectedPlatform = clicked.romPath
                                        selectedIndex = 0
                                    } else {
                                        onShowGameDetail(clicked)
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    selectedIndex = index 
                                    if (clicked != null && clicked.platform != "PLATFORM_SELECTOR") {
                                        viewModel.setSelectedGame(clicked)
                                    }
                                }
                            },
                            onLaunch = { game ->
                                if (game.platform == "PLATFORM_SELECTOR") {
                                    selectedPlatform = game.romPath
                                    selectedIndex = 0
                                } else {
                                    viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context)
                                }
                            },
                            onOpenSettings = onOpenSettings,
                            onOpenProfile = onOpenProfile,
                            onBack = if (!isPlatformView) {
                                {
                                    selectedPlatform = null
                                    selectedIndex = 0
                                }
                            } else null
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun DockAndNav(
    viewModel: GameViewModel,
    currentTheme: PegasusTheme,
    themeIndex: Int,
    currentRoute: String,
    dockInteractionToken: Int,
    onNavigate: (String) -> Unit,
    onTriggerDock: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val isScanning by viewModel.isScanning.collectAsState()

    Box(modifier = modifier) {
        // Floating navigation dock: overlays the content instead of taking layout height.
        BottomDock(
            themeIndex = themeIndex,
            currentRoute = currentRoute,
            onNavigate = onNavigate,
            forceVisible = isScanning,
            interactionToken = dockInteractionToken,
            modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
        )

        // 從底部滑動可暫時顯示浮動 Dock。
        // 48dp 高度搭配中央握把，提供足夠的觸控目標。
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(48.dp)
                .pointerInput(Unit) {
                    detectDragGestures(
                        onDragEnd = { onTriggerDock() },
                        onDragCancel = {},
                        onDrag = { _, _ -> }
                    )
                },
            contentAlignment = Alignment.BottomCenter
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .padding(bottom = 6.dp)
                    .width(48.dp)
                    .height(4.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(2.dp))
                    .background(currentTheme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.35f))
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun TopRail(viewModel: GameViewModel, themeIndex: Int, onOpenProfile: () -> Unit) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext.current
    val isDark by viewModel.isDarkTheme.collectAsState()
    val userProfile by viewModel.userProfile.collectAsState()
    var currentTime by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        val sdf = SimpleDateFormat("hh:mm a", Locale.ENGLISH)
        while (true) {
            currentTime = sdf.format(Date()).uppercase()
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(1000L)
        }
    }

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        // Left rail: SYLPHORA tracked title & time indicator
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
                    .border(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.16f), RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.AccessTime, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp))
                Text(currentTime, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        }

        // Right rail: enlarged avatar profile & quick Light/Dark toggle
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
        ) {
            // Quick Dark/Light Mode Switcher
            IconButton(
                onClick = {
                    SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                    viewModel.toggleDarkTheme()
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(36.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = if (theme.isDark) 0.2f else 0.9f))
                    .border(
                        1.dp,
                        if (theme.isDark) SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.20f) else Color(0xFF80CBC4),
                        CircleShape
                    )
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = if (isDark) Icons.Filled.LightMode else Icons.Filled.DarkMode,
                    contentDescription = if (isDark) "切換為淺色模式" else "切換為深色模式",
                    tint = if (theme.isDark) SylphoraPrimaryCyan else Color(0xFF00695C),
                    modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                )
            }

            if (themeIndex == 0) {
                // Variant C Pill
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = if (theme.isDark) 0.2f else 0.9f))
                        .border(
                            1.dp,
                            if (theme.isDark) SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.20f) else Color(0xFF80CBC4),
                            CircleShape
                        )
                        .shadow(
                            elevation = 15.dp,
                            shape = CircleShape,
                            ambientColor = if (theme.isDark) SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.15f) else Color(0xFF00838F).copy(alpha = 0.15f),
                            spotColor = if (theme.isDark) SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.25f) else Color(0xFF00838F).copy(alpha = 0.20f)
                        )
                        .clickable(onClick = onOpenProfile)
                        .padding(start = 16.dp, end = 6.dp, top = 5.dp, bottom = 5.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = currentTime,
                        color = if (theme.isDark) SylphoraPrimaryCyan.copy(alpha = 0.80f) else Color(0xFF004D40),
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                        letterSpacing = 1.sp
                    )

                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(30.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(if (theme.isDark) Color(0xFF0F0A1E) else Color(0xFFE0F2F1))
                            .border(
                                1.dp,
                                if (theme.isDark) SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.40f) else Color(0xFF80CBC4),
                                CircleShape
                            )
                            .shadow(
                                10.dp,
                                shape = CircleShape,
                                ambientColor = if (theme.isDark) SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.30f) else Color(0xFF00838F).copy(alpha = 0.20f)
                            ),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Text("🌸", fontSize = 15.sp)
                    }
                }
            } else {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.78f))
                        .border(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.20f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .clickable(onClick = onOpenProfile)
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(32.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.25f))
                            .border(1.5.dp, theme.accent, CircleShape),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        val emoji = when (userProfile.avatarType) {
                            "pixel_wizard" -> "🧙"
                            "pixel_cyber" -> "⚡"
                            "pixel_robot" -> "🤖"
                            "pixel_dragon" -> "🐉"
                            else -> "🗡️"
                        }
                        Text(emoji, fontSize = 16.sp)
                    }

                    Column {
                        Text(userProfile.username, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        Text(userProfile.title, color = theme.accent, fontSize = 10.sp)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun PlatformSelector(
    theme: PegasusTheme,
    platforms: Map<String, List<GameEntity>>,
    themeIndex: Int,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit
) {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.padding(horizontal = 10.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = when (themeIndex) {
                    0 -> "探索你的世界"
                    1 -> "PLATFORMS"
                    2 -> "遊戲平台典藏"
                    3 -> "SYSTEM SELECT"
                    4 -> "CHOOSE YOUR PLATFORM"
                    5 -> "// PLATFORM_GATE"
                    6 -> "選擇遊戲主機"
                    else -> "SELECT PLATFORM"
                },
                color = theme.accent,
                fontSize = if (themeIndex == 0) 15.sp else 12.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(3.dp))
            Text(
                text = "先選擇平台，再進入遊戲庫",
                color = theme.textMain,
                fontSize = if (themeIndex == 0) 30.sp else 24.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Black
            )
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
        LazyVerticalGrid(
            columns = GridCells.Adaptive(minSize = 210.dp),
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
        ) {
            items(platforms.keys.toList(), key = { it }) { platform ->
                val platformGames = platforms[platform].orEmpty()
                val hero = platformGames.firstOrNull { !it.backgroundPath.isNullOrBlank() }
                    ?: platformGames.firstOrNull { !it.coverPath.isNullOrBlank() }
                val image = hero?.backgroundPath ?: hero?.coverPath
                val shape = RoundedCornerShape(if (themeIndex == 0) 28.dp else 18.dp)
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .height(if (themeIndex == 0) 190.dp else 165.dp)
                        .clickable { onSelect(platform) },
                    shape = shape,
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = if (themeIndex == 0) 0.48f else 0.90f)),
                    border = BorderStroke(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = if (themeIndex == 0) 0.38f else 0.20f)),
                    elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(if (themeIndex == 0) 10.dp else 5.dp)
                ) {
                    Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                        if (!image.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            AsyncImage(
                                model = image,
                                contentDescription = platform,
                                contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().clip(shape)
                            )
                            Box(
                                Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(
                                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                                        listOf(Color.Transparent, theme.background.copy(alpha = 0.92f))
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        } else {
                            Box(
                                Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(
                                    Brush.radialGradient(
                                        colors = listOf(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.24f), theme.surface),
                                        radius = 400f
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        }
                        Column(
                            Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomStart).padding(16.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(platform, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Black, maxLines = 1)
                            Text("${platformGames.size} 款遊戲", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 11.sp)
                        }
                        Box(
                            Modifier.align(Alignment.TopEnd).padding(12.dp).size(34.dp).clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(theme.background.copy(alpha = 0.58f))
                                .border(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.45f), CircleShape),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.ChevronRight, contentDescription = "進入 $platform", tint = theme.accent)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun PlayerProfileDialog(viewModel: GameViewModel, onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val userProfile by viewModel.userProfile.collectAsState()
    val health = viewModel.calculateHealth()
    val achievements = viewModel.getAchievements()

    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth(0.95f)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
                .background(theme.background)
                .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f), RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
                .padding(24.dp)
        ) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)) {
                // Header
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text("玩家檔案", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    SylphoraIconButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = "關閉", tint = theme.textMain)
                    }
                }

                // Profile Hero
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .background(Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(theme.surface, theme.cardStart)))
                        .padding(16.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
                ) {
                    val emoji = when (userProfile.avatarType) {
                        "pixel_wizard" -> "🧙"
                        "pixel_cyber" -> "⚡"
                        "pixel_robot" -> "🤖"
                        "pixel_dragon" -> "🐉"
                        else -> "🗡️"
                    }
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(64.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                            .border(2.dp, theme.accent, CircleShape),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Text(emoji, fontSize = 32.sp)
                    }

                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(userProfile.username, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        Text(userProfile.title, color = theme.accent, fontSize = 13.sp)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                        Text("總遊戲收藏: ${health.totalGames} 款 · 健康度: ${health.score.toInt()}%", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
                    }
                }

                // Unlocked Achievements Mini List
                Text("🏆 近期解鎖成就", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    achievements.take(2).forEach { ach ->
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                                .padding(12.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(ach.icon, fontSize = 22.sp)
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                Text(ach.title, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 14.sp)
                                Text(ach.description, color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 11.sp)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                SylphoraButton(
                    onClick = onDismiss,
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(46.dp),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                ) {
                    Text("返回", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun GameCarousel(
    games: List<GameEntity>,
    selectedIndex: Int,
    onGameSelected: (Int) -> Unit,
    listState: androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyListState
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    LazyRow(
        state = listState,
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 48.dp, vertical = 24.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(32.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        itemsIndexed(games, key = { _, game -> "${game.id}_${game.romPath}" }) { index, game ->
            val isSelected = index == selectedIndex
            FocusCard(
                game = game,
                isSelected = isSelected,
                onClick = { onGameSelected(index) }
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun FocusCard(
    game: GameEntity,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val targetScale = if (isSelected) 1.05f else 0.92f
    val targetElevation = if (isSelected) 16.dp else 4.dp
    
    val scale by androidx.compose.animation.core.animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = targetScale,
        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 200, easing = FastOutLinearInEasing),
        label = "scale"
    )

    BoxWithConstraints {
        val cardHeight = if (maxHeight > 0.dp) (maxHeight * 0.72f).coerceIn(180.dp, 400.dp) else 430.dp
        val cardWidth = (cardHeight * (310f / 430f)).coerceIn(135.dp, 310.dp)

        Card(
            modifier = Modifier
                .width(cardWidth)
                .height(cardHeight)
                .scale(scale)
                .clickable(onClick = onClick),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(28.dp),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                containerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = if (isSelected) 0.85f else 0.5f)
            ),
            border = BorderStroke(
                width = if (isSelected) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                brush = if (isSelected) {
                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            theme.accent,
                            theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.3f)
                        )
                    )
                } else {
                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f),
                            Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.02f)
                        )
                    )
                }
            ),
            elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = targetElevation)
        ) {
        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(20.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
            ) {
                // Clean hero header: no platform tag and no "unplayed" status badge on the home page.
                Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.End) {
                    if (game.isFavorite) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.Favorite, contentDescription = "已收藏", tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                    }
                }

                // Center Cover Art
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(160.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .background(Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(theme.cardStart, theme.cardEnd))),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    UniversalGameArt(
                        game = game,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                    )
                }

                // Bottom Title
                Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                    Text(
                        text = game.title,
                        color = theme.textMain,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        fontSize = 18.sp,
                        maxLines = 1
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = if (isSelected) "按 A 鍵或點擊啟動" else "點擊選取",
                        color = if (isSelected) theme.accent else theme.textSecondary,
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
}

@Composable
fun BottomDock(
    themeIndex: Int,
    currentRoute: String,
    onNavigate: (String) -> Unit,
    forceVisible: Boolean = false,
    interactionToken: Int = 0,
    autoHideDelayMs: Long = 3000L,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = LocalContext.current

    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }

    LaunchedEffect(interactionToken) {
        if (!visible) return@LaunchedEffect
        if (themeIndex == 10 && currentRoute == "home") {
            // Keep dock permanently visible on Switch 2 Home
        } else {
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(autoHideDelayMs)
            if (!forceVisible) visible = false
        }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(forceVisible) {
        if (forceVisible) visible = true
    }

    AnimatedVisibility(
        visible = visible || forceVisible,
        enter = androidx.compose.animation.fadeIn() + androidx.compose.animation.slideInVertically { it / 2 },
        exit = androidx.compose.animation.fadeOut() + androidx.compose.animation.slideOutVertically { it / 2 },
        modifier = modifier
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            when (themeIndex) {
                0 -> SylphoraDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                1 -> IisuFluidDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                2 -> PegasusImperialDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                3 -> EsdeEsportsDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                4 -> SteamDeckDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                5 -> CyberpunkMatrixDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                6 -> NintendoJoyDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                7 -> GameBoyPixelDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                8 -> AnimeSakuraDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                9 -> Ps5DualSenseDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                10 -> {
                    if (currentRoute == "home") {
                        Switch2OledDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
                    }
                }
                else -> SylphoraDock(theme, currentRoute, onNavigate, context)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SylphoraDock(
    theme: PegasusTheme,
    currentRoute: String,
    onNavigate: (String) -> Unit,
    context: android.content.Context
) {
    val isDark = theme.isDark
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .background(if (isDark) Color(0x33000000) else Color(0xE6FFFFFF))
            .shadow(
                elevation = 20.dp,
                shape = CircleShape,
                ambientColor = if (isDark) SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.20f) else Color(0xFF00838F).copy(alpha = 0.20f),
                spotColor = theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.35f)
            )
            .border(
                width = 1.dp,
                color = if (isDark) SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.20f) else Color(0xFF80CBC4),
                shape = CircleShape
            )
            .padding(horizontal = 22.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp)
    ) {
        SylphoraDockItem(Icons.Filled.GridView, "首頁", currentRoute == "home", isDark) {
            SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
            onNavigate("home")
        }
        SylphoraDockItem(Icons.Filled.SportsEsports, "遊戲庫", currentRoute == "library", isDark) {
            SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
            onNavigate("library")
        }
        SylphoraDockItem(Icons.Filled.ShoppingBag, "AI 館長", currentRoute == "ai", isDark) {
            SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
            onNavigate("ai")
        }
        SylphoraDockItem(Icons.Filled.TravelExplore, "考據", currentRoute == "research", isDark) {
            SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
            onNavigate("research")
        }
        SylphoraDockItem(Icons.Filled.Settings, "設定", currentRoute == "tools", isDark) {
            SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
            onNavigate("tools")
        }
    }
}

) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(30.dp))
            .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.85f))
            .border(1.5.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.55f), RoundedCornerShape(30.dp))
            .shadow(16.dp, RoundedCornerShape(30.dp), ambientColor = theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.35f))
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
    ) {
        listOf(
            Triple("home", Icons.Filled.Home, "首頁"),
            Triple("library", Icons.Filled.SportsEsports, "遊戲庫"),
            Triple("ai", Icons.Filled.AutoAwesome, "iiSU 助理"),
            Triple("research", Icons.Filled.TravelExplore, "考據"),
            Triple("tools", Icons.Filled.Tune, "系統")
        ).forEach { (route, icon, label) ->
            val isSelected = currentRoute == route
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                    .background(if (isSelected) theme.accent else Color.Transparent)
                    .clickable {
                        SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                        onNavigate(route)
                    }
                    .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = icon,
                    contentDescription = label,
                    tint = if (isSelected) theme.background else theme.textMain,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                )
                if (isSelected) {
                    Text(label, color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 12.sp)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun Switch2OledDock(
    theme: PegasusTheme,
    currentRoute: String,
    onNavigate: (String) -> Unit,
    context: android.content.Context

    com.example.ui.theme.Switch2PillDock(
        currentRoute = currentRoute,
        activeDockIndex = when (currentRoute) {

            "library" -> 4
            else -> 0
        },
        onNavigate = onNavigate,
        context = context
    )
}

@Composable
fun SylphoraDockItem(
    icon: ImageVector,
    contentDescription: String,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    isDark: Boolean = true,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "SylphoraDockGlow")
    val pulseGlow by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.40f,
        targetValue = 0.85f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(3000, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "pulseGlow"
    )

    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val activeGlowColor = theme.accent
    val activeIconTint = theme.accent
    val inactiveIconTint = theme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.75f)

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .size(48.dp)
            .clickable(onClick = onClick),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        if (isSelected) {
            // Drop shadow pulse glow
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(34.dp)
                    .background(
                        brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                            colors = listOf(
                                activeGlowColor.copy(alpha = 0.55f * pulseGlow),
                                Color.Transparent
                            ),
                            radius = 50f
                        ),
                        shape = CircleShape
                    )
            )
        }

        Icon(
            imageVector = icon,
            contentDescription = contentDescription,
            tint = if (isSelected) activeIconTint else inactiveIconTint,
            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
        )
    }
}


@Composable
fun DockIcon(
    themeIndex: Int,
    icon: ImageVector,
    label: String,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current

    // Continuous breathing light & pulsing scale animation
    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "DockIconBreathing")
    val breathAlpha by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.50f,
        targetValue = 1.0f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(1300, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "breathAlpha"
    )
    val auraScale by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.96f,
        targetValue = 1.18f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(1500, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "auraScale"
    )

    val flowingBorderBrush = if (themeIndex == 0) {
        Brush.horizontalGradient(
            colors = listOf(
                SylphoraCelestialPurple,
                Color.White.copy(alpha = breathAlpha),
                SylphoraPrimaryCyan,
                SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                SylphoraCelestialPurple
            )
        )
    } else {
        Brush.horizontalGradient(
            colors = listOf(
                theme.accent,
                Color.White.copy(alpha = breathAlpha),
                Color(0xFF82A0D8),
                theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                theme.accent
            )
        )
    }

    Box(
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
        modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 1.dp)
    ) {
        // Continuous breathing light aura halo extending outside the dock container ("流出去"發光呼吸燈)
        if (isSelected) {
            val auraColors = if (themeIndex == 0) {
                listOf(
                    SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.75f * breathAlpha),
                    SylphoraPrimaryCyan.copy(alpha = 0.40f * breathAlpha),
                    Color.Transparent
                )
            } else {
                listOf(
                    theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.85f * breathAlpha),
                    Color(0xFF82A0D8).copy(alpha = 0.45f * breathAlpha),
                    theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.15f * breathAlpha),
                    Color.Transparent
                )
            }
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(54.dp * auraScale)
                    .background(
                        brush = Brush.radialGradient(colors = auraColors, radius = 70f),
                        shape = CircleShape
                    )
            )
        }

        val buttonContainerColor = if (isSelected) {
            if (themeIndex == 0) SylphoraCelestialPurple else theme.accent
        } else {
            theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.4f)
        }

        val iconTint = if (isSelected) {
            if (themeIndex == 0) Color.White else Color.Black
        } else {
            theme.textSecondary
        }

        SylphoraIconButton(
            onClick = onClick,
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(44.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(buttonContainerColor)
                .border(
                    width = if (isSelected) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                    brush = if (isSelected) flowingBorderBrush else SolidColor(if (themeIndex == 0) SylphoraCelestialPurple.copy(alpha = 0.25f) else theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.2f)),
                    shape = CircleShape
                )
                .shadow(
                    elevation = if (isSelected) (14.dp * breathAlpha) else 0.dp,
                    shape = CircleShape,
                    clip = false,
                    ambientColor = if (themeIndex == 0) SylphoraCelestialPurple else theme.accent,
                    spotColor = Color.White
                )
        ) {
            Column(
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = icon,
                    contentDescription = label,
                    tint = iconTint,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                )
                Text(
                    text = label,
                    color = iconTint,
                    fontSize = 9.sp,
                    fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.ExtraBold else FontWeight.Normal
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun ActionButtonHint(text: String) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
            .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f))
            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Text(text = text, color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
    }
}

@Composable
fun ActionLabel(button: String, action: String) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    Row(
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(24.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f))
                .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.2f), CircleShape),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Text(text = button, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        }
        Text(text = action, color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
    }
}

    val roomSavedCount: Int = 0,
    val csvSyncedCount: Int = 0,
    val csvPendingRetryCount: Int = 0,
    val lastError: String? = null

class GameViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {

    private val _selectedGame = MutableStateFlow<GameEntity?>(null)
    val selectedGame: StateFlow<GameEntity?> = _selectedGame.asStateFlow()

    private val _isScanning = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isScanning: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isScanning.asStateFlow()

    private val scanningTaskCount = java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger(0)

    private suspend fun <T> withScanning(block: suspend () -> T): T {
        if (scanningTaskCount.incrementAndGet() == 1) {
            _isScanning.value = true
        }
        try {
            return block()
        } finally {
            if (scanningTaskCount.decrementAndGet() == 0) {
                _isScanning.value = false
            }
        }
    }

    private val _scanProgressText = MutableStateFlow("")
    val scanProgressText: StateFlow<String> = _scanProgressText.asStateFlow()

    private val _isBatchScraping = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isBatchScraping: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isBatchScraping.asStateFlow()

    val batchScrapeProgress = MutableStateFlow("")

    private val _currentThemeFamilyIndex = MutableStateFlow(settingsRepository.getThemeFamilyIndex())

    val discoveredPlatforms: StateFlow<Map<String, List<String>>> = _discoveredPlatforms.asStateFlow()

    val apiProfiles: List<ApiProfile> get() = _apiProfiles.value

    private val modelDiscovery = com.example.api.DefaultAiModelDiscovery
    private val _cachedModels = MutableStateFlow<Map<String, List<com.example.api.AiModel>>>(emptyMap())

    private var cachedResearchEngine: com.example.research.ResearchEngine? = null
    private fun getOrCreateResearchEngine(): com.example.research.ResearchEngine {
        return cachedResearchEngine ?: com.example.research.ResearchEngine(
            context = getApplication(),
            profilesProvider = { _apiProfiles.value },
            rotationManager = rotationManager,
            resolver = aiResolver
        ).also { cachedResearchEngine = it }
    }

    private var cachedCuratorKey: Int? = null
    private var cachedCuratorAgent: com.example.agent.AICuratorAgent? = null
    private fun getOrCreateCuratorAgent(): com.example.agent.AICuratorAgent {
        val profiles = _apiProfiles.value
        val repo = settingsRepository
        val webSearchMode = repo.getWebSearchMode()
        val currentKey = profiles.hashCode() xor webSearchMode.hashCode()
        if (cachedCuratorKey == currentKey && cachedCuratorAgent != null) {
            return cachedCuratorAgent!!
        }
        val engine = getOrCreateResearchEngine()
        val agent = com.example.agent.AICuratorAgent(
            profiles = profiles,
            rotationManager = rotationManager,
            resolver = aiResolver,
            researchEngine = engine,
            searchMode = webSearchMode,
            searchProvider = repo.getWebSearchProvider(),
            searchApiKey = repo.getWebSearchApiKey(),
            searchCustomEndpoint = repo.getWebSearchCustomEndpoint(),
            enrichmentService = com.example.agent.MetadataEnrichmentService(profiles, rotationManager, aiResolver, repository),
            allGamesProvider = { repository.getAllGamesDirect() }
        )
        cachedCuratorKey = currentKey
        cachedCuratorAgent = agent
        return agent
    }
