    init {
        val initialMap = mutableMapOf<String, List<com.example.api.AiModel>>()
        _apiProfiles.value.forEach { p ->
            val cached = settingsRepository.getCachedModels(p.id)
            if (cached.isNotEmpty()) initialMap[p.id] = cached
        }
        _cachedModels.value = initialMap

        settingsRepository.getFeatureProfileBindings().forEach { (feature, profileId) ->
            aiResolver.bindFeatureToProfile(feature, profileId)
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.allGames.collect { games ->
                _allGames.value = games
                val savedId = settingsRepository.getSelectedGameId()
                if (savedId != null) {
                    _selectedGame.value = games.firstOrNull { it.id == savedId } ?: games.firstOrNull()
                } else if (_selectedGame.value == null && games.isNotEmpty()) {
                    _selectedGame.value = games.firstOrNull()
                }
            }
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.descriptionSyncStats
                .distinctUntilChanged()
                .collect { stats ->
                    descriptionSyncStatus.value = DescriptionSyncStatus(
                        roomSavedCount = stats.totalCount,
                        csvSyncedCount = stats.syncedCount,
                        csvPendingRetryCount = (stats.totalCount - stats.syncedCount).coerceAtLeast(0)
                    )
                }
        }
        refreshDiscoveredPlatforms()
    }

    fun setSelectedGame(game: GameEntity) {
        _selectedGame.value = game
        settingsRepository.saveSelectedGameId(game.id)
    }

    fun refreshDiscoveredPlatforms() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val sources = _romSources.value
            val map = mutableMapOf<String, List<String>>()
            for (source in sources) {
                if (source.enabled) {
                    try {
                        val plats = romScanner.discoverPlatforms(source)
                        map[source.id] = plats
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        map[source.id] = emptyList()
                    }
                }
            }
            _discoveredPlatforms.value = map
        }
    }

    // --- ROM Source Management ---
    fun addRomSource(name: String, treeUri: Uri) {
        runCatching {
            val flags = Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION
            getApplication<Application>().contentResolver.takePersistableUriPermission(treeUri, flags)
        }
        val source = com.example.scanner.RomSource(
            name = name.ifBlank { "ROM 資料夾" },
            treeUri = treeUri.toString(),
            enabled = true
        )
        settingsRepository.addRomSource(source)
        _romSources.value = settingsRepository.getRomSources()
        _romRootUri.value = treeUri.toString()
        refreshDiscoveredPlatforms()
    }

    fun removeRomSource(sourceId: String) {
        settingsRepository.removeRomSource(sourceId)
        _romSources.value = settingsRepository.getRomSources()
        refreshDiscoveredPlatforms()
    }

    fun toggleRomSource(sourceId: String, enabled: Boolean) {
        settingsRepository.toggleRomSource(sourceId, enabled)
        _romSources.value = settingsRepository.getRomSources()
        refreshDiscoveredPlatforms()
    }

    fun scanPlatformOnly(sourceId: String, platform: String) {
        val source = _romSources.value.firstOrNull { it.id == sourceId } ?: return
        viewModelScope.launch {
            withScanning {
                _scanProgressText.value = "正在掃描 [$platform] 遊戲..."
                try {
                    val found = romScanner.scanSource(source, platformFilter = platform) { cur, tot, item ->
                        _scanProgressText.value = "[$platform] ($cur/$tot) $item"
                    }
                    if (found.isNotEmpty()) {
                        repository.upsertScannedGamesBatch(getApplication(), found)
                    }
                    _scanProgressText.value = "[$platform] 掃描完成，共計 ${found.size} 款遊戲"
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _scanProgressText.value = "掃描失敗: ${e.message}"
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun scanRomSourcePlatform(source: com.example.scanner.RomSource, platform: String?) {
        if (platform != null) scanPlatformOnly(source.id, platform) else scanAllPlatformsForSource(source.id)
    }

    fun scanRomSourcePlatform(sourceId: String, platform: String?) {
        if (platform != null) scanPlatformOnly(sourceId, platform) else scanAllPlatformsForSource(sourceId)
    }

    fun scanAllPlatformsForSource(sourceId: String) {
        val source = _romSources.value.firstOrNull { it.id == sourceId } ?: return
        viewModelScope.launch {
            withScanning {
                _scanProgressText.value = "正在掃描來源【${source.name}】..."
                try {
                    val found = romScanner.scanSource(source, platformFilter = null) { cur, tot, item ->
                        _scanProgressText.value = "(${cur}/${tot}) $item"
                    }
                    if (found.isNotEmpty()) {
                        repository.upsertScannedGamesBatch(getApplication(), found)
                    }
                    _scanProgressText.value = "【${source.name}】掃描完畢，共收錄 ${found.size} 款遊戲"
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _scanProgressText.value = "掃描失敗: ${e.message}"
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun scanAllRomSources() {
        logUserAction("SCAN_ROMS", "ALL_SOURCES")
        val enabledSources = _romSources.value.filter { it.enabled }
        if (enabledSources.isEmpty()) return

        viewModelScope.launch {
            withScanning {
                _scanProgressText.value = "正在準備掃描全部 ROM 來源..."
                try {
                    var totalFound = 0
                    for ((idx, source) in enabledSources.withIndex()) {
                        _scanProgressText.value = "[${idx + 1}/${enabledSources.size}] 正在掃描來源【${source.name}】..."
                        val found = romScanner.scanSource(source, platformFilter = null) { cur, tot, item ->
                            _scanProgressText.value = "[${idx + 1}/${enabledSources.size}] (${cur}/${tot}) $item"
                        }
                        if (found.isNotEmpty()) {
                            repository.upsertScannedGamesBatch(getApplication(), found)
                        }
                        totalFound += found.size
                    }
                    _scanProgressText.value = "全部來源掃描完成！共收錄 $totalFound 款遊戲"
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _scanProgressText.value = "掃描過程發生錯誤: ${e.message}"
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun startRomScan(uri: Uri) {
        addRomSource("ROM 資料夾", uri)
        val source = _romSources.value.firstOrNull { it.treeUri == uri.toString() }
        if (source != null) {
            scanAllPlatformsForSource(source.id)
        }
    }

    fun scanInstalledApps(context: Context = getApplication()) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            withScanning {
                _scanProgressText.value = "正在掃描已安裝 Android 應用程式..."
                try {
                    val appScanner = AppScanner(context)
                    val apps = appScanner.getInstalledApps()
                    var added = 0
                    for (app in apps) {
                        val canonicalPath = if (app.packageName.startsWith("app://")) app.packageName else "app://${app.packageName}"
                        val exists = _allGames.value.any { it.romPath == canonicalPath || it.romPath == app.packageName }
                        if (!exists) {
                            val game = GameEntity(
                                title = app.name,
                                platform = "Android",
                                romPath = canonicalPath,
                                coverPath = canonicalPath,
                                description = "Android 應用程式: ${app.packageName}"
                            )
                            repository.insertGame(game)
                            added++
                        }
                    }
                    _scanProgressText.value = "Android 應用程式掃描完成！新增 $added 款 App"
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _scanProgressText.value = "App 掃描失敗: ${e.message}"
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun scanInstalledAndroidApps(context: Context = getApplication()) = scanInstalledApps(context)

    data class EmulatorPickerState(
        val game: GameEntity,
        val installedLaunchers: List<com.example.LauncherProfile>
    )

    val activeEmulatorPicker = MutableStateFlow<EmulatorPickerState?>(null)
    val activeNoEmulatorDialog = MutableStateFlow<GameEntity?>(null)

    fun getPreferredLauncherForPlatform(platform: String): String? {
        val prefs = getApplication<Application>().getSharedPreferences("emulator_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        return prefs.getString("pref_launcher_${platform.uppercase()}", null)
    }

    fun setPreferredLauncherForPlatform(platform: String, launcherId: String?) {
        val prefs = getApplication<Application>().getSharedPreferences("emulator_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        if (launcherId == null) {
            prefs.edit().remove("pref_launcher_${platform.uppercase()}").apply()
        } else {
            prefs.edit().putString("pref_launcher_${platform.uppercase()}", launcherId).apply()
        }
    }

    fun recordGamePlay(game: GameEntity) {
        logUserAction("LAUNCH_GAME", game.title)
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val updated = game.copy(
                playCount = game.playCount + 1,
                lastPlayed = System.currentTimeMillis()
            )
            repository.updateGame(updated)
            if (_selectedGame.value?.id == game.id) {
                _selectedGame.value = updated
            }
        }
    }

    fun launchGameWithLauncher(context: Context, game: GameEntity, launcherId: String, setAsDefault: Boolean) {
        if (setAsDefault) {
            setPreferredLauncherForPlatform(game.platform, launcherId)
        }
        activeEmulatorPicker.value = null
        val success = LauncherBridge.launchGame(context, game.platform, game.romPath, game.title, preferredLauncherId = launcherId)
        if (success) {
            recordGamePlay(game)
        } else {
            Toast.makeText(context, "無法啟動 ${game.title}，請確認模擬器與 ROM 檔", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            activeNoEmulatorDialog.value = game
        }
    }

    fun launchGame(context: Context, game: GameEntity) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                if (game.platform.equals("Android", ignoreCase = true)) {
                    val pkg = game.romPath.removePrefix("app://")
                    val launchIntent = context.packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(pkg)
                    if (launchIntent != null) {
                        launchIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                        context.startActivity(launchIntent)
                        recordGamePlay(game)
                    } else {
                        Toast.makeText(context, "未找到安裝的應用程式: $pkg", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                        activeNoEmulatorDialog.value = game
                    }
                } else {
                    val settingsRepo = com.example.settings.SettingsRepository(context)
                    val canonicalPlat = com.example.scanner.PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(game.platform).uppercase()
                    val emuMode = settingsRepo.getEmulatorMode(canonicalPlat)
                    val manualOverrideLauncherId = if (emuMode == "MANUAL") settingsRepo.getEmulatorOverride(canonicalPlat) else null
                    val manualOverrideCore = if (emuMode == "MANUAL") settingsRepo.getCoreOverride(canonicalPlat) else null

                    val prefLauncherId = manualOverrideLauncherId ?: getPreferredLauncherForPlatform(game.platform)
                    val installed = LauncherBridge.getInstalledLaunchersForPlatform(context, game.platform)

                    if (prefLauncherId != null) {
                        val success = LauncherBridge.launchGame(
                            context,
                            game.platform,
                            game.romPath,
                            game.title,
                            preferredLauncherId = prefLauncherId,
                            overrideCore = manualOverrideCore
                        )
                        if (success) {
                            recordGamePlay(game)
                        } else {
                            Toast.makeText(context, "無法啟動 ${game.title}，請確認模擬器與 ROM 檔", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            activeNoEmulatorDialog.value = game
                        }
                    } else if (installed.isEmpty()) {
                        // Attempt blind/direct launch with platform default before showing error dialog
                        val success = LauncherBridge.launchGame(context, game.platform, game.romPath, game.title)
                        if (success) {
                            recordGamePlay(game)
                        } else {
                            activeNoEmulatorDialog.value = game
                        }
                    } else if (installed.size == 1) {
                        val success = LauncherBridge.launchGame(context, game.platform, game.romPath, game.title, preferredLauncherId = installed[0].id)
                        if (success) {
                            recordGamePlay(game)
                        } else {
                            Toast.makeText(context, "無法啟動 ${game.title}，請確認模擬器與 ROM 檔", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            activeNoEmulatorDialog.value = game
                        }
                    } else {
                        activeEmulatorPicker.value = EmulatorPickerState(game, installed)
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
                Toast.makeText(context, "無法啟動遊戲: ${e.message ?: "預期外的錯誤"}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                activeNoEmulatorDialog.value = game
            }
        }
    }

    fun launchGame(game: GameEntity, context: Context) = launchGame(context, game)
    fun recordGameLaunch(context: Context, game: GameEntity) = launchGame(context, game)
    fun recordGameLaunch(game: GameEntity, context: Context) = launchGame(context, game)

    fun calculateHealth(): HealthScore {
        val games = _allGames.value
        val total = games.size
        if (total == 0) return HealthScore(100f, 0, 0, emptyMap())
        val missingDesc = games.count { it.description.isNullOrBlank() }
        val platformCounts = games.groupBy { it.platform }.mapValues { it.value.size }
        val score = ((total - missingDesc).toFloat() / total.toFloat()) * 100f
        return HealthScore(score, total, missingDesc, platformCounts)
    }

    fun calculateFullHealth(): HealthScore = calculateHealth()

    fun getAchievements(): List<AchievementItem> {
        val games = _allGames.value
        return listOf(
            AchievementItem("1", "初出茅廬", "啟動首款遊戲", "🎮", games.any { it.playCount > 0 }, if (games.any { it.playCount > 0 }) 1f else 0f),
            AchievementItem("2", "典藏家", "收錄超過 10 款遊戲", "📚", games.size >= 10, (games.size / 10f).coerceAtMost(1f)),
            AchievementItem("3", "復古玩家", "暢玩 5 款不同平台遊戲", "🕹️", games.map { it.platform }.distinct().size >= 5, (games.map { it.platform }.distinct().size / 5f).coerceAtMost(1f))
        )
    }

    var currentScreenName: String = "LIBRARY"
    var currentMuseumTabName: String? = null
    var lastUserActionName: String? = null
    var lastUserActionTargetName: String? = null

    fun logUserAction(action: String, target: String? = null) {
        lastUserActionName = action
        lastUserActionTargetName = target
    }

    fun updateCuratorNavigation(screen: String, tab: String? = null) {
        currentScreenName = screen
        currentMuseumTabName = tab
    }

    fun selectGame(game: GameEntity) {
        _selectedGame.value = game
        settingsRepository.saveSelectedGameId(game.id)
        logUserAction("SELECT_GAME", game.title)
    }

    fun toggleDarkTheme() {
        val next = !_isDarkTheme.value
        _isDarkTheme.value = next
        settingsRepository.saveThemePreferences(
            _currentThemeFamilyIndex.value,
            next,
            currentLayoutMode.value.name,
            _customWallpaperUri.value,
            _glassOpacity.value
        )
    }

    fun setDarkTheme(isDark: Boolean) {
        _isDarkTheme.value = isDark
        settingsRepository.saveThemePreferences(
            _currentThemeFamilyIndex.value,
            isDark,
            currentLayoutMode.value.name,
            _customWallpaperUri.value,
            _glassOpacity.value
        )
    }

    fun saveThemePreferences(familyIndex: Int, isDark: Boolean, layout: LauncherLayoutMode, wallpaperUri: String?, opacity: Float) {
        _currentThemeFamilyIndex.value = familyIndex
        _isDarkTheme.value = isDark
        currentLayoutMode.value = layout
        _customWallpaperUri.value = wallpaperUri
        _glassOpacity.value = opacity
        settingsRepository.saveThemePreferences(familyIndex, isDark, layout.name, wallpaperUri, opacity)
    }

    fun saveAudioSettings(sfx: Boolean, bgm: Boolean, volume: Float) {
        isSfxEnabled.value = sfx
        isBgmEnabled.value = bgm
        bgmVolume.value = volume
        settingsRepository.saveAudioSettings(sfx, bgm, volume)
        com.example.audio.SoundManager.setSfxEnabled(sfx)
        com.example.audio.SoundManager.setBgmVolume(volume)
        com.example.audio.SoundManager.setBgmEnabled(bgm)
    }

    fun createCollection(name: String) {
        if (name.isNotBlank() && !_customCollectionsList.value.contains(name)) {
            val updated = _customCollectionsList.value + name
            _customCollectionsList.value = updated
            settingsRepository.saveCustomCollections(updated)
        }
    }

    fun deleteCollection(name: String) {
        val updated = _customCollectionsList.value - name
        _customCollectionsList.value = updated
        settingsRepository.saveCustomCollections(updated)
    }

    fun saveUserProfile(username: String, title: String, avatarType: String, customUri: String?) {
        val profile = _userProfile.value.copy(
            username = username,
            title = title,
            avatarType = avatarType,
            customAvatarUri = customUri
        )
        _userProfile.value = profile
        settingsRepository.saveUserProfile(profile)
    }

    fun setAssetRootUri(uri: Uri) {
        runCatching {
            val flags = Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION
            getApplication<Application>().contentResolver.takePersistableUriPermission(uri, flags)
        }
        _assetRootUri.value = uri.toString()
        settingsRepository.saveAssetRootUri(uri.toString())
    }

    fun setDescriptionRootUri(uri: Uri) {
        runCatching {
            val flags = Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION
            getApplication<Application>().contentResolver.takePersistableUriPermission(uri, flags)
        }
        _descriptionRootUri.value = uri.toString()
        settingsRepository.saveDescriptionRootUri(uri.toString())
        syncAllGameDescriptions(allowAiFallback = false)
    }

    fun syncAllGameDescriptions(allowAiFallback: Boolean = false) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val games = _allGames.value
            if (games.isEmpty()) return@launch
            withScanning {
                _scanProgressText.value = "正在同步 Description CSV 主資料庫..."
                try {
                    var loadedFromCsv = 0
                    var appendedToCsv = 0
                    var aiGenerated = 0
                    var unchanged = 0

                    val total = games.size
                    val batchSize = 20
                    for ((idx, game) in games.withIndex()) {
                        if (idx % batchSize == 0 || idx == total - 1) {
                            _scanProgressText.value = "[${idx + 1}/$total] 同步簡介: ${game.title}..."
                        }
                        val result = metadataCsvService.syncGameDescription(
                            rootUriOrPath = _descriptionRootUri.value,
                            game = game,
                            aiGenerator = if (allowAiFallback) { g ->
                                getOrCreateCuratorAgent().generateDescription(g.title, g.platform)
                            } else null
                        )

                        when (result.action) {
                            SyncAction.LOADED_FROM_CSV -> {
                                repository.updateGame(result.updatedGame)
                                loadedFromCsv++
                            }
                            SyncAction.APPENDED_EXISTING_TO_CSV -> {
                                repository.updateGame(result.updatedGame)
                                appendedToCsv++
                            }
                            SyncAction.AI_GENERATED_AND_SAVED -> {
                                repository.updateGame(result.updatedGame)
                                aiGenerated++
                            }
                            SyncAction.NO_CHANGE -> {
                                unchanged++
                            }
                        }

                        if (_selectedGame.value?.id == game.id) {
                            _selectedGame.value = result.updatedGame
                        }
                        if (idx % 50 == 0) yield()
                    }

                    val summary = StringBuilder("Description 同步完成：")
                    if (loadedFromCsv > 0) summary.append(" 從 CSV 載入 $loadedFromCsv 筆;")
                    if (appendedToCsv > 0) summary.append(" 回寫現有簡介 $appendedToCsv 筆;")
                    if (aiGenerated > 0) summary.append(" AI 生成並寫入 $aiGenerated 筆;")
                    if (summary.toString() == "Description 同步完成：") summary.append(" 資料已為最新狀態")
                    _scanProgressText.value = summary.toString()
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _scanProgressText.value = "同步 Description 失敗: ${e.message}"
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun importExternalFile(uri: Uri) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            withScanning {
                _scanProgressText.value = "正在匯入外部中繼資料..."
                try {
                    val records = metadataCsvService.importCsv(uri)
                    var imported = 0
                    for (rec in records) {
                        val existing = _allGames.value.firstOrNull {
                            it.title.equals(rec.title, ignoreCase = true) &&
                                    (rec.platform.isBlank() || it.platform.equals(rec.platform, ignoreCase = true))
                        }
                        if (existing != null) {
                            val updated = existing.copy(
                                description = if (rec.description.isNotBlank()) rec.description else existing.description,
                                genre = if (rec.genre.isNotBlank()) rec.genre else existing.genre,
                                releaseDate = if (rec.releaseDate.isNotBlank()) rec.releaseDate else existing.releaseDate,
                                developer = if (rec.developer.isNotBlank()) rec.developer else existing.developer,
                                publisher = if (rec.publisher.isNotBlank()) rec.publisher else existing.publisher,
                                rating = if (rec.rating > 0f) rec.rating else existing.rating,
                                customCollections = if (rec.customCollections.isNotBlank()) rec.customCollections else existing.customCollections
                            )
                            repository.updateGame(updated)
                            imported++
                        } else if (rec.title.isNotBlank()) {
                            val newGame = GameEntity(
                                title = rec.title,
                                platform = rec.platform.ifBlank { "Retro" },
                                romPath = rec.romPath.ifBlank { "manual://${rec.title}" },
                                coverPath = rec.coverPath.ifBlank { null },
                                description = rec.description,
                                genre = rec.genre,
                                releaseDate = rec.releaseDate,
                                developer = rec.developer,
                                publisher = rec.publisher,
                                rating = rec.rating,
                                customCollections = rec.customCollections
                            )
                            repository.insertGame(newGame)
                            imported++
                        }
                    }
                    _scanProgressText.value = "中繼資料匯入成功！共新增/更新 $imported 筆遊戲紀錄"
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _scanProgressText.value = "匯入失敗: ${e.message}"
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun exportPegasusMetadata(): String = metadataCsvService.exportPegasusMetadata(_allGames.value)
    fun exportFullLibrary(): String = metadataCsvService.exportLibraryToJson(_allGames.value)

    fun exportThemeJson(): String {
        val root = JSONObject().apply {
            put("themeFamilyIndex", _currentThemeFamilyIndex.value)
            put("isDarkTheme", _isDarkTheme.value)
            put("layoutMode", currentLayoutMode.value.name)
            put("glassOpacity", _glassOpacity.value)
            put("customWallpaperUri", _customWallpaperUri.value ?: "")
            put("exportedAt", System.currentTimeMillis())
        }
        return root.toString(2)
    }

    fun retryCsvWriteback(game: GameEntity) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val success = if (!game.description.isNullOrBlank()) {
                metadataCsvService.appendOrUpdatePlatformCsv(
                    rootUriOrPath = _descriptionRootUri.value,
                    platform = game.platform,
                    gameTitle = game.title,
                    description = game.description
                )
            } else false
            val updated = game.copy(descriptionCsvSynced = success)
            repository.updateGame(updated)
            if (_selectedGame.value?.id == game.id) {
                _selectedGame.value = updated
            }
        }
    }

    fun updateFeatureProfileBinding(feature: com.example.api.AiFeature, profileId: String) {
        val cleanId = profileId.trim()
        if (cleanId.isEmpty()) {
            aiResolver.bindFeatureToProfile(feature, "") // or clear
            val currentBindings = settingsRepository.getFeatureProfileBindings().toMutableMap()
            currentBindings.remove(feature)
            settingsRepository.saveFeatureProfileBindings(currentBindings)
        } else {
            aiResolver.bindFeatureToProfile(feature, cleanId)
            val currentBindings = settingsRepository.getFeatureProfileBindings().toMutableMap()
            currentBindings[feature] = cleanId
            settingsRepository.saveFeatureProfileBindings(currentBindings)
        }
    }

    fun getFeatureProfileBindings(): Map<com.example.api.AiFeature, String> {
        return settingsRepository.getFeatureProfileBindings()
    }

    suspend fun discoverModels(profile: ApiProfile, apiKeyOverride: String? = null): Result<List<com.example.api.AiModel>> = kotlinx.coroutines.withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val key = apiKeyOverride?.ifBlank { null } ?: profile.allActiveKeys().firstOrNull().orEmpty()
            val models = modelDiscovery.getAvailableModels(profile, key)
            if (models.isNotEmpty()) {
                _cachedModels.value = _cachedModels.value + (profile.id to models)
                settingsRepository.saveCachedModels(profile.id, models)
            }
            Result.success(models)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    fun getCachedModelsForProfile(profileId: String): List<com.example.api.AiModel> {
        return _cachedModels.value[profileId] ?: settingsRepository.getCachedModels(profileId)
    }

    suspend fun testApiProfile(profile: ApiProfile): ApiTestResult {
        return rotationManager.testProfile(profile)
    }

    fun sendCuratorChat(message: String) {
        val userMsg = ChatMessage("user", message)
        _chatMessages.value = _chatMessages.value + userMsg
        _isAgentTyping.value = true

        viewModelScope.launch {
            val agent = getOrCreateCuratorAgent()
            val history = _chatMessages.value.map { ChatTurn(if (it.sender == "user") "user" else "assistant", it.content) }
            val currentSelected = _selectedGame.value
            val context = CuratorContext(
                librarySize = _allGames.value.size,
                currentPlatform = currentSelected?.platform.orEmpty(),
                selectedGameTitle = currentSelected?.title,
                selectedGamePlatform = currentSelected?.platform,
                selectedGameDescription = currentSelected?.description,
                selectedGameGenre = currentSelected?.genre,
                selectedGameReleaseYear = currentSelected?.releaseDate,
                selectedGameDeveloper = currentSelected?.developer,
                missingAssetCount = _allGames.value.sumOf { com.example.agent.GameDataCompleteness.analyze(it).missingAssets.size },
                favoriteCount = _allGames.value.count { it.isFavorite },
                missingDataFields = com.example.agent.GameDataCompleteness.checkMissingFields(currentSelected),
                currentScreen = currentScreenName,
                currentMuseumTab = currentMuseumTabName,
                lastAction = lastUserActionName,
                lastActionTarget = lastUserActionTargetName,
                allGames = _allGames.value
            )
            
            if (message.contains("開始考據") && currentSelected != null) {
                val game = currentSelected
                _chatMessages.value = _chatMessages.value + ChatMessage("curator", "🔍 收到！正在為您考據《${game.title}》的相關資料...")
                try {
                    startResearchAndFillDataWithProgress(
                        game = game,
                        agent = agent,
                        engine = getOrCreateResearchEngine(),
                        repo = settingsRepository,
                        onProgress = { progressMsg ->
                            _chatMessages.value = _chatMessages.value + ChatMessage("curator", progressMsg)
                        }
                    )
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _chatMessages.value = _chatMessages.value + ChatMessage("curator", "❌ 考據過程中發生錯誤：${e.localizedMessage ?: "未知錯誤"}")
                } finally {
                    _isAgentTyping.value = false
                }
                return@launch
            }
            if (message.contains("先不要")) {
                _chatMessages.value = _chatMessages.value + ChatMessage("curator", "好的，如果您之後需要幫忙，隨時可以告訴我！")
                _isAgentTyping.value = false
                return@launch
            }

            // AUTO_FILL intent is strictly prevented from executing research automatically.
            // Only user explicit action via "開始考據" button can trigger research and DB modification.

            val personality = curatorPersonality.ifBlank { "親切熱情" }
            val res = agent.chat(history, context, personality)
            _isAgentTyping.value = false
            _chatMessages.value = _chatMessages.value + ChatMessage("curator", res.reply)
        }
    }

    private suspend fun startResearchAndFillDataWithProgress(
        game: GameEntity,
        agent: AICuratorAgent,
        engine: com.example.research.ResearchEngine,
        repo: com.example.settings.SettingsRepository,
        onProgress: (String) -> Unit
    ): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        logUserAction("RESEARCH", game.title)
        val missingFields = com.example.agent.GameDataCompleteness.checkMissingFields(game)
        if (missingFields.isEmpty()) {
            onProgress("📝 資料已是最新，無需更新。")
            return@withContext false
        }

        onProgress("📋 發現缺失欄位：${missingFields.joinToString()}")
        onProgress("🌐 正在搜尋網路資料庫與文獻...")
        val candidatePair = engine.researchCandidateData(
            game = game,
            missingFields = missingFields,
            searchMode = repo.getWebSearchMode(),
            searchProvider = repo.getWebSearchProvider(),
            searchApiKey = repo.getWebSearchApiKey(),
            searchCustomEndpoint = repo.getWebSearchCustomEndpoint()
        ) ?: return@withContext false

        onProgress("📝 正在驗證並儲存資料...")
        val candidateEntity = candidatePair.first
        val provenanceJson = candidatePair.second
        val existing = repository.getGameById(game.id) ?: game
        val merged = com.example.database.GameRepository.mergeScannedGame(existing, candidateEntity)
        val rootUri = repo.getDescriptionRootUri()
        val syncResult = metadataCsvService.syncGameDescription(rootUri, merged)
        val finalMerged = syncResult.updatedGame
        repository.updateGame(finalMerged)

        val updatedFields = buildList {
            if (merged.developer != game.developer) add("開發商: ${merged.developer ?: "無"}")
            if (merged.publisher != game.publisher) add("發行商: ${merged.publisher ?: "無"}")
            if (merged.genre != game.genre) add("類型: ${merged.genre ?: "無"}")
            if (merged.releaseDate != game.releaseDate) add("發售日: ${merged.releaseDate ?: "無"}")
            if (merged.description != game.description) add("簡介已更新")
        }
        onProgress(if (updatedFields.isNotEmpty()) {
            "📝 更新了以下資料：\n${updatedFields.joinToString("\n")}"
        } else {
            "📝 資料已是最新，無需更新。"
        })

        val fieldsFound = runCatching {
            org.json.JSONObject(provenanceJson).keys().asSequence().joinToString(", ")
        }.getOrDefault("")
        val csvNotice = when {
            syncResult.action != com.example.metadata.SyncAction.NO_CHANGE -> "並已同步至 Description CSV"
            rootUri != null -> "（Description CSV 保持原樣或已存在）"
            else -> "（尚未設定 Description 資料夾，未寫入外部 CSV）"
        }
        onProgress("✅ 考據完成！已驗證欄位：$fieldsFound，已登錄至館藏資料庫 $csvNotice")
        true
    }

    fun drawFate() {
        val games = _allGames.value
        if (games.isNotEmpty()) {
            _fateGame.value = games.random()
        }
    }

    fun recommendByMood(mood: String): List<GameEntity> {
        val games = _allGames.value
        if (games.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
        val m = mood.lowercase()
        val filtered = when {
            m.contains("action") || m.contains("熱血") || m.contains("爽快") || m.contains("刺激") ->
                games.filter { (it.genre ?: "").contains("動作") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("格鬥") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("射擊") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("ACT") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("STG") }
            m.contains("relax") || m.contains("放鬆") || m.contains("治癒") || m.contains("休閒") ->
                games.filter { (it.genre ?: "").contains("益智") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("休閒") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("音樂") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("PUZ") || (it.platform.equals("GBA", ignoreCase = true) || it.platform.equals("NDS", ignoreCase = true)) }
            m.contains("story") || m.contains("沉浸") || m.contains("冒險") || m.contains("探索") || m.contains("劇情") ->
                games.filter { (it.genre ?: "").contains("RPG") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("角色扮演") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("冒險") || (it.genre ?: "").contains("AVG") }
            m.contains("arcade") || m.contains("街機") || m.contains("硬核") || m.contains("挑戰") ->
                games.filter { it.platform.contains("Arcade", ignoreCase = true) || it.platform.contains("CPS", ignoreCase = true) || (it.genre ?: "").contains("動作") || it.rating >= 4.0f }
            else -> games
        }
        return if (filtered.isNotEmpty()) filtered.shuffled().take(6) else games.shuffled().take(6)
    }

    fun toggleFavorite(game: GameEntity) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.updateGame(game.copy(isFavorite = !game.isFavorite))
        }
    }

        viewModelScope.launch {

        }
    }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.updateGame(game.copy(playTimeMinutes = game.playTimeMinutes + minutes))
        }
    }

    fun toggleGameCollection(game: GameEntity, collection: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val currentList = game.customCollections.split(",").map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotBlank() }.toMutableList()
            if (currentList.contains(collection)) {
                currentList.remove(collection)
            } else {
                currentList.add(collection)
            }
            repository.updateGame(game.copy(customCollections = currentList.joinToString(",")))
        }
    }

    fun updateGame(game: GameEntity) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val success = if (!game.description.isNullOrBlank()) {
                metadataCsvService.appendOrUpdatePlatformCsv(
                    rootUriOrPath = _descriptionRootUri.value,
                    platform = game.platform,
                    gameTitle = game.title,
                    description = game.description
                )
            } else false
            val updatedWithSync = game.copy(descriptionCsvSynced = if (game.description.isNullOrBlank()) null else success)
            repository.updateGame(updatedWithSync)
            if (_selectedGame.value?.id == game.id) {
                _selectedGame.value = updatedWithSync
            }
        }
    }

    fun deleteGame(game: GameEntity) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.deleteGame(game)
        }
    }

    fun rematchSingleGame(game: GameEntity, assetType: String? = null) {
        val rootUriStr = _assetRootUri.value
        if (rootUriStr.isNullOrBlank()) {
            _scanProgressText.value = "尚未選取 Asset 圖檔根目錄，請先設定"
            return
        }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            withScanning {
                _scanProgressText.value = "正在重新比對《${game.title}》資材..."
                try {
                    repository.getAssetMatchDao()?.deleteRecordForGameAsset(game.id, assetType)
                    val matchedMap = assetDiscoveryService.matchAssetsForLibrary(
                        assetRootUri = Uri.parse(rootUriStr),
                        games = listOf(game),
                        assetMatchDao = repository.getAssetMatchDao(),
                        forceRematch = true
                    ) { current, total, msg ->
                        _scanProgressText.value = "($current/$total) $msg"
                    }

                    for ((gameId, assets) in matchedMap) {
                        val existingGame = _allGames.value.find { it.id == gameId } ?: game
                        val updatedGame = existingGame.copy(
                            coverPath = assets.boxFront ?: existingGame.coverPath,
                            backgroundPath = assets.hero ?: existingGame.backgroundPath,
                            logoPath = assets.logo ?: existingGame.logoPath,
                            screenshotPath = assets.slide ?: existingGame.screenshotPath,
                            iconPath = assets.icon ?: existingGame.iconPath,
                            description = assets.description ?: existingGame.description
                        )
                        repository.updateGame(updatedGame)
                    }
                    _scanProgressText.value = "《${game.title}》資材重新比對完成"
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _scanProgressText.value = "重新比對失敗: ${e.message}"
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun matchLocalAssetsFromAssetRoot(platformFilter: String? = null, missingOnly: Boolean = true, forceRematch: Boolean = false) {
        val rootUriStr = _assetRootUri.value
        if (rootUriStr.isNullOrBlank()) {
            _scanProgressText.value = "尚未選取 Asset 圖檔根目錄，請先設定"
            return
        }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            withScanning {
                _scanProgressText.value = "正在比對本地 Asset 圖檔..."
                try {
                    if (forceRematch) {
                        repository.getAssetMatchDao()?.deleteAllRecords()
                    }
                    val matchedMap = assetDiscoveryService.matchAssetsForLibrary(
                        assetRootUri = Uri.parse(rootUriStr),
                        games = _allGames.value,
                        platformFilter = platformFilter,
                        missingOnly = missingOnly,
                        assetMatchDao = repository.getAssetMatchDao(),
                        forceRematch = forceRematch
                    ) { current, total, msg ->
                        _scanProgressText.value = "($current/$total) $msg"
                    }

                    val currentGamesMap = _allGames.value.associateBy { it.id }
                    val updatedGamesList = mutableListOf<GameEntity>()

                    for ((gameId, assets) in matchedMap) {
                        val existingGame = currentGamesMap[gameId] ?: continue
                        val newCover = assets.boxFront ?: existingGame.coverPath
                        val newBg = assets.hero ?: existingGame.backgroundPath
                        val newLogo = assets.logo ?: existingGame.logoPath
                        val newSlide = assets.slide ?: existingGame.screenshotPath
                        val newIcon = assets.icon ?: existingGame.iconPath

                        var newDesc = existingGame.description
                        var newCsvSynced = existingGame.descriptionCsvSynced
                        if (!assets.description.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            if (assets.isDescriptionFromCsv) {
                                if (existingGame.description != assets.description) {
                                    newDesc = assets.description
                                    newCsvSynced = false
                                }
                            } else {
                                if (existingGame.description.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                    newDesc = assets.description
                                    newCsvSynced = false
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        val isModified = newCover != existingGame.coverPath ||
                                newBg != existingGame.backgroundPath ||
                                newLogo != existingGame.logoPath ||
                                newSlide != existingGame.screenshotPath ||
                                newIcon != existingGame.iconPath ||
                                newDesc != existingGame.description ||
                                newCsvSynced != existingGame.descriptionCsvSynced

                        if (isModified) {
                            updatedGamesList.add(
                                existingGame.copy(
                                    coverPath = newCover,
                                    backgroundPath = newBg,
                                    logoPath = newLogo,
                                    screenshotPath = newSlide,
                                    iconPath = newIcon,
                                    description = newDesc,
                                    descriptionCsvSynced = newCsvSynced
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (updatedGamesList.isNotEmpty()) {
                        repository.updateGames(updatedGamesList)
                    }

                    _scanProgressText.value = "本地 Asset 比對完成！已更新 ${updatedGamesList.size} 款遊戲資產"
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    _scanProgressText.value = "Asset 比對失敗: ${e.message}"
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun scrapeSingleGame(game: GameEntity, kinds: Set<AssetKind>? = null, missingOnly: Boolean = true) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            batchScrapeProgress.value = "正在刮削: ${game.title}..."
            try {
                val scraper = ImageScraperService(getApplication(), sgdbKey)
                val rootUri = _assetRootUri.value?.let { Uri.parse(it) }
                val targetKinds = kinds ?: AssetKind.entries.toSet()
                val report = scraper.scrapeGame(game.title, game.platform, rootUri, targetKinds, missingOnly)

                var newCover = game.coverPath
                var newHero = game.backgroundPath
                var newLogo = game.logoPath
                var newSlide = game.screenshotPath
                var newIcon = game.iconPath

                for (result in report.results) {
                    if (result.localUri != null) {
                        when (result.kind) {
                            AssetKind.BOX_FRONT -> newCover = result.localUri
                            AssetKind.HERO -> newHero = result.localUri
                            AssetKind.TITLE -> newLogo = result.localUri
                            AssetKind.SLIDE -> newSlide = result.localUri
                            AssetKind.ICON -> newIcon = result.localUri
                        }
                    }
                }

                repository.updateAssets(game.id, newCover, newHero, newLogo, newSlide, newIcon)
                batchScrapeProgress.value = "刮削完成: ${game.title} (成功: ${report.successCount}, 跳過: ${report.skippedCount})"
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                batchScrapeProgress.value = "刮削失敗 [${game.title}]: ${e.message}"
            }
        }
    }

    fun startBatchSteamGridScraping(kinds: Set<AssetKind>?) {
        startBatchSteamGridScraping(platform = null, kinds = kinds, missingOnly = true)
    }

    fun startBatchSteamGridScraping(platform: String? = null, kinds: Set<AssetKind>? = null, missingOnly: Boolean = true) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _isBatchScraping.value = true
            try {
                val candidates = if (platform.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    _allGames.value
                } else {
                    _allGames.value.filter { it.platform.equals(platform, ignoreCase = true) }
                }

                if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
                    batchScrapeProgress.value = "沒有符合條件的遊戲可供刮削"
                    return@launch
                }

                val scraper = ImageScraperService(getApplication(), sgdbKey)
                val rootUri = _assetRootUri.value?.let { Uri.parse(it) }
                val targetKinds = kinds ?: AssetKind.entries.toSet()

                var totalSuccess = 0
                for ((index, game) in candidates.withIndex()) {
                    batchScrapeProgress.value = "(${index + 1}/${candidates.size}) 正在刮削: ${game.title}..."
                    try {
                        val report = scraper.scrapeGame(game.title, game.platform, rootUri, targetKinds, missingOnly)
                        var newCover = game.coverPath
                        var newHero = game.backgroundPath
                        var newLogo = game.logoPath
                        var newSlide = game.screenshotPath
                        var newIcon = game.iconPath

                        for (res in report.results) {
                            if (res.localUri != null) {
                                when (res.kind) {
                                    AssetKind.BOX_FRONT -> newCover = res.localUri
                                    AssetKind.HERO -> newHero = res.localUri
                                    AssetKind.TITLE -> newLogo = res.localUri
                                    AssetKind.SLIDE -> newSlide = res.localUri
                                    AssetKind.ICON -> newIcon = res.localUri
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        if (report.successCount > 0) {
                            repository.updateAssets(game.id, newCover, newHero, newLogo, newSlide, newIcon)
                            totalSuccess += report.successCount
                        }
                    } catch (_: Exception) {}
                }

                batchScrapeProgress.value = "批次刮削完畢！共更新 $totalSuccess 個資產項目"
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                batchScrapeProgress.value = "批次刮削失敗: ${e.message}"
            } finally {
                _isBatchScraping.value = false
            }
        }
    }

    // --- Knowledge Services Persistence ---
    suspend fun generateAndSaveDescription(game: GameEntity): String? {
        val result = metadataCsvService.syncGameDescription(
            rootUriOrPath = _descriptionRootUri.value,
            game = game,
            aiGenerator = { g ->
                val agent = getOrCreateCuratorAgent()
                agent.generateDescription(g.title, g.platform)
            }
        )

        if (result.action != SyncAction.NO_CHANGE) {
            repository.updateGame(result.updatedGame)
            if (_selectedGame.value?.id == game.id) {
                _selectedGame.value = result.updatedGame
            }
        }
        return result.updatedGame.description
    }

    suspend fun fetchOrGenerateEvolution(game: GameEntity): com.example.agent.EvolutionData? {
        logUserAction("EVOLUTION_GENERATE", game.title)
        val result = knowledgeService.generateEvolution(game.title, game.platform)
        if (result != null) {
            val json = com.squareup.moshi.Moshi.Builder().build().adapter(com.example.agent.EvolutionData::class.java).toJson(result)
            repository.updateGame(game.copy(evolutionJson = json))
        }
        return result
    }

    suspend fun fetchOrGenerateCheats(game: GameEntity): com.example.agent.CheatGuideData? {
        logUserAction("CHEATS_GUIDE", game.title)
        val result = knowledgeService.generateCheats(game.title, game.platform)
        val json = com.squareup.moshi.Moshi.Builder().build().adapter(com.example.agent.CheatGuideData::class.java).toJson(result)
        repository.updateGame(game.copy(cheatsJson = json))
        return result
    }

    suspend fun fetchOrGenerateMuseum(game: GameEntity): com.example.agent.MuseumFields? {
        logUserAction("MUSEUM_HISTORY", game.title)
        val result = knowledgeService.generateMuseum(game.title, game.platform)
        if (result != null) {
            val json = com.squareup.moshi.Moshi.Builder().build().adapter(com.example.agent.MuseumFields::class.java).toJson(result)
            repository.updateGame(game.copy(museumJson = json))
        }
        return result
    }

    suspend fun fetchOrGenerateDna(game: GameEntity): com.example.agent.GameDna? {
        logUserAction("DNA_GENERATE", game.title)
        val result = knowledgeService.generateDna(game.title, game.platform)
        if (result != null) {
            val json = com.squareup.moshi.Moshi.Builder().build().adapter(com.example.agent.GameDna::class.java).toJson(result)
            repository.updateGame(game.copy(dnaJson = json))
        }
        return result
    }

    fun findLibraryMatchForTitle(title: String): GameEntity? {
        return _allGames.value.firstOrNull { it.title.equals(title, ignoreCase = true) }
    }
}

================================================================================

)

    fun isPackageInstalled(context: Context, packageName: String): Boolean {
        return try {
            context.packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, 0)
            true
        } catch (e: PackageManager.NameNotFoundException) {
            false
        }
    }

    /**
     * Resolves the actual installed package for a launcher profile,
     * checking its primary package and any alternative package variants.
     */
    fun resolveInstalledPackage(context: Context, profile: LauncherProfile): String? {
        if (isPackageInstalled(context, profile.packageName)) {
            return profile.packageName
        }
        for (alt in profile.alternativePackages) {
            if (isPackageInstalled(context, alt)) {
                return alt
            }
        }
        return null
    }

    fun getInstalledLaunchersForPlatform(context: Context, platform: String): List<LauncherProfile> {
        val canonicalPlat = PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(platform).uppercase()
        val upperPlat = platform.uppercase()
        val launcherIds = PLATFORM_LAUNCHERS[canonicalPlat] ?: PLATFORM_LAUNCHERS[upperPlat] ?: run {
            if (upperPlat.contains("ARCADE") || upperPlat.contains("MAME") || upperPlat.contains("CAPCOM") || upperPlat.contains("NEO")) {
                PLATFORM_LAUNCHERS["ARCADE"] ?: emptyList()
            } else emptyList()
        }
        return launcherIds.mapNotNull { id ->
            val profile = LAUNCHER_PROFILES[id] ?: return@mapNotNull null
            val installedPkg = resolveInstalledPackage(context, profile)
            if (installedPkg != null) {
                profile.copy(packageName = installedPkg)
            } else null
        }
    }

    data class CoreOption(val id: String, val label: String, val coreFileName: String)
    data class EmulatorOption(val id: String, val label: String, val packageName: String, val isRetroArch: Boolean, val cores: List<CoreOption> = emptyList())
    data class PlatformEmulatorConfig(
        val platform: String,
        val displayName: String,
        val emulators: List<EmulatorOption>,
        val defaultEmulatorId: String,
        val defaultCoreFileName: String?
    )

    fun disableStrictModeFileUriExposure() {
        try {
            val builder = StrictMode.VmPolicy.Builder()
            StrictMode.setVmPolicy(builder.build())
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // ignore
        }
    }

    fun launchEdenEmulator(context: Context, packageName: String, romPathUri: String): Boolean {
        disableStrictModeFileUriExposure()
        val resolvedRomPath = getFileSystemPathFromUri(context, romPathUri)
        val romFile = File(resolvedRomPath)

        val fileUri = Uri.fromFile(romFile)
        val parsedUri = try {
            if (romPathUri.startsWith("content://") || romPathUri.startsWith("file://")) {
                Uri.parse(romPathUri)
            } else {
                fileUri
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            fileUri
        }

        val urisToTry = listOf(parsedUri, fileUri).distinct()

        val activities = listOf(
            "$packageName.features.emulation.ui.EmulationActivity",
            "org.eden.eden_emu.features.emulation.ui.EmulationActivity",
            "$packageName.ui.main.MainActivity",
            "org.eden.eden_emu.ui.main.MainActivity"
        ).distinct()

        for (act in activities) {
            for (targetUri in urisToTry) {
                try {
                    val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                        component = android.content.ComponentName(packageName, act)
                        setDataAndType(targetUri, "application/octet-stream")
                        clipData = ClipData.newRawUri("ROM", targetUri)
                        putExtra("ROM", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("path", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("GamePath", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("EXTRA_GAME_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("EXTRA_ROM_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("selected_title", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("selectedTitle", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("org.eden.eden_emu.ROM_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("org.yuzu.yuzu_emu.ROM_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or
                                Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP or
                                Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or
                                Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION or
                                Intent.FLAG_GRANT_PERSISTABLE_URI_PERMISSION
                    }
                    context.startActivity(intent)
                    android.util.Log.d("EdenLaunch", "Launched Eden with activity=$act uri=$targetUri")
                    return true
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    android.util.Log.w("EdenLaunch", "Eden activity=$act failed: ${e.message}")
                }
            }
        }

        // Implicit ACTION_VIEW for package
        for (targetUri in urisToTry) {
            try {
                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                    setPackage(packageName)
                    setDataAndType(targetUri, "application/octet-stream")
                    clipData = ClipData.newRawUri("ROM", targetUri)
                    putExtra("ROM", resolvedRomPath)
                    putExtra("path", resolvedRomPath)
                    putExtra("GamePath", resolvedRomPath)
                    putExtra("EXTRA_GAME_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                    putExtra("EXTRA_ROM_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                    flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or
                            Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP or
                            Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or
                            Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION
                }
                context.startActivity(intent)
                android.util.Log.d("EdenLaunch", "Launched Eden with package ACTION_VIEW uri=$targetUri")
                return true
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                android.util.Log.w("EdenLaunch", "Eden package ACTION_VIEW failed: ${e.message}")
            }
        }

        return false
    }

    fun launchYuzuEmulator(context: Context, packageName: String, romPathUri: String): Boolean {
        disableStrictModeFileUriExposure()
        val resolvedRomPath = getFileSystemPathFromUri(context, romPathUri)
        val fileUri = Uri.fromFile(File(resolvedRomPath))
        val parsedUri = try {
            if (romPathUri.startsWith("content://") || romPathUri.startsWith("file://")) Uri.parse(romPathUri) else fileUri
        } catch (e: Exception) { fileUri }

        val uris = listOf(parsedUri, fileUri).distinct()
        val activities = listOf(
            "$packageName.features.emulation.ui.EmulationActivity",
            "org.yuzu.yuzu_emu.features.emulation.ui.EmulationActivity",
            "$packageName.ui.main.MainActivity"
        ).distinct()

        for (act in activities) {
            for (uri in uris) {
                try {
                    val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                        component = android.content.ComponentName(packageName, act)
                        setDataAndType(uri, "application/octet-stream")
                        clipData = ClipData.newRawUri("ROM", uri)
                        putExtra("ROM", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("path", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("GamePath", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("EXTRA_GAME_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("EXTRA_ROM_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION
                    }
                    context.startActivity(intent)
                    return true
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    // Try next
                }
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    fun launchSudachiEmulator(context: Context, packageName: String, romPathUri: String): Boolean {
        disableStrictModeFileUriExposure()
        val resolvedRomPath = getFileSystemPathFromUri(context, romPathUri)
        val fileUri = Uri.fromFile(File(resolvedRomPath))
        val parsedUri = try {
            if (romPathUri.startsWith("content://") || romPathUri.startsWith("file://")) Uri.parse(romPathUri) else fileUri
        } catch (e: Exception) { fileUri }

        val uris = listOf(parsedUri, fileUri).distinct()
        val activities = listOf(
            "$packageName.features.emulation.ui.EmulationActivity",
            "dev.sudachi.sudachi.features.emulation.ui.EmulationActivity",
            "$packageName.ui.main.MainActivity"
        ).distinct()

        for (act in activities) {
            for (uri in uris) {
                try {
                    val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                        component = android.content.ComponentName(packageName, act)
                        setDataAndType(uri, "application/octet-stream")
                        clipData = ClipData.newRawUri("ROM", uri)
                        putExtra("ROM", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("path", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("EXTRA_GAME_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION
                    }
                    context.startActivity(intent)
                    return true
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    // Try next
                }
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    fun launchSkylineEmulator(context: Context, packageName: String, romPathUri: String): Boolean {
        disableStrictModeFileUriExposure()
        val resolvedRomPath = getFileSystemPathFromUri(context, romPathUri)
        val fileUri = Uri.fromFile(File(resolvedRomPath))
        val parsedUri = try {
            if (romPathUri.startsWith("content://") || romPathUri.startsWith("file://")) Uri.parse(romPathUri) else fileUri
        } catch (e: Exception) { fileUri }

        val uris = listOf(parsedUri, fileUri).distinct()
        val activities = listOf(
            "skyline.MainActivity",
            "$packageName.MainActivity"
        ).distinct()

        for (act in activities) {
            for (uri in uris) {
                try {
                    val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                        component = android.content.ComponentName(packageName, act)
                        setDataAndType(uri, "application/octet-stream")
                        clipData = ClipData.newRawUri("ROM", uri)
                        putExtra("ROM", resolvedRomPath)
                        flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION
                    }
                    context.startActivity(intent)
                    return true
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    // Try next
                }
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    fun launchGame(context: Context, game: com.example.database.GameEntity) {
        launchGame(context, game.platform, game.romPath, game.title)
    }

    fun getFileSystemPathFromUri(context: Context, uriString: String): String {
        try {
            if (uriString.startsWith("content://com.android.externalstorage.documents")) {
                val uri = Uri.parse(uriString)
                val docId = android.provider.DocumentsContract.getDocumentId(uri)
                val split = docId.split(":")
                if (split.size >= 2) {
                    val type = split[0]
                    val path = split[1]
                    if ("primary".equals(type, ignoreCase = true)) {
                        return android.os.Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory().toString() + "/" + path
                    } else {
                        return "/storage/" + type + "/" + path
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            android.util.Log.e("LauncherBridge", "Failed to parse SAF URI", e)
        }
        return if (uriString.startsWith("file://")) uriString.removePrefix("file://") else uriString
    }

    fun launchRetroArchDirect(context: Context, packageName: String, coreFileName: String?, romPathUri: String): Boolean {
        disableStrictModeFileUriExposure()
        val resolvedRomPath = getFileSystemPathFromUri(context, romPathUri)
        val configFile = "/data/data/$packageName/retroarch.cfg"
        val libretroCore = if (!coreFileName.isNullOrBlank()) {
            if (coreFileName.startsWith("/")) coreFileName else "/data/data/$packageName/cores/$coreFileName"
        } else {
            ""
        }

        android.util.Log.d("RetroArchLaunch", "RetroArch Launch\n" +
                "package=$packageName\n" +
                "action=android.intent.action.MAIN\n" +
                "romOriginal=$romPathUri\n" +
                "romResolved=$resolvedRomPath\n" +
                "configFile=$configFile\n" +
                "libretro=$libretroCore")

        val activities = listOf(
            "com.retroarch.browser.retroactivity.RetroActivityFuture",
            "com.retroarch.browser.retroactivity.RetroActivity"
        )

        for (act in activities) {
            try {
                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_MAIN).apply {
                    component = android.content.ComponentName(packageName, act)
                    putExtra("ROM", resolvedRomPath)
                    if (libretroCore.isNotEmpty()) {
                        putExtra("LIBRETRO", libretroCore)
                    }
                    putExtra("CONFIGFILE", configFile)
                    flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
                }
                context.startActivity(intent)
                return true
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // Try next activity
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    fun launchGame(
        context: Context,
        platform: String,
        romPathUri: String,
        title: String,
        preferredLauncherId: String? = null,
        overrideCore: String? = null
    ): Boolean {
        disableStrictModeFileUriExposure()
        val canonicalPlat = PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(platform).uppercase()
        val upperPlat = platform.uppercase()

        // 1. Android App package direct launch
        if (romPathUri.startsWith("app://")) {
            val pkg = romPathUri.removePrefix("app://")
            val launchIntent = context.packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(pkg)
            if (launchIntent != null) {
                launchIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                context.startActivity(launchIntent)
                return true
            } else {
                Toast.makeText(context, "找不到應用程式: $pkg", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                return false
            }
        }

        // Check persistent settings override (User Override -> Platform Default -> Global Default)
        val settingsRepo = com.example.settings.SettingsRepository(context)
        val emuMode = settingsRepo.getEmulatorMode(canonicalPlat)
        val activeLauncherId = if (emuMode == "MANUAL") {
            settingsRepo.getEmulatorOverride(canonicalPlat) ?: preferredLauncherId
        } else {
            preferredLauncherId ?: getPlatformEmulatorConfigs().find { it.platform.equals(canonicalPlat, ignoreCase = true) }?.defaultEmulatorId
        }
        val activeCore = if (emuMode == "MANUAL") {
            settingsRepo.getCoreOverride(canonicalPlat) ?: overrideCore
        } else {
            overrideCore ?: getPlatformEmulatorConfigs().find { it.platform.equals(canonicalPlat, ignoreCase = true) }?.defaultCoreFileName
        }

        val preferredProfile = activeLauncherId?.let { LAUNCHER_PROFILES[it] }

        // Find candidates by canonical and original platform
        val launcherIds = PLATFORM_LAUNCHERS[canonicalPlat] ?: PLATFORM_LAUNCHERS[upperPlat] ?: run {
            if (upperPlat.contains("ARCADE") || upperPlat.contains("MAME") || upperPlat.contains("CAPCOM") || upperPlat.contains("NEO")) {
                PLATFORM_LAUNCHERS["ARCADE"] ?: emptyList()
            } else emptyList()
        }

        val candidateProfile = preferredProfile ?: run {
            launcherIds.mapNotNull { LAUNCHER_PROFILES[it] }.firstOrNull { profile ->
                resolveInstalledPackage(context, profile) != null
            }
        } ?: return false

        val resolvedPkg = resolveInstalledPackage(context, candidateProfile) ?: return false

        val uri = try {
            if (romPathUri.startsWith("content://") || romPathUri.startsWith("file://")) {
                Uri.parse(romPathUri)
            } else {
                Uri.parse("file://$romPathUri")
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Uri.parse(romPathUri)
        }

        try {
            // 2. RetroArch launch with proper activity names & core
            if (candidateProfile.kind == "retroarch") {
                val coreToUse = activeCore ?: candidateProfile.core
                val success = launchRetroArchDirect(context, resolvedPkg, coreToUse, romPathUri)
                if (success) return true
            }

            // 3. Dedicated Switch Emulator Dispatchers
            if (candidateProfile.id == "eden" || (canonicalPlat == "SWITCH" && resolvedPkg.contains("eden"))) {
                val success = launchEdenEmulator(context, resolvedPkg, romPathUri)
                if (success) return true
            } else if (candidateProfile.id == "yuzu" || (canonicalPlat == "SWITCH" && resolvedPkg.contains("yuzu"))) {
                val success = launchYuzuEmulator(context, resolvedPkg, romPathUri)
                if (success) return true
            } else if (candidateProfile.id == "suyu" || (canonicalPlat == "SWITCH" && resolvedPkg.contains("suyu"))) {
                val success = launchSuyuEmulator(context, resolvedPkg, romPathUri)
                if (success) return true
            } else if (candidateProfile.id == "sudachi" || (canonicalPlat == "SWITCH" && resolvedPkg.contains("sudachi"))) {
                val success = launchSudachiEmulator(context, resolvedPkg, romPathUri)
                if (success) return true
            } else if (candidateProfile.id == "skyline" || (canonicalPlat == "SWITCH" && resolvedPkg.contains("skyline"))) {
                val success = launchSkylineEmulator(context, resolvedPkg, romPathUri)
                if (success) return true
            }

            // 4. Standalone Emulator launch via View Intent
            if (candidateProfile.kind == "view-intent" && isPackageInstalled(context, resolvedPkg)) {
                val resolvedRomPath = getFileSystemPathFromUri(context, romPathUri)
                try {
                    val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                        setDataAndType(uri, "*/*")
                        clipData = ClipData.newRawUri("ROM", uri)
                        setPackage(resolvedPkg)
                        putExtra("ROM", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("EXTRA_GAME_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("EXTRA_ROM_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION
                    }
                    context.startActivity(intent)
                    return true
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    // Fallback to launcher default activity
                    val fallbackIntent = context.packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(resolvedPkg)?.apply {
                        setDataAndType(uri, "*/*")
                        clipData = ClipData.newRawUri("ROM", uri)
                        putExtra("ROM", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("EXTRA_GAME_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        putExtra("EXTRA_ROM_PATH", resolvedRomPath)
                        flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION
                    }
                    if (fallbackIntent != null) {
                        context.startActivity(fallbackIntent)
                        return true
                    }
                }
            }

            // 5. Any installed RetroArch fallback if standalone was not installed
            for (raPkg in RETROARCH_64_PACKAGES) {
                if (isPackageInstalled(context, raPkg)) {
                    val coreToUse = activeCore ?: candidateProfile.core
                    val success = launchRetroArchDirect(context, raPkg, coreToUse, romPathUri)
                    if (success) return true
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
        }
        return false
    }
}
