package com.example

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.KeyEvent
import android.view.MotionEvent
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
import androidx.core.view.WindowInsetsCompat
import androidx.core.view.WindowInsetsControllerCompat
import com.example.ui.theme.MyApplicationTheme
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableSharedFlow

val GlobalGamepadEvents = MutableSharedFlow<Int>(extraBufferCapacity = 64)

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
  override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    enableEdgeToEdge()
    
    val windowInsetsController = WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView)
    windowInsetsController.systemBarsBehavior = WindowInsetsControllerCompat.BEHAVIOR_SHOW_TRANSIENT_BARS_BY_SWIPE
    windowInsetsController.hide(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
    
    setContent {
      MyApplicationTheme(darkTheme = true) {
        GameLauncherScreen()
      }
    }
  }

  override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent?): Boolean {
    GlobalGamepadEvents.tryEmit(keyCode)
    return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/agent/AICuratorAgent.kt
================================================================================
package com.example.agent

import com.example.api.AiFeature
import com.example.api.ApiKeyRotationManager
import com.example.api.ApiProfile
import com.example.api.AiProviderResolver
import com.example.api.AiRequest
import com.example.research.ResearchEngine
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

data class ChatTurn(
    val role: String, // "user" or "assistant"
    val content: String
)

data class CuratorContext(
    val librarySize: Int = 0,
    val currentPlatform: String = "",
    val selectedGameTitle: String? = null,
    val selectedGamePlatform: String? = null,
    val selectedGameDescription: String? = null,
    val selectedGameGenre: String? = null,
    val selectedGameReleaseYear: String? = null,
    val selectedGameDeveloper: String? = null,
    val missingAssetCount: Int = 0,
    val favoriteCount: Int = 0,
    val missingDataFields: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val currentScreen: String? = null,
    val currentMuseumTab: String? = null,
    val lastAction: String? = null,
    val lastActionTarget: String? = null,
    val allGames: List<com.example.database.GameEntity> = emptyList()
)

data class CuratorResponse(
    val reply: String,
    val providerUsed: String,
    val modelUsed: String,
    val toolCallSuggestion: String? = null
)

class AICuratorAgent(
    private val profiles: List<ApiProfile>,
    private val rotationManager: ApiKeyRotationManager = ApiKeyRotationManager(),
    private val resolver: AiProviderResolver = AiProviderResolver(),
    private val researchEngine: ResearchEngine? = null,
    private val searchMode: String = "SMART_SEARCH",
    private val searchProvider: String = "DUCKDUCKGO",
    private val searchApiKey: String? = null,
    private val searchCustomEndpoint: String? = null,
    private val enrichmentService: MetadataEnrichmentService? = null,
    private val allGamesProvider: (suspend () -> List<com.example.database.GameEntity>)? = null
) {

    suspend fun evaluateActionIntent(userMessage: String, context: CuratorContext): String? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        if (context.missingDataFields.isEmpty()) return@withContext null
        
        val systemPrompt = """
            玩家目前正在瀏覽遊戲《${context.selectedGameTitle}》。
            該遊戲缺少以下資料：${context.missingDataFields.joinToString()}。
            請分析玩家的最後一句話，判斷玩家是否同意或要求你「幫忙考據並補齊缺失的資料」。
            請只輸出 JSON 格式：{"intent": "AUTO_FILL"} 或 {"intent": "NONE"}
        """.trimIndent()
        
        val request = AiRequest(
            prompt = userMessage,
            systemPrompt = systemPrompt,
            temperature = 0.1f,
            maxTokens = TokenBudgetManager.resolveOutputBudget("ACTION_INTENT")
        )
        
        val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(AiFeature.CURATOR, profiles)
        val exec = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, request)
        if (exec.success && exec.text.contains("AUTO_FILL")) {
            return@withContext "AUTO_FILL"
        }
        return@withContext null
    }

    /**
     * True multi-turn conversation with the AI Curator with full library context, local CSV data, and Smart Search support.
     */
    suspend fun chat(
        conversation: List<ChatTurn>,
        context: CuratorContext,
        personality: String = "親切熱情"
    ): CuratorResponse = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val lastUserMessage = conversation.lastOrNull { it.role == "user" }?.content.orEmpty()
        val dedupKey = "curator_${lastUserMessage.trim().lowercase()}_${context.selectedGameTitle.orEmpty()}_${context.allGames.size}"
        TokenBudgetManager.getDeduplicatedResponse(dedupKey)?.let { cachedReply ->
            return@withContext CuratorResponse(
                reply = cachedReply,
                providerUsed = "Cache",
                modelUsed = "Dedup"
            )
        }

        val router = CuratorRouter(profiles, rotationManager, resolver)
        val decision = router.routeIntent(lastUserMessage, context)

        when (decision.intent) {
            CuratorIntent.DIRECT_DATABASE -> {
                val games = context.allGames.filter { g ->
                    (decision.platformFilter == null || g.platform.contains(decision.platformFilter, ignoreCase = true)) &&
                    (decision.genreFilter == null || g.genre?.contains(decision.genreFilter, ignoreCase = true) == true)
                }
                TokenBudgetManager.recordRetrieval(context.allGames.size, games.size)
                return@withContext CuratorResponse(
                    reply = "根據資料庫查詢，您有 ${games.size} 款符合條件的遊戲。",
                    providerUsed = "LocalDB",
                    modelUsed = "DirectQuery"
                )
            }
            CuratorIntent.BATCH_ANALYSIS -> {
                val games = context.allGames.filter { g ->
                    (decision.platformFilter == null || g.platform.contains(decision.platformFilter, ignoreCase = true)) &&
                    (decision.genreFilter == null || g.genre?.contains(decision.genreFilter, ignoreCase = true) == true)
                }
                TokenBudgetManager.recordRetrieval(context.allGames.size, games.size)
                
                if (games.isEmpty()) {
                    return@withContext CuratorResponse(
                        reply = "資料庫中沒有找到符合條件的遊戲。",
                        providerUsed = "LocalDB",
                        modelUsed = "RouterDecision"
                    )
                }

                // Adaptive chunking: chunk by 50, but we can dynamically adjust based on context limit
                val chunkSize = 50
                val chunks = games.chunked(chunkSize)
                val intermediateResults = mutableListOf<String>()
                val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(AiFeature.CURATOR, profiles)
                
                for ((idx, chunk) in chunks.withIndex()) {
                    val compactBatch = chunk.joinToString("\n") { g ->
                        "- ${g.title} [${g.platform}] (${g.genre ?: "無分類"})"
                    }
                    val batchPrompt = "玩家查詢：$lastUserMessage\n請極度精簡地從下列清單中挑選出符合查詢的代表作，或總結關鍵特徵：\n$compactBatch"
                    val batchRequest = AiRequest(
                        prompt = batchPrompt,
                        systemPrompt = "你是一個資料壓縮助理。請用條列式或極度精簡的文字輸出分析結果，禁止任何開場白或長篇大論。",
                        temperature = 0.3f,
                        maxTokens = 300
                    )
                    val res = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, batchRequest)
                    if (res.success && res.text.isNotBlank()) {
                        intermediateResults.add("批次 ${idx + 1} 摘要:\n${res.text.trim()}")
                    }
                }
                
                val finalPrompt = "玩家問題：$lastUserMessage\n以下是從全資料庫分批掃描所提取的特徵與代表作摘要：\n${intermediateResults.joinToString("\n\n")}\n\n請綜合上述分析，以 AI 館長的身分給出最終推薦或回答。"
                val finalRequest = AiRequest(
                    prompt = finalPrompt,
                    systemPrompt = "你是 Sylphora 專屬 AI 館長。性格：$personality。請根據提供的分批摘要給出專業、熱情的總結性回答。",
                    temperature = 0.7f,
                    maxTokens = TokenBudgetManager.resolveOutputBudget("CHAT")
                )
                val finalRes = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, finalRequest)
                
                return@withContext if (finalRes.success && finalRes.text.isNotBlank()) {
                    CuratorResponse(
                        reply = finalRes.text.trim(),
                        providerUsed = finalRes.provider,
                        modelUsed = finalRes.model
                    )
                } else {
                    CuratorResponse(
                        reply = "抱歉，在跨庫大批次分析時發生錯誤 (${finalRes.toHumanReadableError()})",
                        providerUsed = finalRes.provider,
                        modelUsed = finalRes.model
                    )
                }
            }
            CuratorIntent.METADATA_ENRICHMENT -> {
                val fieldsToEnrich = decision.requiredFields
                val gamesToEnrich = context.allGames.filter { g ->
                    fieldsToEnrich.any { field ->
                        when (field.lowercase()) {
                            "developer" -> g.developer.isNullOrBlank()
                            "publisher" -> g.publisher.isNullOrBlank()
                            "genre" -> g.genre.isNullOrBlank()
                            "releasedate" -> g.releaseDate.isNullOrBlank()
                            "composer" -> g.composer.isNullOrBlank()
                            "description" -> g.description.isNullOrBlank()
                            else -> false
                        }
                    } && (decision.platformFilter == null || g.platform.contains(decision.platformFilter, ignoreCase = true))
                }.take(10) // Limit to prevent massive requests

                var anyEnriched = false
                if (enrichmentService != null && gamesToEnrich.isNotEmpty()) {
                    for (game in gamesToEnrich) {
                        val missingForGame = fieldsToEnrich.filter { field ->
                             when (field.lowercase()) {
                                "developer" -> game.developer.isNullOrBlank()
                                "publisher" -> game.publisher.isNullOrBlank()
                                "genre" -> game.genre.isNullOrBlank()
                                "releasedate" -> game.releaseDate.isNullOrBlank()
                                "composer" -> game.composer.isNullOrBlank()
                                "description" -> game.description.isNullOrBlank()
                                else -> false
                             }
                        }
                        if (missingForGame.isNotEmpty()) {
                            val success = enrichmentService.enrichGameMetadata(game, missingForGame)
                            if (success) {
                                anyEnriched = true
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (anyEnriched && allGamesProvider != null) {
                    val freshGames = allGamesProvider.invoke()
                    val newContext = context.copy(allGames = freshGames)
                    
                    // Re-route the intent with fresh context (avoids infinite loop because data is no longer missing, assuming threshold met)
                    val newDecision = router.routeIntent(lastUserMessage, newContext)
                    
                    if (newDecision.intent != CuratorIntent.METADATA_ENRICHMENT) {
                        return@withContext chat(conversation, newContext, personality)
                    }
                }
                
                val fields = decision.requiredFields.joinToString()
                return@withContext CuratorResponse(
                    reply = "我注意到有部分遊戲缺少 $fields 的資料，且我目前無法達到足夠的信心水準為您補齊。請至「進階設定」或針對該遊戲點選「開始考據」來補充，以便我為您提供更精確的分析。",
                    providerUsed = "LocalDB",
                    modelUsed = "RouterDecision"
                )
            }
            else -> {} // Proceed to standard AI handling below
        }

        // 1. Check if Smart Search / Research is needed when ResearchEngine is present
        if (researchEngine != null && lastUserMessage.isNotBlank() && decision.intent == CuratorIntent.RESEARCH) {
            val (needSearch, reason) = researchEngine.evaluateNeedWebSearch(
                userPrompt = lastUserMessage,
                searchMode = searchMode,
                localDataAvailable = !context.selectedGameDescription.isNullOrBlank()
            )

            if (needSearch) {
                val researchRes = researchEngine.performResearch(
                    gameTitle = context.selectedGameTitle.orEmpty(),
                    platform = context.selectedGamePlatform.orEmpty(),
                    userQuery = lastUserMessage,
                    searchMode = searchMode,
                    searchProvider = searchProvider,
                    searchApiKey = searchApiKey,
                    searchCustomEndpoint = searchCustomEndpoint,
                    localDescription = context.selectedGameDescription,
                    appContext = context
                )

                return@withContext CuratorResponse(
                    reply = researchRes.answerText,
                    providerUsed = researchRes.providerUsed.ifBlank { "WebSearch" },
                    modelUsed = researchRes.modelUsed.ifBlank { "CuratorAI" }
                )
            }
        }

        // 2. Direct AI Conversation (Search not required or SEARCH_OFF)
        val systemPrompt = buildString {
            append("你是 Sylphora 復古遊戲主機庫的專屬 AI 館長 (Curator)。\n")
            append("你精通電子遊戲歷史、硬體架構、名作背景、隱藏秘笈與玩家社群梗。\n")
            append("【你的性格設定】：$personality。請根據此性格設定對話風格與語意。\n")
            append("【目前館藏狀態】：總遊戲數: ${context.librarySize} 款，當前瀏覽平台: ${context.currentPlatform.ifBlank { "全部" }}，收藏遊戲: ${context.favoriteCount} 款。\n")
            
            if (decision.intent == CuratorIntent.DATABASE_PLUS_AI || decision.intent == CuratorIntent.ANALYSIS) {
                val filteredGames = context.allGames.filter { g ->
                    (decision.platformFilter == null || g.platform.contains(decision.platformFilter, ignoreCase = true)) &&
                    (decision.genreFilter == null || g.genre?.contains(decision.genreFilter, ignoreCase = true) == true)
                }.take(30)
                TokenBudgetManager.recordRetrieval(context.allGames.size, filteredGames.size)
                
                if (filteredGames.isNotEmpty()) {
                    append("【檢索到的候選遊戲 (精簡摘要)】：\n")
                    filteredGames.forEach { g ->
                        val metadataHash = java.util.Objects.hash(g.title, g.platform, g.genre, g.releaseDate, g.rating, g.isFavorite, g.playCount, g.playStatus)
                        val summary = TokenBudgetManager.getOrCreateCompactSummary(g.id, metadataHash) {
                            val year = g.releaseDate?.take(4) ?: "未知"
                            val fav = if (g.isFavorite) "★" else ""
                            "- [${g.platform}] ${g.title} (類型: ${g.genre ?: "未知"}, 年份: $year, 評分: ${g.rating}★$fav)"
                        }
                        append(summary).append("\n")
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!context.selectedGameTitle.isNullOrBlank()) {
                append("【玩家目前選取的作品】：【${context.selectedGamePlatform}】《${context.selectedGameTitle}》")
                if (!context.selectedGameGenre.isNullOrBlank()) append(" (類型: ${context.selectedGameGenre})")
                if (!context.selectedGameDeveloper.isNullOrBlank()) append(" (開發商: ${context.selectedGameDeveloper})")
                if (!context.selectedGameReleaseYear.isNullOrBlank()) append(" (發售年份: ${context.selectedGameReleaseYear})")
                append("\n")
                if (!context.selectedGameDescription.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    append("【本機館藏說明 (CSV / 官方紀錄)】: ${context.selectedGameDescription}\n")
                    append("注意：解答關於該遊戲背景或特色時，請優先以【本機館藏說明】為依據與起點。\n")
                }
                
                if (context.missingDataFields.isNotEmpty()) {
                    append("【發現本機資料缺失】：目前這款遊戲缺少以下欄位資料：${context.missingDataFields.joinToString()}。\n")
                    append("【主動補齊任務】：當玩家主動詢問或你正在介紹這款遊戲時，若發現資料缺失，請你在回答完玩家的問題後，主動詢問玩家：「我注意到這款遊戲的 ${context.missingDataFields.first()} 等資訊缺失，需要我為您考據並補齊資料嗎？」\n")
                    append("注意：請勿在沒有確認的情況下自行捏造或直接宣稱已補齊資料，僅主動詢問玩家意願即可。\n")
                }
            }

            if (!context.currentScreen.isNullOrBlank()) {
                append("【使用者操作情境】：目前處於 ${context.currentScreen}")
                if (!context.currentMuseumTab.isNullOrBlank()) append(" (分頁: ${context.currentMuseumTab})")
                if (!context.lastAction.isNullOrBlank()) append("，最近執行動作: ${context.lastAction} (目標: ${context.lastActionTarget ?: "無"})")
                append("\n")
            }

            if (searchMode.equals("SEARCH_OFF", ignoreCase = true)) {
                append("【網路搜尋狀態】：目前已關閉網路搜尋，請依 AI 館長現有知識與本機資料回答。若玩家要求即時查證，請親切說明目前已關閉網路搜尋。\n")
            }

            append("請以親切、專業、富有歷史底蘊的繁體中文與玩家對話。嚴禁捏造任何未發起的搜尋連結。")
        }

        val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(AiFeature.CURATOR, profiles)
        val modelUsed = targetProfiles.firstOrNull()?.model ?: ""
        val compression = TokenBudgetManager.compressHistoryWithSummary(
            systemPrompt = systemPrompt,
            latestMessage = lastUserMessage,
            history = conversation.dropLast(1).map { it.role to it.content },
            modelName = modelUsed
        )

        val finalSystemPrompt = if (!compression.prunedSummary.isNullOrBlank()) {
            "$systemPrompt\n${compression.prunedSummary}\n"
        } else {
            systemPrompt
        }

        val request = AiRequest(
            prompt = lastUserMessage,
            systemPrompt = finalSystemPrompt,
            conversationHistory = compression.compressedHistory,
            temperature = 0.7f,
            maxTokens = TokenBudgetManager.resolveOutputBudget("CHAT")
        )
        val execResult = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, request)

        if (execResult.success && execResult.text.isNotBlank()) {
            val replyText = execResult.text.trim()
            TokenBudgetManager.putDeduplicatedResponse(dedupKey, replyText)
            CuratorResponse(
                reply = replyText,
                providerUsed = execResult.provider,
                modelUsed = execResult.model
            )
        } else {
            CuratorResponse(
                reply = "館長暫時無法回應 (${execResult.toHumanReadableError()})",
                providerUsed = execResult.provider,
                modelUsed = execResult.model
            )
        }
    }

    /**
     * Specialized clean description generator.
     */
    suspend fun generateDescription(gameTitle: String, platform: String): String? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val cacheKey = "desc_${platform}_${gameTitle}"
        TokenBudgetManager.getDeduplicatedResponse(cacheKey)?.let { return@withContext it }

        val prompt = "請為「$platform」平台上的電子遊戲《$gameTitle》寫一段繁體中文的背景介紹與特色說明。" +
                "要求：1. 字數約 100 至 150 字。 2. 直接輸出介紹內文，嚴禁任何開場白或結語。 3. 若完全不知道此遊戲，僅回覆：UNKNOWN_GAME"

        val request = AiRequest(
            prompt = prompt,
            systemPrompt = "你是一位精通復古電玩與遊戲歷史的資深電玩博覽館長。請以客觀且富有趣味的角度撰寫遊戲特色。",
            temperature = 0.5f,
            maxTokens = TokenBudgetManager.resolveOutputBudget("DESCRIPTION")
        )

        val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(AiFeature.DESCRIPTION, profiles)
        val result = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, request)

        if (!result.success || result.text.isBlank()) return@withContext null

        val cleanText = result.text
            .replace("```markdown", "")
            .replace("```", "")
            .trim()

        if (cleanText.contains("UNKNOWN_GAME", ignoreCase = true) || cleanText.length < 10) {
            return@withContext null
        }

        TokenBudgetManager.putDeduplicatedResponse(cacheKey, cleanText)
        cleanText
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/agent/CuratorRouter.kt
================================================================================
package com.example.agent

import com.example.api.AiFeature
import com.example.api.ApiKeyRotationManager
import com.example.api.ApiProfile
import com.example.api.AiProviderResolver
import com.example.api.AiRequest
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.json.JSONObject

enum class CuratorIntent {
    DIRECT_DATABASE,
    DATABASE_PLUS_AI,
    ANALYSIS,
    METADATA_ENRICHMENT,
    BATCH_ANALYSIS,
    RESEARCH,
    ASK_CLARIFICATION,
    NO_DATABASE_NEEDED
}

data class RouterDecision(
    val intent: CuratorIntent,
    val platformFilter: String? = null,
    val genreFilter: String? = null,
    val requiredFields: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val reasoning: String = ""
)

class CuratorRouter(
    private val profiles: List<ApiProfile>,
    private val rotationManager: ApiKeyRotationManager = ApiKeyRotationManager(),
    private val resolver: AiProviderResolver = AiProviderResolver()
) {
    suspend fun routeIntent(userQuery: String, context: CuratorContext): RouterDecision = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val systemPrompt = """
            你是 Sylphora 復古遊戲主機庫的 AI 館長大腦 (Router)。你的唯一任務是判斷使用者的意圖，並決定後續系統應該如何處理。
            
            可用的 Decision (intent) 包含：
            - DIRECT_DATABASE: 使用者詢問單純的數量或清單，例如「我有幾款 GBA 遊戲？」不需要 AI 重新分析。
            - DATABASE_PLUS_AI: 使用者詢問特定條件的遊戲清單，需要 AI 整理呈現，例如「我有哪些 GBA RPG？」。
            - ANALYSIS: 使用者需要推薦、比較或分析，例如「推薦我幾款最值得玩的 GBA RPG」。
            - METADATA_ENRICHMENT: 使用者需要的過濾條件 (例如 player count, multiplayer mode) 在現有 DB 通常不存在，需要先觸發資料擴充。
            - BATCH_ANALYSIS: 使用者要求分析整個圖書館或大量資料，例如「分析我所有遊戲」。
            - RESEARCH: 使用者詢問特定遊戲的歷史、評價、背景等外部知識，例如「為什麼 Golden Sun 是經典？」。
            - ASK_CLARIFICATION: 使用者問題太模糊，例如「推薦我一款遊戲」，需要反問偏好。
            - NO_DATABASE_NEEDED: 單純的閒聊或打招呼。

            請輸出唯一的 JSON 格式，不要包含 Markdown 語法 (```json)：
            {
              "intent": "DIRECT_DATABASE",
              "platformFilter": "GBA", 
              "genreFilter": "RPG", 
              "requiredFields": [], 
              "reasoning": "使用者詢問特定數量，只需 DB 處理"
            }
            如果不需要特定 filter，請填寫 null。
        """.trimIndent()

        val request = AiRequest(
            prompt = userQuery,
            systemPrompt = systemPrompt,
            temperature = 0.1f,
            maxTokens = 200
        )

        val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(AiFeature.CURATOR, profiles)
        val execResult = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, request)

        if (!execResult.success || execResult.text.isBlank()) {
            return@withContext RouterDecision(CuratorIntent.DATABASE_PLUS_AI) // Safe fallback
        }

        try {
            val cleanJson = execResult.text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").trim()
            val jsonObj = JSONObject(cleanJson)
            val intentStr = jsonObj.optString("intent", "DATABASE_PLUS_AI")
            val intent = try { CuratorIntent.valueOf(intentStr) } catch(e: Exception) { CuratorIntent.DATABASE_PLUS_AI }
            
            val platform = if (jsonObj.isNull("platformFilter")) null else jsonObj.optString("platformFilter")
            val genre = if (jsonObj.isNull("genreFilter")) null else jsonObj.optString("genreFilter")
            val reasoning = jsonObj.optString("reasoning", "")
            
            val reqFields = mutableListOf<String>()
            val fieldsArray = jsonObj.optJSONArray("requiredFields")
            if (fieldsArray != null) {
                for (i in 0 until fieldsArray.length()) {
                    reqFields.add(fieldsArray.getString(i))
                }
            }

            RouterDecision(intent, platform, genre, reqFields, reasoning)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            RouterDecision(CuratorIntent.DATABASE_PLUS_AI) // Fallback
        }
    }
}

package com.example.agent

import com.example.api.AiFeature
import com.example.api.ApiKeyRotationManager
import com.example.api.ApiProfile
import com.example.api.AiProviderResolver
import com.example.api.AiRequest
import com.squareup.moshi.Moshi
import com.squareup.moshi.kotlin.reflect.KotlinJsonAdapterFactory
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

data class MuseumFields(
    val background: String = "暫無背景故事",
    val historical_status: String = "經典復古之作",
    val music_highlights: String = "優質時代配樂",
    val dev_story: String = "經典匠心研發",
    val play_order: String = "推薦單人沈浸通關",
    val similar_games: String = "同平台同期代表作",
    val series_works: String = "經典系列作品"
)

data class DnaAxis(
    val label: String,
    val score: Int
)

data class GameDna(
    val axes: List<DnaAxis> = emptyList(),
    val genre_tag: String = "經典遊戲",
    val summary: String = "特徵鮮明的復古遊戲"
)

data class EvolutionLink(
    val relation: String, // original, sequel, prequel, remake, remaster, reboot, spinoff
    val title: String,
    val note: String? = null
)

data class EvolutionData(
    val known: Boolean = true,
    val links: List<EvolutionLink> = emptyList()
)

data class CheatItem(
    val title: String,
    val type: String, // "秘技指令", "金手指代碼", "通關攻略", "隱藏要素"
    val content: String,
    val code: String? = null
)

data class CheatGuideData(
    val gameTitle: String,
    val cheats: List<CheatItem> = emptyList(),
    val quickTips: List<String> = emptyList()
)

class GameKnowledgeService(
    private val profilesProvider: () -> List<ApiProfile>,
    private val rotationManager: ApiKeyRotationManager = ApiKeyRotationManager(),
    private val resolver: AiProviderResolver = AiProviderResolver()
) {
    private val moshi = Moshi.Builder().add(KotlinJsonAdapterFactory()).build()

    private suspend fun executeJsonPrompt(feature: AiFeature, prompt: String, systemInstruction: String): String? {
        val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(feature, profilesProvider())
        val taskKey = when (feature) {
            AiFeature.MUSEUM -> "MUSEUM"
            AiFeature.DNA -> "DNA"
            AiFeature.EVOLUTION -> "EVOLUTION"
            AiFeature.CHEATS -> "CHEATS"
            else -> "KNOWLEDGE"
        }
        val request = AiRequest(
            prompt = prompt,
            systemPrompt = "$systemInstruction 請只輸出純標準 JSON 格式字串，嚴禁輸出 Markdown 代碼塊或任何多餘文字說明。",
            temperature = 0.3f,
            maxTokens = TokenBudgetManager.resolveOutputBudget(taskKey)
        )
        val result = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, request)
        if (result.success && result.text.isNotBlank()) {
            return result.text
        }
        return null
    }

    suspend fun generateMuseum(gameTitle: String, platform: String): MuseumFields? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val prompt = """
            你是一位資深電玩歷史學家，請為「$platform」平台上的電子遊戲《$gameTitle》撰寫遊戲資料館導覽內容。
            請只回傳標準 JSON 格式，欄位包含：
            {
              "background": "背景故事，約80-120字",
              "historical_status": "歷史地位，約60-100字",
              "music_highlights": "配樂特色，約50-80字",
              "dev_story": "開發幕後故事，約60-100字",
              "play_order": "推薦遊玩順序或上手建議，約40-60字",
              "similar_games": "相似遊戲推薦，2-3款，用頓號分隔",
              "series_works": "系列作品名稱"
            }
        """.trimIndent()

        try {
            val raw = executeJsonPrompt(AiFeature.MUSEUM, prompt, "你是一位精通復古遊戲歷史的博覽館長。") ?: return@withContext null
            val cleanJson = cleanJsonString(raw)
            val adapter = moshi.adapter(MuseumFields::class.java)
            return@withContext adapter.fromJson(cleanJson)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            return@withContext null
        }
    }

    suspend fun generateDna(gameTitle: String, platform: String): GameDna? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val prompt = """
            請為「$platform」平台上的《$gameTitle》分析其 Game DNA 特徵維度（4-6個座標軸，每個分數0-100）。
            只輸出 JSON 格式：
            {
              "axes": [
                {"label": "劇情深度", "score": 85},
                {"label": "操作爽快", "score": 90},
                {"label": "探索自由", "score": 75},
                {"label": "難度硬核", "score": 60},
                {"label": "音樂氛圍", "score": 88}
              ],
              "genre_tag": "JRPG / 動作冒險",
              "summary": "一句話總結其核心魅力"
            }
        """.trimIndent()

        try {
            val raw = executeJsonPrompt(AiFeature.DNA, prompt, "你是一位遊戲設計架構分析師。") ?: return@withContext null
            val cleanJson = cleanJsonString(raw)
            val adapter = moshi.adapter(GameDna::class.java)
            return@withContext adapter.fromJson(cleanJson)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            return@withContext null
        }
    }

    suspend fun generateEvolution(gameTitle: String, platform: String): EvolutionData? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val prompt = """
            請分析「$platform」平台上的《$gameTitle》與其他前後作/衍生作的關係。
            關係類型限用: original (初代), sequel (續作), prequel (前傳), remake (重製版), remaster (復刻版), spinoff (外傳)。
            只輸出 JSON 格式：
            {
              "known": true,
              "links": [
                {"relation": "sequel", "title": "續作名稱", "note": "補充說明"},
                {"relation": "prequel", "title": "前作名稱", "note": "補充說明"}
              ]
            }
        """.trimIndent()

        try {
            val raw = executeJsonPrompt(AiFeature.EVOLUTION, prompt, "你是一位電玩歷史學家。") ?: return@withContext null
            val cleanJson = cleanJsonString(raw)
            val adapter = moshi.adapter(EvolutionData::class.java)
            return@withContext adapter.fromJson(cleanJson)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            return@withContext null
        }
    }

    suspend fun generateCheats(gameTitle: String, platform: String): CheatGuideData = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val prompt = """
            請為「$platform」平台上的經典復古遊戲《$gameTitle》提供詳細的【秘技指令、金手指代碼(GameShark/RAW)、通關卡關提示、隱藏要素與角色】。
            只輸出標準 JSON 格式（不要包含任何額外說明）：
            {
              "gameTitle": "$gameTitle",
              "cheats": [
                {
                  "title": "無敵/30命/全武器秘技",
                  "type": "秘技指令",
                  "content": "在標題畫面輸入特定按鍵順序...",
                  "code": "上 上 下 下 左 右 左 右 B A"
                },
                {
                  "title": "無限生命/金錢金手指",
                  "type": "金手指代碼",
                  "content": "鎖定生命值不減",
                  "code": "01FF16D0"
                },
                {
                  "title": "卡關要點與Boss攻略",
                  "type": "通關攻略",
                  "content": "第3關Boss弱點為雷屬性，攻擊前搖時快速閃避..."
                },
                {
                  "title": "真結局觸發與隱藏角色",
                  "type": "隱藏要素",
                  "content": "收集全部關鍵道具可在通關時解鎖隱藏結局..."
                }
              ],
              "quickTips": [
                "新手入門第一條實用建議",
                "重要隱藏道具獲取位置",
                "最佳存檔時機與裝備搭配推薦"
              ]
            }
        """.trimIndent()

        try {
            val raw = executeJsonPrompt(AiFeature.CHEATS, prompt, "你是一位電玩秘技與金手指專家。")
            if (raw != null) {
                val cleanJson = cleanJsonString(raw)
                val adapter = moshi.adapter(CheatGuideData::class.java)
                val result = adapter.fromJson(cleanJson)
                if (result != null && result.cheats.isNotEmpty()) {
                    return@withContext result
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
        }

        // Curated fallback
        getCuratedCheats(gameTitle, platform)
    }

    private suspend fun executeTextPrompt(feature: AiFeature, prompt: String, systemInstruction: String): String? {
        val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(feature, profilesProvider())
        if (targetProfiles.isEmpty()) return null

        val taskKey = when (feature) {
            AiFeature.RESEARCH -> "RESEARCH"
            AiFeature.DESCRIPTION -> "DESCRIPTION"
            else -> "KNOWLEDGE"
        }
        val request = com.example.api.AiRequest(
            prompt = prompt,
            systemPrompt = systemInstruction,
            temperature = 0.5f,
            maxTokens = TokenBudgetManager.resolveOutputBudget(taskKey)
        )
        val result = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, request)
        return result?.text
    }

    suspend fun generateResearch(gameTitle: String, platform: String, topicName: String, topicPrompt: String): String? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val prompt = """
            請研究電子遊戲《$gameTitle》（平台：$platform）的「$topicName」。
            研究方向：$topicPrompt
            請提供條理清晰、可保存的繁體中文研究成果，區分已知史實與未證實傳聞，若沒有可靠資訊，請明確說明已知未知，不要捏造。
            請以適合閱讀的 Markdown 文章格式輸出。
        """.trimIndent()
        executeTextPrompt(AiFeature.RESEARCH, prompt, "你是嚴謹的遊戲研究助理。若沒有可靠資訊，請明確說明未知，不要編造。")
            ?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
    }

    private fun getCuratedCheats(title: String, platform: String): CheatGuideData {
        val t = title.lowercase()
        val cheats = when {
            t.contains("chrono trigger") || t.contains("超時空") -> listOf(
                CheatItem("解鎖全部13種多結局", "隱藏要素", "在二周目新遊戲+ (New Game+) 中，在不同的時間點直接進入時間盡頭右側水桶挑戰拉沃斯，即可觸發不同的特殊結局，包括開發者工作室結局！"),
                CheatItem("無限力量膠囊密技", "秘技指令", "在黑之鳥號擊敗達爾頓後，利用艾拉 (Ayla) 的魅惑技能對特定恐龍敵人使用，可持續竊取力量膠囊與敏捷膠囊。"),
                CheatItem("全隊滿級無限金錢 (SNES)", "金手指代碼", "鎖定全隊伍經驗與金幣為最高上限", "7E0040 FF / 7E0041 FF / 7E0042 FF"),
                CheatItem("魔王決戰最優解", "通關攻略", "在古代王國殘骸中面對魔王時，建議選擇「否」不進行戰鬥，魔王將正式加入隊伍成為強力同伴，並解鎖專屬隱藏劇情。")
            )
            t.contains("castlevania") || t.contains("惡魔城") || t.contains("月下") -> listOf(
                CheatItem("里希特模式解鎖", "秘技指令", "遊戲通關後在創建新存檔時輸入名字「RICHTER」，即可直接使用吸血鬼獵人里希特開始冒險！", "RICHTER"),
                CheatItem("極限幸運阿魯卡多 (Lapis Lazuli)", "秘技指令", "新建存檔輸入名字「X-X!V''Q」，起始幸運值變為 99，容易爆出各類罕見神裝！", "X-X!V''Q"),
                CheatItem("真空刃 (Crissaegrim) 獲取指南", "通關攻略", "進入逆城圖書館後，裝備雙青金石戒指，連續刷飛頭幽靈怪物 (Schmoo)，即可掉落全遊戲最強連擊神兵真空刃！"),
                CheatItem("無限生命與MP金手指 (PSX)", "金手指代碼", "鎖定生命與魔力值", "80097BA0 03E7 / 80097BA4 03E7")
            )
            t.contains("contra") || t.contains("魂斗羅") -> listOf(
                CheatItem("傳奇 Konami 30命秘技", "秘技指令", "在標題畫面標題滾動結束前，按順序輸入 Konami 經典密碼，成功後會聽到提示音！", "上 上 下 下 左 右 左 右 B A 選擇鍵 開始鍵"),
                CheatItem("選關模式密碼", "秘技指令", "在標題畫面按住 1P 搖桿左上 + 選擇鍵 + A 鍵，然後按開始鍵進入關卡選擇選單。"),
                CheatItem("S彈散彈全屏秒殺 (NES)", "金手指代碼", "鎖定武器為威力最強的 S 散彈槍", "00AA-01-02")
            )
            else -> listOf(
                CheatItem("目前無可靠資料", "系統提示", "Sylphora AI 與本地庫目前無法為《$title》找到可靠的秘技或攻略。我們拒絕生成虛假的金手指代碼。")
            )
        }

        return CheatGuideData(
            gameTitle = title,
            cheats = cheats,
            quickTips = if (cheats.any { it.title == "目前無可靠資料" }) emptyList() else listOf(
                "善用模擬器的即時存檔 (Save State) 功能隨時備份 Boss 戰前進度",
                "定期檢查裝備庫存與屬性抗性，針對不同弱點選擇合適的攻擊方式",
                "多探索地圖邊緣與隱藏牆壁，往往藏有稀有道具與永久屬性加成"
            )
        )
    }

    private fun cleanJsonString(raw: String): String {
        var str = raw.trim()
        if (str.startsWith("```json")) {
            str = str.removePrefix("```json").trim()
        } else if (str.startsWith("```")) {
            str = str.removePrefix("```").trim()
        }
        if (str.endsWith("```")) {
            str = str.removeSuffix("```").trim()
        }
        return str
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/agent/TokenBudgetManager.kt
================================================================================
package com.example.agent

import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong

data class TokenDiagnostics(
    val totalLibraryGames: Int = 0,
    val totalRetrievedCandidates: Long = 0,
    val totalSentToAiCandidates: Long = 0,
    val totalActualInputTokens: Long = 0,
    val totalActualOutputTokens: Long = 0,
    val totalEstimatedInputTokens: Long = 0,
    val totalEstimatedOutputTokens: Long = 0,
    val cacheHits: Long = 0,
    val cacheSavedTokensEstimated: Long = 0
)

/**
 * Token and Context Budget Manager (Phase 1 & Phase 7)
 * Replaces naive hardcoded token limits with dynamic budget tracking,
 * multi-tier retrieval, compact summary caching, and request deduplication.
 */
object TokenBudgetManager {
    private val totalRetrieved = AtomicLong(0)
    private val totalSentToAi = AtomicLong(0)
    private val totalActualInputTokens = AtomicLong(0)
    private val totalActualOutputTokens = AtomicLong(0)
    private val totalEstimatedInputTokens = AtomicLong(0)
    private val totalEstimatedOutputTokens = AtomicLong(0)
    private val totalCacheHits = AtomicLong(0)
    private val totalSavedTokens = AtomicLong(0)

    // Compact Summary Cache: gameId -> (metadataHash, compactSummary)
    private val compactSummaryCache = ConcurrentHashMap<Int, Pair<Int, String>>()

    // Request Deduplication Cache: requestHash -> (timestampMs, responseText)
    private val deduplicationCache = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Pair<Long, String>>()

    // Safe heuristic for mixed CJK + English: roughly 1.5 ~ 2 chars per token.
    fun estimateTokens(text: String): Int {
        if (text.isBlank()) return 0
        return (text.length / 1.5).toInt() + 2
    }

    fun recordRetrieval(retrievedCount: Int, sentCount: Int) {
        totalRetrieved.addAndGet(retrievedCount.toLong())
        totalSentToAi.addAndGet(sentCount.toLong())
    }

    fun recordActualTokens(inputTokens: Int, outputTokens: Int) {
        totalActualInputTokens.addAndGet(inputTokens.toLong())
        totalActualOutputTokens.addAndGet(outputTokens.toLong())
    }

    fun recordEstimatedTokens(inputTokens: Int, outputTokens: Int) {
        totalEstimatedInputTokens.addAndGet(inputTokens.toLong())
        totalEstimatedOutputTokens.addAndGet(outputTokens.toLong())
    }

    fun recordTokens(inputTokens: Int, outputTokens: Int) {
        recordActualTokens(inputTokens, outputTokens)
    }

    fun recordCacheHit(savedTokensEstimate: Int) {
        totalCacheHits.incrementAndGet()
        totalSavedTokens.addAndGet(savedTokensEstimate.toLong())
    }

    fun getDiagnostics(totalLibraryCount: Int = 0): TokenDiagnostics {
        return TokenDiagnostics(
            totalLibraryGames = totalLibraryCount,
            totalRetrievedCandidates = totalRetrieved.get(),
            totalSentToAiCandidates = totalSentToAi.get(),
            totalActualInputTokens = totalActualInputTokens.get(),
            totalActualOutputTokens = totalActualOutputTokens.get(),
            totalEstimatedInputTokens = totalEstimatedInputTokens.get(),
            totalEstimatedOutputTokens = totalEstimatedOutputTokens.get(),
            cacheHits = totalCacheHits.get(),
            cacheSavedTokensEstimated = totalSavedTokens.get()
        )
    }

    fun getOrCreateCompactSummary(
        gameId: Int,
        metadataHash: Int,
        generator: () -> String
    ): String {
        val cached = compactSummaryCache[gameId]
        if (cached != null && cached.first == metadataHash) {
            recordCacheHit(30)
            return cached.second
        }
        val generated = generator()
        compactSummaryCache[gameId] = Pair(metadataHash, generated)
        return generated
    }

    fun getDeduplicatedResponse(key: String, maxAgeMs: Long = 60_000L): String? {
        val entry = deduplicationCache[key] ?: return null
        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - entry.first < maxAgeMs) {
            recordCacheHit(estimateTokens(entry.second))
            return entry.second
        }
        deduplicationCache.remove(key)
        return null
    }

    fun putDeduplicatedResponse(key: String, response: String) {
        if (response.isNotBlank()) {
            deduplicationCache[key] = Pair(System.currentTimeMillis(), response)
        }
    }

    fun clearAllCaches() {
        compactSummaryCache.clear()
        deduplicationCache.clear()
    }

    fun resetDiagnostics() {
        totalRetrieved.set(0)
        totalSentToAi.set(0)
        totalActualInputTokens.set(0)
        totalActualOutputTokens.set(0)
        totalEstimatedInputTokens.set(0)
        totalEstimatedOutputTokens.set(0)
        totalCacheHits.set(0)
        totalSavedTokens.set(0)
    }

    fun getContextBudgetForModel(modelName: String): Int {
        val name = modelName.lowercase()
        return when {
            name.contains("gpt-4") || name.contains("128k") -> 100_000
            name.contains("gemini") -> 100_000
            name.contains("claude-3") -> 100_000
            name.contains("llama-3") || name.contains("llama3") || name.contains("8k") -> 6_000
            else -> 4000
        }
    }

    /**
     * Resolve the output token budget dynamically based on task type.
     */
    fun resolveOutputBudget(taskType: String): Int {
        return when (taskType.uppercase()) {
            "CHAT" -> 2000
            "RESEARCH" -> 2500
            "RESEARCH_CANDIDATE" -> 1500
            "DESCRIPTION" -> 600
            "MUSEUM" -> 3000
            "DNA" -> 2000
            "EVOLUTION" -> 2500
            "CHEATS" -> 2500
            "ACTION_INTENT" -> 50
            "SEARCH_DECISION" -> 100
            "KNOWLEDGE" -> 3000
            else -> 1000
        }
    }

    data class HistoryCompressionResult(
        val compressedHistory: List<Pair<String, String>>,
        val prunedSummary: String? = null
    )

    /**
     * Compress and prune conversation history to fit safely within context limits.
     * When older turns are pruned, extracts high-level intent/topics as summary.
     */
    fun compressHistoryWithSummary(
        systemPrompt: String,
        latestMessage: String,
        history: List<Pair<String, String>>,
        modelName: String
    ): HistoryCompressionResult {
        val totalBudget = getContextBudgetForModel(modelName)
        val inputBudget = (totalBudget - 3000).coerceAtLeast(1000) 
        
        val sysTokens = estimateTokens(systemPrompt)
        val latestMsgTokens = estimateTokens(latestMessage)
        
        var availableForHistory = inputBudget - sysTokens - latestMsgTokens
        
        if (availableForHistory <= 0) return HistoryCompressionResult(emptyList(), null)

        val kept = mutableListOf<Pair<String, String>>()
        val pruned = mutableListOf<Pair<String, String>>()

        // Process from most recent (end of list) to oldest
        for (turn in history.reversed()) {
            val tokens = estimateTokens(turn.first + turn.second)
            if (availableForHistory >= tokens) {
                kept.add(turn)
                availableForHistory -= tokens
            } else {
                pruned.add(turn)
            }
        }

        val prunedSummary = if (pruned.isNotEmpty()) {
            val userTopics = pruned.filter { it.first == "user" }.map { it.second.take(40) }
            if (userTopics.isNotEmpty()) {
                "【前置對話記憶要點】：玩家曾探討過：" + userTopics.takeLast(4).joinToString("；")
            } else null
        } else null

        val resultHistory = kept.reversed()

        return HistoryCompressionResult(
            compressedHistory = resultHistory,
            prunedSummary = prunedSummary
        )
    }

    /**
     * Compress and prune conversation history to fit safely within context limits.
     * Keeps recent messages.
     */
    fun compressHistory(
        systemPrompt: String,
        latestMessage: String,
        history: List<Pair<String, String>>,
        modelName: String
    ): List<Pair<String, String>> {
        return compressHistoryWithSummary(systemPrompt, latestMessage, history, modelName).compressedHistory
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/api/AiTransport.kt

package com.example.api

import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL

data class AiRequest(
    val prompt: String,
    val systemPrompt: String? = null,
    val conversationHistory: List<Pair<String, String>> = emptyList(), // role, content
    val temperature: Float = 0.7f,
    val maxTokens: Int = 1500
)

data class AiResponse(
    val text: String,
    val providerUsed: String,
    val modelUsed: String,
    val keyIndexUsed: Int = 0,
    val isSuccess: Boolean = true,
    val error: String? = null
)

data class AiTransportResult(
    val text: String,
    val inputTokens: Int? = null,
    val outputTokens: Int? = null
)

interface AiTransport {
    suspend fun execute(
        profile: ApiProfile,
        apiKey: String,
        request: AiRequest
    ): AiTransportResult
}

object GenericOpenAiCompatibleTransport : AiTransport {
    override suspend fun execute(
        profile: ApiProfile,
        apiKey: String,
        request: AiRequest
    ): AiTransportResult = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val endpoint = profile.baseUrl?.trimEnd('/')?.ifBlank { null } ?: when (profile.provider.uppercase()) {
            "GROQ" -> "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1"
            "GITHUB" -> "https://models.inference.ai.azure.com"
            "OPENROUTER" -> "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"
            "LOCAL", "OLLAMA" -> "http://10.0.2.2:11434/v1" // local emulator fallback
            else -> "https://api.openai.com/v1"
        }
        val fullUrl = if (endpoint.endsWith("/chat/completions")) endpoint else "$endpoint/chat/completions"

        if (profile.model.isBlank()) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("${profile.displayName.ifBlank { profile.provider }} 未指定 AI Model，請在設定中取得可用模型或手動輸入 Model")
        }

        val rootJson = JSONObject()
        rootJson.put("model", profile.model)
        rootJson.put("temperature", request.temperature)
        rootJson.put("max_tokens", request.maxTokens)

        val messages = JSONArray()
        val sysPrompt = request.systemPrompt ?: profile.systemPrompt
        if (!sysPrompt.isNullOrBlank()) {
            val sysObj = JSONObject()
            sysObj.put("role", "system")
            sysObj.put("content", sysPrompt)
            messages.put(sysObj)
        }

        for ((role, content) in request.conversationHistory) {
            val msgObj = JSONObject()
            msgObj.put("role", role)
            msgObj.put("content", content)
            messages.put(msgObj)
        }

        val userObj = JSONObject()
        userObj.put("role", "user")
        userObj.put("content", request.prompt)
        messages.put(userObj)

        rootJson.put("messages", messages)

        val connection = URL(fullUrl).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
        connection.requestMethod = "POST"
        connection.connectTimeout = 15000
        connection.readTimeout = 30000
        connection.doOutput = true
        connection.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        if (apiKey.isNotBlank()) {
            connection.setRequestProperty("Authorization", "Bearer $apiKey")
        }

        OutputStreamWriter(connection.outputStream, Charsets.UTF_8).use { it.write(rootJson.toString()) }

        val responseCode = connection.responseCode
        if (responseCode in 200..299) {
            val responseBody = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(connection.inputStream, Charsets.UTF_8)).use { it.readText() }
            val respJson = JSONObject(responseBody)
            val choices = respJson.optJSONArray("choices")
            if (choices != null && choices.length() > 0) {
                val message = choices.getJSONObject(0).optJSONObject("message")
                val usage = respJson.optJSONObject("usage")
                return@withContext AiTransportResult(
                    text = message?.optString("content", "").orEmpty(),
                    inputTokens = usage?.optInt("prompt_tokens"),
                    outputTokens = usage?.optInt("completion_tokens")
                )
            }
            return@withContext AiTransportResult(text = responseBody)
        } else {
            val errBody = try {
                BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(connection.errorStream, Charsets.UTF_8)).use { it.readText() }
            } catch (_: Exception) { "" }
            throw RuntimeException("HTTP $responseCode: $errBody")
        }
    }
}

object GeminiTransport : AiTransport {
    override suspend fun execute(
        profile: ApiProfile,
        apiKey: String,
        request: AiRequest
    ): AiTransportResult = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        if (profile.model.isBlank()) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("${profile.displayName.ifBlank { "Gemini" }} 未指定 AI Model，請先在設定中取得可用模型或手動輸入 Model")
        }
        val model = profile.model
        val targetUrl = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/$model:generateContent?key=$apiKey"

        val root = JSONObject()
        val contents = JSONArray()

        if (!request.systemPrompt.isNullOrBlank() || !profile.systemPrompt.isNullOrBlank()) {
            val sysInst = JSONObject()
            val sysParts = JSONArray().put(JSONObject().put("text", request.systemPrompt ?: profile.systemPrompt))
            sysInst.put("parts", sysParts)
            root.put("systemInstruction", sysInst)
        }

        for ((role, content) in request.conversationHistory) {
            val turnRole = if (role == "assistant") "model" else "user"
            val turn = JSONObject()
            turn.put("role", turnRole)
            turn.put("parts", JSONArray().put(JSONObject().put("text", content)))
            contents.put(turn)
        }

        val userTurn = JSONObject()
        userTurn.put("role", "user")
        userTurn.put("parts", JSONArray().put(JSONObject().put("text", request.prompt)))
        contents.put(userTurn)

        root.put("contents", contents)

        val genConfig = JSONObject()
        genConfig.put("temperature", request.temperature)
        genConfig.put("maxOutputTokens", request.maxTokens)
        root.put("generationConfig", genConfig)

        val conn = URL(targetUrl).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
        conn.requestMethod = "POST"
        conn.connectTimeout = 15000
        conn.readTimeout = 30000
        conn.doOutput = true
        conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")

        OutputStreamWriter(conn.outputStream, Charsets.UTF_8).use { it.write(root.toString()) }

        val code = conn.responseCode
        if (code in 200..299) {
            val resp = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(conn.inputStream, Charsets.UTF_8)).use { it.readText() }
            val respObj = JSONObject(resp)
            val candidates = respObj.optJSONArray("candidates")
            val usageMetadata = respObj.optJSONObject("usageMetadata")
            if (candidates != null && candidates.length() > 0) {
                val contentObj = candidates.getJSONObject(0).optJSONObject("content")
                val parts = contentObj?.optJSONArray("parts")
                if (parts != null && parts.length() > 0) {
                    return@withContext AiTransportResult(
                        text = parts.getJSONObject(0).optString("text", ""),
                        inputTokens = usageMetadata?.optInt("promptTokenCount"),
                        outputTokens = usageMetadata?.optInt("candidatesTokenCount")
                    )
                }
            }
            return@withContext AiTransportResult(text = resp)
        } else {
            val err = try {
                BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(conn.errorStream, Charsets.UTF_8)).use { it.readText() }
            } catch (_: Exception) { "" }
            throw RuntimeException("Gemini HTTP $code: $err")
        }
    }
}

object CloudflareTransport : AiTransport {
    override suspend fun execute(
        profile: ApiProfile,
        apiKey: String,
        request: AiRequest
    ): AiTransportResult = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val accountId = profile.accountId?.ifBlank { null } ?: "default"
        if (profile.model.isBlank()) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("${profile.displayName.ifBlank { "Cloudflare" }} 未指定 AI Model，請在設定中取得模型或手動輸入")
        }
        val model = profile.model
        val targetUrl = "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/$accountId/ai/run/$model"

        val body = JSONObject()
        val messages = JSONArray()
        if (!request.systemPrompt.isNullOrBlank()) {
            messages.put(JSONObject().put("role", "system").put("content", request.systemPrompt))
        }
        for ((role, content) in request.conversationHistory) {
            messages.put(JSONObject().put("role", role).put("content", content))
        }
        messages.put(JSONObject().put("role", "user").put("content", request.prompt))
        body.put("messages", messages)
        body.put("max_tokens", request.maxTokens)

        val conn = URL(targetUrl).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
        conn.requestMethod = "POST"
        conn.connectTimeout = 15000
        conn.readTimeout = 30000
        conn.doOutput = true
        conn.setRequestProperty("Authorization", "Bearer $apiKey")
        conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json")

        OutputStreamWriter(conn.outputStream, Charsets.UTF_8).use { it.write(body.toString()) }

        val code = conn.responseCode
        if (code in 200..299) {
            val resp = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(conn.inputStream, Charsets.UTF_8)).use { it.readText() }
            val json = JSONObject(resp)
            val result = json.optJSONObject("result")
            // Cloudflare doesn't explicitly return token usage yet in this format, but we'll return AiTransportResult
            return@withContext AiTransportResult(text = result?.optString("response") ?: resp)
        } else {
            val err = try {
                BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(conn.errorStream, Charsets.UTF_8)).use { it.readText() }
            } catch (_: Exception) { "" }
            throw RuntimeException("Cloudflare HTTP $code: $err")
        }
    }
}
