package com.example.database

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import androidx.room.migration.Migration
import androidx.sqlite.db.SupportSQLiteDatabase

// v9: added indices for games (romPath, platform/title) and asset_match_records (gameId, romIdentity)
@Database(entities = [GameEntity::class, ResearchEntity::class, TaskCheckpointEntity::class, AssetMatchEntity::class], version = 9, exportSchema = false)
abstract class AppDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun gameDao(): GameDao
    abstract fun researchDao(): ResearchDao
    abstract fun taskCheckpointDao(): TaskCheckpointDao
    abstract fun assetMatchDao(): AssetMatchDao

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: AppDatabase? = null

        val MIGRATION_1_2 = object : Migration(1, 2) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN customCollections TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN screenshotPath TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN logoPath TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
            }
        }

        val MIGRATION_2_3 = object : Migration(2, 3) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN playTimeMinutes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN playStatus TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UNPLAYED'")
            }
        }

        val MIGRATION_3_4 = object : Migration(3, 4) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN descriptionCsvSynced INTEGER DEFAULT NULL")
            }
        }

        val MIGRATION_4_5 = object : Migration(4, 5) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN iconPath TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN sourceId TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
            }
        }
        
        val MIGRATION_5_6 = object : Migration(5, 6) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `research_tasks` (`id` TEXT NOT NULL, `gameTitle` TEXT NOT NULL, `platform` TEXT NOT NULL, `topicId` TEXT NOT NULL, `topicTitle` TEXT NOT NULL, `status` TEXT NOT NULL, `progress` REAL NOT NULL, `result` TEXT, `error` TEXT, `updatedAt` INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(`id`))")
                db.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `task_checkpoints` (`id` TEXT NOT NULL, `name` TEXT NOT NULL, `stage` TEXT NOT NULL, `status` TEXT NOT NULL, `completedCount` INTEGER NOT NULL, `totalCount` INTEGER NOT NULL, `currentItem` TEXT NOT NULL, `dryRun` INTEGER NOT NULL, `updatedAt` INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(`id`))")
            }
        }

        val MIGRATION_6_7 = object : Migration(6, 7) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN file_name TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN file_path TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE games ADD COLUMN ai_detected_title TEXT DEFAULT NULL")
            }
        }

        val MIGRATION_7_8 = object : Migration(7, 8) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `asset_match_records` (`gameId` INTEGER NOT NULL, `romIdentity` TEXT NOT NULL, `assetType` TEXT NOT NULL, `source` TEXT NOT NULL, `sourceKey` TEXT NOT NULL, `matchedAssetUri` TEXT, `status` TEXT NOT NULL, `lastCheckedAt` INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(`romIdentity`, `assetType`, `source`))")
            }
        }

        val MIGRATION_8_9 = object : Migration(8, 9) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS `index_games_romPath` ON `games` (`romPath`)")
                db.execSQL("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS `index_games_platform_title` ON `games` (`platform`, `title`)")
                db.execSQL("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS `index_asset_match_records_gameId` ON `asset_match_records` (`gameId`)")
                db.execSQL("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS `index_asset_match_records_romIdentity` ON `asset_match_records` (`romIdentity`)")
            }
        }

        fun getDatabase(context: Context): AppDatabase {
            return INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                val instance = Room.databaseBuilder(
                    context.applicationContext,
                    AppDatabase::class.java,
                    "pegasus_database"
                )
                .addMigrations(MIGRATION_1_2, MIGRATION_2_3, MIGRATION_3_4, MIGRATION_4_5, MIGRATION_5_6, MIGRATION_6_7, MIGRATION_7_8, MIGRATION_8_9)
                .build()
                INSTANCE = instance
                instance
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/database/GameRepository.kt
================================================================================
package com.example.database

import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

class GameRepository(
    private val gameDao: GameDao,
    private val assetMatchDao: AssetMatchDao? = null
) {
    val allGames: Flow<List<GameEntity>> = gameDao.getAllGames()
    val descriptionSyncStats: Flow<DescriptionSyncStats> = gameDao.getDescriptionSyncStats()
    
    suspend fun getAllGamesDirect(): List<GameEntity> {
        return gameDao.getAllGamesDirect()
    }

    fun getAssetMatchDao(): AssetMatchDao? = assetMatchDao

    suspend fun getAssetMatchRecord(romIdentity: String, assetType: String, source: String): AssetMatchEntity? {
        return assetMatchDao?.getMatchRecord(romIdentity, assetType, source)
    }

    suspend fun getAssetMatchRecordsForRom(romIdentity: String): List<AssetMatchEntity> {
        return assetMatchDao?.getRecordsForRom(romIdentity).orEmpty()
    }

    suspend fun saveAssetMatchRecord(record: AssetMatchEntity) {
        assetMatchDao?.insertRecord(record)
    }

    suspend fun saveAssetMatchRecords(records: List<AssetMatchEntity>) {
        assetMatchDao?.insertRecords(records)
    }

    suspend fun clearAssetMatchRecords() {
        assetMatchDao?.deleteAllRecords()
    }

    suspend fun insertGame(game: GameEntity) {
        gameDao.insertGame(game)
    }

    suspend fun updateGame(game: GameEntity) {
        gameDao.insertGame(game)
    }
    
    suspend fun insertGames(games: List<GameEntity>) {
        gameDao.insertGames(games)
    }

    suspend fun updateGames(games: List<GameEntity>) {
        gameDao.updateGames(games)
    }

    suspend fun upsertScannedGamesBatch(context: android.content.Context, scannedList: List<GameEntity>) {
        if (scannedList.isEmpty()) return
        val allExisting = gameDao.getAllGamesDirect()
        val romPathMap = allExisting.associateBy { it.romPath }.toMutableMap()
        val identityMap = mutableMapOf<String, GameEntity>()
        for (existing in allExisting) {
            val identity = com.example.p3.asset.RomIdentityUtils.getStableRomIdentity(context, existing)
            if (identity.startsWith("ROMHASH_")) {
                identityMap[identity] = existing
            }
        }

        val gamesToInsert = mutableListOf<GameEntity>()
        val gamesToUpdate = mutableListOf<GameEntity>()

        for (scanned in scannedList) {
            // Priority 1: Exact romPath match
            var matched = romPathMap[scanned.romPath]

            // Priority 2: Stable content-based ROM identity match (e.g. file moved or renamed)
            if (matched == null) {
                val scannedIdentity = com.example.p3.asset.RomIdentityUtils.getStableRomIdentity(context, scanned)
                if (scannedIdentity.startsWith("ROMHASH_")) {
                    matched = identityMap[scannedIdentity]
                }
            }

            if (matched != null) {
                val merged = mergeScannedGame(matched, scanned)
                gamesToUpdate.add(merged)
                romPathMap[merged.romPath] = merged
            } else {
                gamesToInsert.add(scanned)
                romPathMap[scanned.romPath] = scanned
                val scannedIdentity = com.example.p3.asset.RomIdentityUtils.getStableRomIdentity(context, scanned)
                if (scannedIdentity.startsWith("ROMHASH_")) {
                    identityMap[scannedIdentity] = scanned
                }
            }
        }

        if (gamesToInsert.isNotEmpty()) {
            gameDao.insertGames(gamesToInsert)
        }
        if (gamesToUpdate.isNotEmpty()) {
            gameDao.insertGames(gamesToUpdate)
        }
    }

    suspend fun upsertScannedGamesBatch(scannedList: List<GameEntity>) {
        gameDao.upsertScannedGamesBatch(scannedList)
    }

    suspend fun getGameById(id: Int): GameEntity? = gameDao.getGameById(id)

    suspend fun getGamesByPlatformDirect(platform: String): List<GameEntity> = gameDao.getGamesByPlatformDirect(platform)

    suspend fun getGamesBySourceId(sourceId: String): List<GameEntity> = gameDao.getGamesBySourceId(sourceId)

    suspend fun updateAssets(id: Int, coverPath: String?, backgroundPath: String?, logoPath: String?, screenshotPath: String?, iconPath: String?) {
        gameDao.updateAssets(id, coverPath, backgroundPath, logoPath, screenshotPath, iconPath)
    }

    suspend fun updateDescription(id: Int, description: String, synced: Boolean?) {
        gameDao.updateDescription(id, description, synced)
    }

    suspend fun updateKnowledge(id: Int, museumJson: String?, dnaJson: String?, evolutionJson: String?, cheatsJson: String?) {
        gameDao.updateKnowledge(id, museumJson, dnaJson, evolutionJson, cheatsJson)
    }

    /**
     * Merge a freshly scanned ROM record into the existing library without
     * destroying user-generated / user-owned data.
     */
    suspend fun upsertScannedGame(context: android.content.Context, scanned: GameEntity) {
        upsertScannedGamesBatch(context, listOf(scanned))
    }

    suspend fun upsertScannedGame(scanned: GameEntity) {
        val existing = gameDao.getGameByRomPath(scanned.romPath)
        if (existing == null) {
            gameDao.insertGame(scanned)
            return
        }
        gameDao.insertGame(mergeScannedGame(existing, scanned))
    }

    companion object {
        private fun safeMergeString(existing: String?, scanned: String?): String? {
            if (!existing.isNullOrBlank()) return existing
            if (!scanned.isNullOrBlank()) return scanned
            return existing
        }

        /**
         * Merge scanner/metadata fields while preserving user-owned and generated data.
         * This is intentionally pure so the rule can be regression-tested without Room.
         */
        fun mergeScannedGame(existing: GameEntity, scanned: GameEntity): GameEntity = existing.copy(
            romPath = scanned.romPath,
            fileName = scanned.fileName ?: existing.fileName,
            filePath = scanned.filePath ?: existing.filePath,
            title = safeMergeString(existing.title, scanned.title) ?: existing.title,
            platform = safeMergeString(existing.platform, scanned.platform) ?: existing.platform,
            sourceId = safeMergeString(existing.sourceId, scanned.sourceId),
            iconPath = safeMergeString(existing.iconPath, scanned.iconPath),
            coverPath = safeMergeString(existing.coverPath, scanned.coverPath),
            backgroundPath = safeMergeString(existing.backgroundPath, scanned.backgroundPath),
            logoPath = safeMergeString(existing.logoPath, scanned.logoPath),
            screenshotPath = safeMergeString(existing.screenshotPath, scanned.screenshotPath),
            description = safeMergeString(existing.description, scanned.description),
            developer = safeMergeString(existing.developer, scanned.developer),
            publisher = safeMergeString(existing.publisher, scanned.publisher),
            genre = safeMergeString(existing.genre, scanned.genre),
            releaseDate = safeMergeString(existing.releaseDate, scanned.releaseDate),
            composer = safeMergeString(existing.composer, scanned.composer),
            rating = if (existing.rating != 0f) existing.rating else scanned.rating,
            isFavorite = existing.isFavorite,
            playCount = existing.playCount,
            playTimeMinutes = existing.playTimeMinutes,
            playStatus = existing.playStatus,
            customCollections = existing.customCollections,
            lastPlayed = existing.lastPlayed,
            addedAt = existing.addedAt,
            museumJson = existing.museumJson,
            dnaJson = existing.dnaJson,
            evolutionJson = existing.evolutionJson,
            cheatsJson = existing.cheatsJson,
            retroAchievementsJson = existing.retroAchievementsJson
        )
    }

    suspend fun deleteGameById(id: Int) {
        gameDao.deleteGameById(id)
    }

    suspend fun deleteGame(game: GameEntity) {
        gameDao.deleteGameById(game.id)
    }

    suspend fun clearAll() {
        gameDao.deleteAllGames()
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/p3/asset/AssetDiscoveryService.kt
================================================================================
package com.example.p3.asset

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import androidx.documentfile.provider.DocumentFile
import com.example.database.GameEntity
import com.example.scanner.PlatformRegistry
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

data class LocalAssetSet(
    val icon: String? = null,
    val logo: String? = null,
    val hero: String? = null,
    val slide: String? = null,
    val boxFront: String? = null,
    val description: String? = null,
    val isDescriptionFromCsv: Boolean = false
)

class AssetDiscoveryService(private val context: Context) {

    /**
     * Traverses an Asset Root directory structure supporting multiple common layouts:
     * 1. Standard Per-Game Folder:
     *    Asset Root / Platform / GameTitle / [boxfront.png, icon.png, title.png, hero_1.png, slide_1.png, desc.txt]
     * 2. Libretro / RetroArch Thumbnails:
     *    Asset Root / Platform / Named_Boxarts / GameTitle.png
     *    Asset Root / Platform / Named_Snaps / GameTitle.png
     *    Asset Root / Platform / Named_Titles / GameTitle.png
     * 3. Pegasus / EmulationStation Media:
     *    Asset Root / Platform / media / [box2d, screenshot, wheel] / GameTitle.png
     *    Asset Root / Platform / [covers, backgrounds, screenshots, titles, icons] / GameTitle.png
     * 4. Flat Game File Naming:
     *    Asset Root / Platform / GameTitle.png, GameTitle_screenshot.png, GameTitle_icon.png, GameTitle.txt
     * 5. Metadata files: gamelist.xml, metadata.pegasus.txt, metadata.txt, metadata.csv
     */
    suspend fun matchAssetsForLibrary(
        assetRootUri: Uri,
        games: List<GameEntity>,
        platformFilter: String? = null,
        missingOnly: Boolean = true,
        fuzzyThreshold: Double = 0.70,
        assetMatchDao: com.example.database.AssetMatchDao? = null,
        forceRematch: Boolean = false,
        onProgress: (current: Int, total: Int, message: String) -> Unit = { _, _, _ -> }
    ): Map<Int, LocalAssetSet> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val rootDoc = DocumentFile.fromTreeUri(context, assetRootUri) ?: return@withContext emptyMap()
        val matchedResults = mutableMapOf<Int, LocalAssetSet>()
        val recordsToPersist = mutableListOf<com.example.database.AssetMatchEntity>()

        val canonicalFilter = platformFilter?.let { PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(it) }
        val platformDirs = rootDoc.listFiles().filter { 
            it.isDirectory && !it.name.orEmpty().startsWith(".") &&
            (canonicalFilter == null || PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(it.name.orEmpty()).equals(canonicalFilter, ignoreCase = true))
        }
        val gamesByPlatform = games.groupBy { PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(it.platform) }

        var processed = 0
        val totalGames = games.size

        for (pDir in platformDirs) {
            val rawPlatformName = pDir.name ?: continue
            val canonicalPlatform = PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(rawPlatformName)
            if (canonicalFilter != null && !canonicalPlatform.equals(canonicalFilter, ignoreCase = true)) {
                continue
            }
            val allCandidateGames = gamesByPlatform[canonicalPlatform] ?: games.filter {
                it.platform.equals(rawPlatformName, ignoreCase = true) ||
                PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(it.platform).equals(canonicalPlatform, ignoreCase = true)
            }

            // Incremental optimization: if missingOnly is enabled, filter games that still miss any asset or description
            val candidateGames = if (missingOnly && !forceRematch) {
                allCandidateGames.filter { g ->
                    g.coverPath.isNullOrBlank() ||
                    g.backgroundPath.isNullOrBlank() ||
                    g.logoPath.isNullOrBlank() ||
                    g.screenshotPath.isNullOrBlank() ||
                    g.iconPath.isNullOrBlank() ||
                    g.description.isNullOrBlank()
                }
            } else {
                allCandidateGames
            }

            if (candidateGames.isEmpty()) continue

            // Collect all subdirectories and direct files in this platform directory
            val subItems = pDir.listFiles().filter { !it.name.orEmpty().startsWith(".") }
            val subDirectories = subItems.filter { it.isDirectory }
            val directFiles = subItems.filter { it.isFile }

            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            // A. Parse gamelist.xml if present
            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            val gamelistFile = directFiles.firstOrNull { it.name.equals("gamelist.xml", ignoreCase = true) }
            val gamelistData = mutableMapOf<String, Map<String, String>>()
            if (gamelistFile != null) {
                try {
                    context.contentResolver.openInputStream(gamelistFile.uri)?.use { stream ->
                        val xmlText = stream.bufferedReader().readText()
                        val gameRegex = Regex("<game>(.*?)</game>", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL)
                        val nameRegex = Regex("<name>(.*?)</name>", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL)
                        val pathRegex = Regex("<path>(.*?)</path>")
                        val descRegex = Regex("<desc>(.*?)</desc>", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL)
                        val imageRegex = Regex("<image>(.*?)</image>")
                        val marqueeRegex = Regex("<marquee>(.*?)</marquee>")
                        val videoRegex = Regex("<video>(.*?)</video>")
                        val thumbnailRegex = Regex("<thumbnail>(.*?)</thumbnail>")
                        
                        gameRegex.findAll(xmlText).forEach { match ->
                            val gameBlock = match.groupValues[1]
                            val nameMatch = nameRegex.find(gameBlock)
                            val pathMatch = pathRegex.find(gameBlock)
                            val key = pathMatch?.groupValues?.get(1)?.substringAfterLast("/")?.trim() ?: nameMatch?.groupValues?.get(1)?.trim()
                            
                            if (key != null) {
                                val data = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
                                nameMatch?.let { data["name"] = it.groupValues[1].trim() }
                                descRegex.find(gameBlock)?.let { data["desc"] = it.groupValues[1].trim() }
                                imageRegex.find(gameBlock)?.let { data["image"] = it.groupValues[1].trim() }
                                marqueeRegex.find(gameBlock)?.let { data["marquee"] = it.groupValues[1].trim() }
                                videoRegex.find(gameBlock)?.let { data["video"] = it.groupValues[1].trim() }
                                thumbnailRegex.find(gameBlock)?.let { data["thumbnail"] = it.groupValues[1].trim() }
                                gamelistData[key] = data
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    e.printStackTrace()
                }
            }

            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            // B. Parse metadata.pegasus.txt / metadata.txt if present
            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            val pegasusFile = directFiles.firstOrNull { 
                it.name.equals("metadata.pegasus.txt", ignoreCase = true) || it.name.equals("metadata.txt", ignoreCase = true) 
            }
            val pegasusData = mutableMapOf<String, Map<String, String>>()
            if (pegasusFile != null) {
                try {
                    context.contentResolver.openInputStream(pegasusFile.uri)?.use { stream ->
                        val lines = stream.bufferedReader().readLines()
                        var currentKey: String? = null
                        var currentMap = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
                        
                        for (rawLine in lines) {
                            val line = rawLine.trim()
                            if (line.startsWith("game:") || line.startsWith("file:")) {
                                val valStr = line.substringAfter(":").trim()
                                val keyName = valStr.substringAfterLast("/")
                                if (currentKey != null && currentMap.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    pegasusData[currentKey] = currentMap
                                }
                                currentKey = keyName
                                if (!pegasusData.containsKey(currentKey)) {
                                    currentMap = mutableMapOf()
                                } else {
                                    currentMap = pegasusData[currentKey]?.toMutableMap() ?: mutableMapOf()
                                }
                            } else if (currentKey != null && line.contains(":")) {
                                val k = line.substringBefore(":").trim().lowercase()
                                val v = line.substringAfter(":").trim()
                                currentMap[k] = v
                            }
                        }
                        if (currentKey != null && currentMap.isNotEmpty()) {
                            pegasusData[currentKey] = currentMap
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    e.printStackTrace()
                }
            }

            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            // C. Parse CSV metadata (metadata.csv / description.csv) if present
            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            val csvFile = directFiles.firstOrNull { 
                it.name.equals("metadata.csv", ignoreCase = true) || it.name.equals("description.csv", ignoreCase = true) || it.name.equals("gamelist.csv", ignoreCase = true)
            }
            val csvData = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
            if (csvFile != null) {
                try {
                    context.contentResolver.openInputStream(csvFile.uri)?.use { stream ->
                        val bytes = stream.readBytes()
                        val text = String(bytes, Charsets.UTF_8)
                        val csvService = com.example.metadata.MetadataCsvService(context)
                        val rows = csvService.parseFullCsv(text)
                        for (row in rows) {
                            if (row.size >= 2) {
                                val titleOrFile = row[0].trim()
                                val desc = row.getOrNull(2)?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: row.getOrNull(1)?.trim().orEmpty()
                                if (titleOrFile.isNotBlank() && desc.isNotBlank()) {
                                    csvData[titleOrFile] = desc
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    e.printStackTrace()
                }
            }
            
            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            // OPTIMIZED: We no longer scan ALL subdirectories here because 
            // if a user has 2000 game folders, this makes 2000 SAF IPC calls.
            // Instead, we only prefetch the typed asset directories (like media/)
            // which are usually just 1-5 folders per platform.
            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            val allPrefetchedFiles = mutableMapOf<String, DocumentFile>()
            directFiles.forEach { allPrefetchedFiles[it.name.orEmpty()] = it }

            // Check if there are typed asset directories (like Named_Boxarts, covers, media/box2d, etc.)
            val typedDirs = mutableMapOf<AssetKind, MutableList<DocumentFile>>()
            val validGameFolders = mutableListOf<DocumentFile>()
            
            for (subDir in subDirectories) {
                val dName = subDir.name.orEmpty().lowercase()
                when {
                    dName == "named_boxarts" || dName == "covers" || dName == "boxart" || dName == "boxarts" || dName == "box2d" -> {
                        val files = subDir.listFiles().filter { it.isFile }
                        typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.BOX_FRONT) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                        files.forEach { allPrefetchedFiles[it.name.orEmpty()] = it }
                    }
                    dName == "named_snaps" || dName == "screenshots" || dName == "snaps" || dName == "screenshot" || dName == "snap" -> {
                        val files = subDir.listFiles().filter { it.isFile }
                        typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.SLIDE) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                        typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.HERO) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                        files.forEach { allPrefetchedFiles[it.name.orEmpty()] = it }
                    }
                    dName == "named_titles" || dName == "titles" || dName == "logos" || dName == "wheel" || dName == "clearlogo" || dName == "logo" || dName == "marquee" -> {
                        val files = subDir.listFiles().filter { it.isFile }
                        typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.TITLE) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                        files.forEach { allPrefetchedFiles[it.name.orEmpty()] = it }
                    }
                    dName == "heroes" || dName == "backgrounds" || dName == "fanart" || dName == "backdrops" || dName == "banner" -> {
                        val files = subDir.listFiles().filter { it.isFile }
                        typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.HERO) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                        files.forEach { allPrefetchedFiles[it.name.orEmpty()] = it }
                    }
                    dName == "icons" || dName == "avatars" || dName == "icon" || dName == "thumbs" -> {
                        val files = subDir.listFiles().filter { it.isFile }
                        typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.ICON) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                        files.forEach { allPrefetchedFiles[it.name.orEmpty()] = it }
                    }
                    dName == "media" -> {
                        // Check subdirectories inside media/ (e.g. media/box2d, media/screenshot)
                        for (mediaSub in subDir.listFiles().filter { it.isDirectory }) {
                            val mName = mediaSub.name.orEmpty().lowercase()
                            val files = mediaSub.listFiles().filter { it.isFile }
                            files.forEach { allPrefetchedFiles[it.name.orEmpty()] = it }
                            when {
                                mName.contains("box") || mName.contains("cover") -> typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.BOX_FRONT) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                                mName.contains("screenshot") || mName.contains("snap") -> {
                                    typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.SLIDE) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                                    typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.HERO) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                                }
                                mName.contains("wheel") || mName.contains("logo") || mName.contains("title") || mName.contains("marquee") -> typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.TITLE) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                                mName.contains("background") || mName.contains("fanart") || mName.contains("banner") -> typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.HERO) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                                mName.contains("icon") || mName.contains("thumb") -> typedDirs.getOrPut(AssetKind.ICON) { mutableListOf() }.addAll(files)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    else -> {
                        validGameFolders.add(subDir)
                    }
                }
            }

            for (game in candidateGames) {
                processed++
                onProgress(processed, totalGames, "比對資產: [${game.platform}] ${game.title}")

                val romIdentity = RomIdentityUtils.getStableRomIdentity(context, game)
                val cachedRecords = if (!forceRematch && assetMatchDao != null) {
                    val recs = assetMatchDao.getRecordsForRom(romIdentity).ifEmpty { assetMatchDao.getRecordsForGame(game.id) }
                    recs.associateBy { it.assetType }
                } else {
                    emptyMap()
                }

                fun shouldSearchAsset(record: com.example.database.AssetMatchEntity?): Boolean {
                    if (forceRematch || record == null) return true
                    if (record.status == "MATCHED" || record.status == "FOUND" || record.status == "VERIFIED") {
                        return false // FOUND asset is permanently skipped from re-searching
                    }
                    return true // NOT_FOUND assets retry immediately when user manually starts Asset Scan
                }

                val searchCover = shouldSearchAsset(cachedRecords["COVER"] ?: cachedRecords["BOX_FRONT"])
                val searchIcon = shouldSearchAsset(cachedRecords["ICON"])
                val searchLogo = shouldSearchAsset(cachedRecords["LOGO"] ?: cachedRecords["TITLE"])
                val searchBg = shouldSearchAsset(cachedRecords["BACKGROUND"] ?: cachedRecords["HERO"])
                val searchScreenshot = shouldSearchAsset(cachedRecords["SCREENSHOT"] ?: cachedRecords["SLIDE"])
                val searchDesc = shouldSearchAsset(cachedRecords["DESCRIPTION"])

                var boxFrontUri: String? = (cachedRecords["COVER"] ?: cachedRecords["BOX_FRONT"])?.matchedAssetUri
                var iconUri: String? = cachedRecords["ICON"]?.matchedAssetUri
                var titleUri: String? = (cachedRecords["LOGO"] ?: cachedRecords["TITLE"])?.matchedAssetUri
                var heroUri: String? = (cachedRecords["BACKGROUND"] ?: cachedRecords["HERO"])?.matchedAssetUri
                var slideUri: String? = (cachedRecords["SCREENSHOT"] ?: cachedRecords["SLIDE"])?.matchedAssetUri
                var parsedDesc: String? = cachedRecords["DESCRIPTION"]?.matchedAssetUri

                val needsAnySearch = searchCover || searchIcon || searchLogo || searchBg || searchScreenshot || searchDesc

                if (needsAnySearch) {
                    // 1. Check matching game folder (Asset Root / Platform / GameTitle / ...)
                    val matchedFolder = AssetMatcher.findBestMatchCandidate(
                        candidates = validGameFolders,
                        getName = { it.name.orEmpty() },
                        gameTitle = game.title,
                        fuzzyThreshold = fuzzyThreshold
                    )

                    if (matchedFolder != null) {
                        val assetFiles = matchedFolder.listFiles().filter { it.isFile && !it.name.orEmpty().startsWith(".") }
                        for (file in assetFiles) {
                            val fname = file.name.orEmpty()
                            val ext = fname.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase()
                            if (ext == "txt" || ext == "desc") {
                                if (searchDesc && parsedDesc == null) {
                                    try {
                                        context.contentResolver.openInputStream(file.uri)?.use { stream ->
                                            parsedDesc = stream.bufferedReader().readText().trim()
                                        }
                                    } catch (_: Exception) {}
                                }
                            } else {
                                if (searchCover && boxFrontUri == null && AssetMatcher.isMatch(fname, AssetKind.BOX_FRONT)) boxFrontUri = file.uri.toString()
                                if (searchIcon && iconUri == null && AssetMatcher.isMatch(fname, AssetKind.ICON)) iconUri = file.uri.toString()
                                if (searchLogo && titleUri == null && AssetMatcher.isMatch(fname, AssetKind.TITLE)) titleUri = file.uri.toString()
                                if (searchBg && heroUri == null && AssetMatcher.isMatch(fname, AssetKind.HERO)) heroUri = file.uri.toString()
                                if (searchScreenshot && slideUri == null && AssetMatcher.isMatch(fname, AssetKind.SLIDE)) slideUri = file.uri.toString()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // 2. Check typed directories (e.g. Named_Boxarts, covers, media/box2d)
                if (searchCover && boxFrontUri == null) {
                    typedDirs[AssetKind.BOX_FRONT]?.let { candidates ->
                        AssetMatcher.findBestMatchCandidate(
                            candidates = candidates,
                            getName = { it.name.orEmpty().substringBeforeLast('.') },
                            gameTitle = game.title,
                            fuzzyThreshold = fuzzyThreshold
                        )
                    }?.let { boxFrontUri = it.uri.toString() }
                }

                if (searchScreenshot && slideUri == null) {
                    typedDirs[AssetKind.SLIDE]?.let { candidates ->
                        AssetMatcher.findBestMatchCandidate(
                            candidates = candidates,
                            getName = { it.name.orEmpty().substringBeforeLast('.') },
                            gameTitle = game.title,
                            fuzzyThreshold = fuzzyThreshold
                        )
                    }?.let { slideUri = it.uri.toString() }
                }

                if (searchLogo && titleUri == null) {
                    typedDirs[AssetKind.TITLE]?.let { candidates ->
                        AssetMatcher.findBestMatchCandidate(
                            candidates = candidates,
                            getName = { it.name.orEmpty().substringBeforeLast('.') },
                            gameTitle = game.title,
                            fuzzyThreshold = fuzzyThreshold
                        )
                    }?.let { titleUri = it.uri.toString() }
                }

                if (searchBg && heroUri == null) {
                    typedDirs[AssetKind.HERO]?.let { candidates ->
                        AssetMatcher.findBestMatchCandidate(
                            candidates = candidates,
                            getName = { it.name.orEmpty().substringBeforeLast('.') },
                            gameTitle = game.title,
                            fuzzyThreshold = fuzzyThreshold
                        )
                    }?.let { heroUri = it.uri.toString() }
                }

                if (searchIcon && iconUri == null) {
                    typedDirs[AssetKind.ICON]?.let { candidates ->
                        AssetMatcher.findBestMatchCandidate(
                            candidates = candidates,
                            getName = { it.name.orEmpty().substringBeforeLast('.') },
                            gameTitle = game.title,
                            fuzzyThreshold = fuzzyThreshold
                        )
                    }?.let { iconUri = it.uri.toString() }
                }

                // 3. Check direct files inside the platform directory
                // Flexibly split separators: `-`, `_`, `.`
                for (file in directFiles) {
                    val fname = file.name.orEmpty()
                    val ext = fname.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase()
                    val baseName = fname.substringBeforeLast('.')
                    
                    if (ext == "txt" || ext == "desc") {
                        val gamePart = if (baseName.contains("-")) baseName.substringBeforeLast("-")
                                       else if (baseName.contains("_")) baseName.substringBeforeLast("_")
                                       else baseName
                        if (parsedDesc == null && AssetMatcher.matchGameFolder(gamePart, game.title, fuzzyThreshold)) {
                            try {
                                context.contentResolver.openInputStream(file.uri)?.use { stream ->
                                    parsedDesc = stream.bufferedReader().readText().trim()
                                }
                            } catch (_: Exception) {}
                        }
                    } else {
                        val sepIdx = maxOf(baseName.lastIndexOf('-'), baseName.lastIndexOf('_'))
                        if (sepIdx > 0) {
                            val gamePart = baseName.substring(0, sepIdx)
                            val suffix = baseName.substring(sepIdx + 1).lowercase()
                            if (AssetMatcher.matchGameFolder(gamePart, game.title, fuzzyThreshold)) {
                                when {
                                    AssetMatcher.isMatch(suffix, AssetKind.BOX_FRONT) || suffix == "boxart" || suffix == "cover" || suffix == "image" -> if (boxFrontUri == null) boxFrontUri = file.uri.toString()
                                    AssetMatcher.isMatch(suffix, AssetKind.SLIDE) || suffix == "video" || suffix == "snap" || suffix == "screenshot" || suffix == "screen" -> if (slideUri == null) slideUri = file.uri.toString()
                                    AssetMatcher.isMatch(suffix, AssetKind.HERO) || suffix == "banner" || suffix == "fanart" -> if (heroUri == null) heroUri = file.uri.toString()
                                    AssetMatcher.isMatch(suffix, AssetKind.TITLE) || suffix == "logo" || suffix == "wheel" || suffix == "marquee" -> if (titleUri == null) titleUri = file.uri.toString()
                                    AssetMatcher.isMatch(suffix, AssetKind.ICON) || suffix == "thumb" || suffix == "avatar" -> if (iconUri == null) iconUri = file.uri.toString()
                                }
                            }
                        } else if (AssetMatcher.matchGameFolder(baseName, game.title, fuzzyThreshold)) {
                            if (boxFrontUri == null) boxFrontUri = file.uri.toString()
                            if (heroUri == null) heroUri = file.uri.toString()
                            if (iconUri == null) iconUri = file.uri.toString()
                        }
                    }
                }

                // 4. Check metadata files (gamelist.xml, metadata.pegasus.txt, description.csv)
                val decodedRomPath = android.net.Uri.decode(game.romPath)
                val romFilename = decodedRomPath.substringAfterLast("/").substringAfterLast(":")

                // 4a. gamelist.xml
                val matchedGamelistKey = gamelistData.keys.firstOrNull { it.equals(romFilename, ignoreCase = true) } 
                    ?: gamelistData.keys.firstOrNull { AssetMatcher.matchGameFolder(it, game.title, fuzzyThreshold) }
                
                matchedGamelistKey?.let { key ->
                    val data = gamelistData[key]
                    if (parsedDesc == null) parsedDesc = data?.get("desc")
                    
                    fun resolveGamelistImage(path: String?): String? {
                        if (path.isNullOrBlank()) return null
                        val filename = path.substringAfterLast("/")
                        val fastMatch = allPrefetchedFiles[filename]?.uri?.toString()
                        if (fastMatch != null) return fastMatch
                        
                        // Fallback: Exact path walk for missing images (avoids upfront N^2 listFiles)
                        var currentDoc = pDir
                        val segments = path.replace("\\", "/").split("/").filter { it.isNotBlank() && it != "." }
                        for (segment in segments) {
                            currentDoc = currentDoc.findFile(segment) ?: return null
                        }
                        return currentDoc.uri.toString()
                    }
                    
                    if (boxFrontUri == null) boxFrontUri = resolveGamelistImage(data?.get("image"))
                    if (titleUri == null) titleUri = resolveGamelistImage(data?.get("marquee"))
                    if (slideUri == null) slideUri = resolveGamelistImage(data?.get("thumbnail")) ?: resolveGamelistImage(data?.get("video"))
                }

                // 4b. metadata.pegasus.txt
                val matchedPegasusKey = pegasusData.keys.firstOrNull { it.equals(romFilename, ignoreCase = true) }
                    ?: pegasusData.keys.firstOrNull { AssetMatcher.matchGameFolder(it, game.title, fuzzyThreshold) }
                
                matchedPegasusKey?.let { key ->
                    val data = pegasusData[key]
                    if (parsedDesc == null) parsedDesc = data?.get("description") ?: data?.get("summary")
                    
                    fun resolvePegasusImage(path: String?): String? {
                        if (path.isNullOrBlank()) return null
                        val filename = path.substringAfterLast("/").substringAfterLast('\\')
                        val fastMatch = allPrefetchedFiles[filename]?.uri?.toString()
                        if (fastMatch != null) return fastMatch
                        
                        // Fallback: Exact path walk
                        var currentDoc = pDir
                        val segments = path.replace("\\", "/").split("/").filter { it.isNotBlank() && it != "." }
                        for (segment in segments) {
                            currentDoc = currentDoc.findFile(segment) ?: return null
                        }
                        return currentDoc.uri.toString()
                    }
                    
                    if (boxFrontUri == null) boxFrontUri = resolvePegasusImage(data?.get("assets.box_front") ?: data?.get("assets.boxfront"))
                    if (titleUri == null) titleUri = resolvePegasusImage(data?.get("assets.logo"))
                    if (heroUri == null) heroUri = resolvePegasusImage(data?.get("assets.background") ?: data?.get("assets.banner"))
                    if (slideUri == null) slideUri = resolvePegasusImage(data?.get("assets.screenshot"))
                }

                // 4c. description.csv / metadata.csv (Highest Priority for Description)
                var isDescFromCsv = false
                val csvMatch = AssetMatcher.findBestMatchCandidate(
                    candidates = csvData.entries.toList(),
                    getName = { it.key },
                    gameTitle = game.title,
                    fuzzyThreshold = fuzzyThreshold
                )
                if (csvMatch != null) {
                    parsedDesc = csvMatch.value
                    isDescFromCsv = true
                }

                if (boxFrontUri != null || iconUri != null || titleUri != null || heroUri != null || slideUri != null || parsedDesc != null) {
                    val assetSet = LocalAssetSet(
                        icon = iconUri ?: game.iconPath,
                        logo = titleUri ?: game.logoPath,
                        hero = heroUri ?: game.backgroundPath,
                        slide = slideUri ?: game.screenshotPath,
                        boxFront = boxFrontUri ?: game.coverPath,
                        description = parsedDesc,
                        isDescriptionFromCsv = isDescFromCsv
                    )
                    matchedResults[game.id] = assetSet
                }
                
                if (needsAnySearch && assetMatchDao != null) {
                    val addRecord = { type: String, uri: String?, searched: Boolean ->
                        val existingRec = cachedRecords[type]
                        val finalUri = uri ?: existingRec?.matchedAssetUri
                        val finalStatus = when {
                            !finalUri.isNullOrBlank() -> "MATCHED"
                            existingRec?.status == "MATCHED" || existingRec?.status == "FOUND" -> "MATCHED"
                            else -> "NOT_FOUND"
                        }
                        recordsToPersist.add(
                            com.example.database.AssetMatchEntity(
                                gameId = game.id,
                                romIdentity = romIdentity,
                                assetType = type,
                                source = "LOCAL_MATCH",
                                sourceKey = finalUri ?: "",
                                matchedAssetUri = finalUri,
                                status = finalStatus,
                                lastCheckedAt = if (searched) System.currentTimeMillis() else (existingRec?.lastCheckedAt ?: System.currentTimeMillis())
                            )
                        )
                    }
                    addRecord("COVER", boxFrontUri, searchCover)
                    addRecord("ICON", iconUri, searchIcon)
                    addRecord("LOGO", titleUri, searchLogo)
                    addRecord("BACKGROUND", heroUri, searchBg)
                    addRecord("SCREENSHOT", slideUri, searchScreenshot)
                    addRecord("DESCRIPTION", parsedDesc, searchDesc)
                }
            }
        }

        if (recordsToPersist.isNotEmpty() && assetMatchDao != null) {
            assetMatchDao.insertRecords(recordsToPersist)
        }

        matchedResults
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/p3/asset/AssetMatcher.kt

package com.example.p3.asset

object AssetMatcher {

    fun isMatch(filename: String, kind: AssetKind): Boolean {
        val name = filename.lowercase().substringBeforeLast('.')
        return when (kind) {
            AssetKind.BOX_FRONT -> name.startsWith("boxfront") || name.startsWith("cover") ||
                    name.startsWith("front") || name.startsWith("poster") || name.startsWith("grid") ||
                    name.contains("boxart") || name == "2d" || name == "3d"
            AssetKind.ICON -> name.startsWith("icon") || name.startsWith("avatar") || name.startsWith("square")
            AssetKind.TITLE -> name.startsWith("title") || name.startsWith("logo") ||
                    name.startsWith("wheel") || name.startsWith("marquee") || name.startsWith("clearlogo")
            AssetKind.HERO -> name.startsWith("hero") || name.startsWith("background") ||
                    name.startsWith("fanart") || name.startsWith("banner") || name.startsWith("art") ||
                    name.contains("backdrop") || name.contains("wallpaper")
            AssetKind.SLIDE -> name.startsWith("slide") || name.startsWith("snap") ||
                    name.startsWith("screenshot") || name.startsWith("screen") || name.contains("ingame")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Safe normalization preserving region/tag info (USA, Australia, Europe, Japan, Rev 1, numbers).
     */
    fun safeNormalize(s: String): String {
        val nameWithoutExt = s.trim().substringBeforeLast('.')
        val articleNormalized = GameNameNormalizer.normalizeArticles(nameWithoutExt)
        return articleNormalized.lowercase()
            .replace(Regex("[_\\-]"), " ")
            .replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ")
            .trim()
    }

    /**
     * Extract important tokens for mismatch prevention.
     */
    fun extractImportantTokens(s: String): Map<String, Set<String>> {
        val lower = safeNormalize(s)
        val tokens = mutableMapOf<String, MutableSet<String>>()

        // Digits / Numbers
        val digits = Regex("""\b\d+\b""").findAll(lower).map { it.value }.toSet()
        if (digits.isNotEmpty()) tokens["digits"] = digits.toMutableSet()

        // Roman Numerals
        val romanRegex = Regex("""\b(i|ii|iii|iv|v|vi|vii|viii|ix|x|xi|xii|xiii|xiv|xv)\b""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
        val romans = romanRegex.findAll(lower).map { it.value.lowercase() }.toSet()
        if (romans.isNotEmpty()) tokens["romans"] = romans.toMutableSet()

        // Regions
        val regionKeywords = setOf("usa", "europe", "japan", "world", "korea", "asia", "australia", "germany", "france", "spain", "italy", "sweden", "netherlands", "brazil", "china", "pal", "ntsc")
        val foundRegions = lower.split(Regex("""[^a-z0-9]+""")).filter { it in regionKeywords }.toSet()
        if (foundRegions.isNotEmpty()) tokens["regions"] = foundRegions.toMutableSet()

        // Languages
        val languageKeywords = setOf("en", "ja", "fr", "de", "es", "it", "nl", "pt", "sv", "no", "da", "fi", "zh", "ko")
        val foundLanguages = lower.split(Regex("""[^a-z0-9]+""")).filter { it in languageKeywords }.toSet()
        if (foundLanguages.isNotEmpty()) tokens["languages"] = foundLanguages.toMutableSet()

        // Color / Game sub-versions (e.g. Pokemon Red vs Blue)
        val versionKeywords = setOf("red", "blue", "yellow", "gold", "silver", "ruby", "sapphire", "emerald", "diamond", "pearl", "black", "white", "sword", "shield", "scarlet", "violet")
        val foundVersions = lower.split(Regex("""[^a-z0-9]+""")).filter { it in versionKeywords }.toSet()
        if (foundVersions.isNotEmpty()) tokens["versions"] = foundVersions.toMutableSet()

        // Revisions
        val revRegex = Regex("""\brev\s*[0-9a-z]+\b""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
        val foundRevs = revRegex.findAll(lower).map { it.value.lowercase() }.toSet()
        if (foundRevs.isNotEmpty()) tokens["revisions"] = foundRevs.toMutableSet()

        return tokens
    }

    /**
     * Check if two strings have conflicting important tokens (e.g., Red vs Blue, IV vs V, 1 vs 2, USA vs Japan).
     */
    fun hasTokenConflict(s1: String, s2: String): Boolean {
        val t1 = extractImportantTokens(s1)
        val t2 = extractImportantTokens(s2)

        for ((category, set1) in t1) {
            val set2 = t2[category] ?: continue
            if (set1.isNotEmpty() && set2.isNotEmpty() && set1.intersect(set2).isEmpty()) {
                return true
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    /**
     * Compute Levenshtein similarity distance between two strings using safe normalization.
     */
    fun similarity(s1: String, s2: String): Double {
        val n1 = safeNormalize(s1)
        val n2 = safeNormalize(s2)
        if (n1 == n2) return 1.0
        if (n1.isEmpty() || n2.isEmpty()) return 0.0

        val maxLen = maxOf(n1.length, n2.length)
        val dp = Array(n1.length + 1) { IntArray(n2.length + 1) }

        for (i in 0..n1.length) dp[i][0] = i
        for (j in 0..n2.length) dp[0][j] = j

        for (i in 1..n1.length) {
            for (j in 1..n2.length) {
                val cost = if (n1[i - 1] == n2[j - 1]) 0 else 1
                dp[i][j] = minOf(
                    dp[i - 1][j] + 1,      // deletion
                    dp[i][j - 1] + 1,      // insertion
                    dp[i - 1][j - 1] + cost // substitution
                )
            }
        }

        val distance = dp[n1.length][n2.length]
        return 1.0 - (distance.toDouble() / maxLen.toDouble())
    }

    /**
     * Match a folder or candidate name against a game title using strict 5-level match priority.
     */
    fun matchGameFolder(folderName: String, gameTitle: String, fuzzyThreshold: Double = 0.85): Boolean {
        val folderTrim = folderName.trim()
        val titleTrim = gameTitle.trim()

        // Level 1: Exact Match (case-insensitive)
        if (folderTrim.equals(titleTrim, ignoreCase = true)) {
            return true
        }

        // Level 2: Safe Normalization Match
        val folderNorm = safeNormalize(folderName)
        val titleNorm = safeNormalize(gameTitle)
        if (folderNorm == titleNorm) {
            return true
        }

        // Check token conflict early
        if (hasTokenConflict(folderName, gameTitle)) {
            return false
        }

        // Level 3: Canonical / Core Title Match
        val folderCore = GameNameNormalizer.canonicalCoreTitle(folderName)
        val titleCore = GameNameNormalizer.canonicalCoreTitle(gameTitle)
        if (folderCore.isNotBlank() && folderCore == titleCore) {
            return true
        }

        // Level 4: Safe Variant match
        val variants = GameNameNormalizer.variants(gameTitle)
        if (variants.any { safeNormalize(it) == folderNorm || GameNameNormalizer.canonicalCoreTitle(it) == folderCore }) {
            return true
        }

        // Level 5: Strict Fuzzy Levenshtein
        val sim = similarity(folderName, gameTitle)
        return sim >= fuzzyThreshold
    }

    /**
     * Finds the single best match candidate from a list of items for a game title,
     * strictly enforcing:
     * Level 1: Raw Exact Match
     * Level 2: Safe Normalized Exact Match
     * Level 3: Canonical / Core Title Exact Match (with Deterministic Metadata Ranking)
     * Level 4: Safe Variants Match
     * Level 5: Single High-Confidence Fuzzy (with Token Protection & Candidate Margin)
     * Level 6: Reject (returns null)
     */
    fun <T> findBestMatchCandidate(
        candidates: List<T>,
        getName: (T) -> String,
        gameTitle: String,
        fuzzyThreshold: Double = 0.85
    ): T? {
        if (candidates.isEmpty()) return null
        val titleTrim = gameTitle.trim()

        // Level 1: Raw Exact Match (case-insensitive)
        val exact = candidates.firstOrNull { getName(it).trim().equals(titleTrim, ignoreCase = true) }
        if (exact != null) return exact

        // Level 2: Safe Normalized Exact Match
        val titleNorm = safeNormalize(gameTitle)
        val safeNormMatch = candidates.firstOrNull { safeNormalize(getName(it)) == titleNorm }
        if (safeNormMatch != null) return safeNormMatch

        // Level 3: Canonical / Core Title Exact Match
        val titleCore = GameNameNormalizer.canonicalCoreTitle(gameTitle)
        if (titleCore.isNotBlank()) {
            val coreMatches = candidates.filter { candidate ->
                val candName = getName(candidate)
                !hasTokenConflict(candName, gameTitle) &&
                GameNameNormalizer.canonicalCoreTitle(candName) == titleCore
            }
            if (coreMatches.size == 1) {
                return coreMatches.first()
            } else if (coreMatches.size > 1) {
                val resolved = resolveCoreConflict(coreMatches, getName, gameTitle)
                if (resolved != null) {
                    return resolved
                }
            }
        }

        // Level 4: Safe Project Variants Match
        val variants = GameNameNormalizer.variants(gameTitle)
        val variantMatches = candidates.filter { candidate ->
            val candName = getName(candidate)
            val normCand = safeNormalize(candName)
            val candCore = GameNameNormalizer.canonicalCoreTitle(candName)
            !hasTokenConflict(candName, gameTitle) &&
            variants.any { safeNormalize(it) == normCand || GameNameNormalizer.canonicalCoreTitle(it) == candCore }
        }
        if (variantMatches.size == 1) {
            return variantMatches.first()
        } else if (variantMatches.size > 1) {
            val resolved = resolveCoreConflict(variantMatches, getName, gameTitle)
            if (resolved != null) {
                return resolved
            }
        }

        // Level 5: Strict Fuzzy Match with Token Protection & Candidate Margin
        val scored = candidates.mapNotNull { candidate ->
            val candidateName = getName(candidate)
            if (hasTokenConflict(candidateName, gameTitle)) {
                null
            } else {
                val sim = similarity(candidateName, gameTitle)
                if (sim >= fuzzyThreshold) candidate to sim else null
            }
        }.sortedByDescending { it.second }

        if (scored.isNotEmpty()) {
            val topScore = scored.first().second
            val topCandidate = scored.first().first
            if (scored.size == 1) {
                // High confidence threshold (>= 0.88) required for a single fuzzy candidate
                if (topScore >= 0.88) return topCandidate
            } else {
                val secondScore = scored[1].second
                val gap = topScore - secondScore
                // High confidence (>= 0.92) or strong candidate margin gap (>= 0.08) required
                if (topScore >= 0.92 || gap >= 0.08) {
                    return topCandidate
                }
            }
        }

        // Level 6: Reject
        return null
    }

    /**
     * Deterministic resolution when multiple candidates share the exact same Canonical / Core Title.
     * Compares extracted metadata tokens (regions, versions, revisions, languages).
     * If safely distinguishable, returns the uniquely best matching candidate.
     * If ambiguous or conflicting, returns null to avoid mismatching different editions/regions.
     */
    private fun <T> resolveCoreConflict(
        candidates: List<T>,
        getName: (T) -> String,
        gameTitle: String
    ): T? {
        val gameTokens = extractImportantTokens(gameTitle)
        val gameRegions = gameTokens["regions"] ?: emptySet()
        val gameVersions = gameTokens["versions"] ?: emptySet()
        val gameRevs = gameTokens["revisions"] ?: emptySet()

        // 1. If game specifies a region, filter candidates containing that region
        if (gameRegions.isNotEmpty()) {
            val regionMatches = candidates.filter { candidate ->
                val candTokens = extractImportantTokens(getName(candidate))
                val candRegions = candTokens["regions"] ?: emptySet()
                candRegions.intersect(gameRegions).isNotEmpty()
            }
            if (regionMatches.size == 1) return regionMatches.first()
            if (regionMatches.size > 1) {
                return resolveCoreConflict(regionMatches, getName, gameTitle)
            }
        }

        // 2. If game specifies a sub-version (e.g. Red / Blue / Ruby / Sapphire)
        if (gameVersions.isNotEmpty()) {
            val versionMatches = candidates.filter { candidate ->
                val candTokens = extractImportantTokens(getName(candidate))
                val candVersions = candTokens["versions"] ?: emptySet()
                candVersions.intersect(gameVersions).isNotEmpty()
            }
            if (versionMatches.size == 1) return versionMatches.first()
        }

        // 3. If game specifies a revision (e.g. Rev 1, Rev A)
        if (gameRevs.isNotEmpty()) {
            val revMatches = candidates.filter { candidate ->
                val candTokens = extractImportantTokens(getName(candidate))
                val candRevs = candTokens["revisions"] ?: emptySet()
                candRevs.intersect(gameRevs).isNotEmpty()
            }
            if (revMatches.size == 1) return revMatches.first()
        }

        // 4. If game specifies language(s) (e.g. En, Fr, Ja, Zh), filter candidates containing those languages
        val gameLanguages = gameTokens["languages"] ?: emptySet()
        if (gameLanguages.isNotEmpty()) {
            val languageMatches = candidates.filter { candidate ->
                val candTokens = extractImportantTokens(getName(candidate))
                val candLanguages = candTokens["languages"] ?: emptySet()
                candLanguages.intersect(gameLanguages).isNotEmpty()
            }
            if (languageMatches.size == 1) return languageMatches.first()
            if (languageMatches.size > 1) {
                return resolveCoreConflict(languageMatches, getName, gameTitle)
            }
        }

        // 5. Standard region preference fallback if game title itself has no region specified
        if (gameRegions.isEmpty()) {
            val preferredRegions = listOf(
                setOf("world"),
                setOf("usa"),
                setOf("europe"),
                setOf("japan")
            )
            for (pref in preferredRegions) {
                val matches = candidates.filter { candidate ->
                    val candTokens = extractImportantTokens(getName(candidate))
                    val candRegions = candTokens["regions"] ?: emptySet()
                    candRegions.intersect(pref).isNotEmpty()
                }
                if (matches.size == 1) return matches.first()
            }
        }

        // Unresolvable ambiguity: reject rather than mismatch
        return null
    }
}
