package com.example.research

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import com.example.agent.AICuratorAgent
import com.example.api.AiFeature
import com.example.api.ApiKeyRotationManager
import com.example.api.ApiProfile
import com.example.api.AiProviderResolver
import com.example.api.AiRequest
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.File

data class ResearchResult(
    val gameTitle: String,
    val platform: String,
    val answerText: String,
    val sources: List<ResearchSource>,
    val usedWebSearch: Boolean,
    val searchReason: String? = null,
    val providerUsed: String = "",
    val modelUsed: String = "",
    val timestampMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
)

class ResearchEngine(
    private val context: Context,
    private val profilesProvider: () -> List<ApiProfile>,
    private val rotationManager: ApiKeyRotationManager = ApiKeyRotationManager(),
    private val resolver: AiProviderResolver = AiProviderResolver(),
    private val searchService: WebSearchService = DefaultWebSearchService
) {
    private val TAG = "ResearchEngine"

    /**
     * Evaluates whether Web Search is needed given a user query, search mode, and local data status.
     */
    suspend fun evaluateNeedWebSearch(
        userPrompt: String,
        searchMode: String,
        localDataAvailable: Boolean = false
    ): Pair<Boolean, String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        when (searchMode.uppercase()) {
            "SEARCH_OFF" -> return@withContext Pair(false, "使用者設定停用 Web Search")
            "ALWAYS_SEARCH" -> return@withContext Pair(true, "使用者設定一律使用 Web Search")
            else -> { // SMART_SEARCH
                // 1. Explicit search / verify triggers
                val explicitSearchKeywords = listOf(
                    "搜尋", "查證", "網路查", "網路上", "verify", "search", "google", "查一下", "找一下"
                )
                if (explicitSearchKeywords.any { userPrompt.contains(it, ignoreCase = true) }) {
                    return@withContext Pair(true, "使用者明確要求網路檢索與查證")
                }

                // 2. Time-sensitive, current info, price, version, Steam queries
                val timeKeywords = listOf(
                    "目前", "最新", "現在", "今天", "最近", "近況", "2025", "2026",
                    "發售", "更新", "版本", "價格", "Steam", "折扣", "特價", "支援", "是否還有",
                    "latest", "current", "recent", "today", "release", "update", "version", "price"
                )
                if (timeKeywords.any { userPrompt.contains(it, ignoreCase = true) }) {
                    return@withContext Pair(true, "詢問即時最新動態、版本或價格特價資訊")
                }

                // 3. Basic history / common knowledge questions without time sensitivity
                val commonKnowledgePatterns = listOf(
                    "什麼是", "簡介", "介紹", "玩法", "主角是誰", "世界觀", "創作理念", "歷史地位"
                )
                val isBasicOverview = commonKnowledgePatterns.any { userPrompt.contains(it, ignoreCase = true) }
                if (isBasicOverview && localDataAvailable) {
                    return@withContext Pair(false, "本機館藏資料庫已有完整紀錄與基礎知識")
                }

                // 4. Smart AI Decision Prompt
                val systemPrompt = """
                    你是一個電玩館藏檢索決策器。請評估使用者詢問的內容是否需要發起網際網路搜尋。
                    需要搜尋 (needWebSearch = true)：
                    1. 詢問最新動態、目前版本的發售或更新狀態、Steam 特價、最新模擬器現況
                    2. 詢問具爭議性或冷門的確切價格、特定發售年月日、跨平台移植狀態
                    3. 需要外部權威網站查證的多來源資料
                    不需要搜尋 (needWebSearch = false)：
                    1. 經典遊戲的歷史常識、特色介紹、開發者故事或著名經典主題
                    2. 基礎遊戲玩法、控制方式與風格美學討論
                    
                    請嚴格僅回覆 JSON 格式，勿加 markdown 標記：
                    {"needWebSearch": true, "reason": "說明原因"}
                """.trimIndent()

                val request = AiRequest(
                    prompt = userPrompt,
                    systemPrompt = systemPrompt,
                    temperature = 0.1f,
                    maxTokens = com.example.agent.TokenBudgetManager.resolveOutputBudget("SEARCH_DECISION")
                )

                val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(AiFeature.RESEARCH, profilesProvider())
                val exec = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, request)

                if (exec.success && exec.text.isNotBlank()) {
                    try {
                        val jsonText = exec.text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").trim()
                        val obj = JSONObject(jsonText)
                        val need = obj.optBoolean("needWebSearch", false)
                        val reason = obj.optString("reason", "Smart Search 智能判定")
                        return@withContext Pair(need, reason)
                    } catch (_: Exception) {
                        Log.w(TAG, "Failed to parse Smart Search decision JSON: ${exec.text}")
                    }
                }

                return@withContext Pair(false, "無即時關鍵字，預設使用 AI 館長現有知識")
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Performs research with Smart / Always / Off search mode.
     */
    suspend fun researchCandidateData(
        game: com.example.database.GameEntity,
        missingFields: List<String>,
        searchMode: String,
        searchProvider: String,
        searchApiKey: String?,
        searchCustomEndpoint: String?
    ): Pair<com.example.database.GameEntity, String>? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        if (missingFields.isEmpty()) return@withContext null

        val userQuery = "請考據《${game.title}》(${game.platform}) 的以下缺失資訊：${missingFields.joinToString()}。"
        val researchRes = performResearch(
            gameTitle = game.title,
            platform = game.platform,
            userQuery = userQuery,
            searchMode = searchMode,
            searchProvider = searchProvider,
            searchApiKey = searchApiKey,
            searchCustomEndpoint = searchCustomEndpoint,
            localDescription = null
        )

        val systemPrompt = """
            你是一個資料解析與驗證器。請根據以下 Research 報告，提取出遊戲的 metadata。
            為了確保資料可信度，請為每個欄位提供 value、source 與 confidence (HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW)。
            必須只輸出合法的 JSON，不要加上 markdown 標記。
            範例格式：
            {
              "description": { "value": "文字介紹...", "source": "Wikipedia", "confidence": "HIGH" },
              "developer": { "value": "Nintendo", "source": "IGN", "confidence": "HIGH" },
              "publisher": { "value": "Nintendo", "source": "Official", "confidence": "HIGH" },
              "releaseDate": { "value": "1990-11-21", "source": "GameSpot", "confidence": "HIGH" },
              "genre": { "value": "Platformer", "source": "Wiki", "confidence": "HIGH" }
            }
            如果報告中找不到該欄位，請將該欄位填入 null。
        """.trimIndent()

        val request = AiRequest(
            prompt = "Research 報告與來源：\n${researchRes.answerText}\n來源：${researchRes.sources.map { it.url }}",
            systemPrompt = systemPrompt,
            temperature = 0.1f,
            maxTokens = com.example.agent.TokenBudgetManager.resolveOutputBudget("RESEARCH_CANDIDATE")
        )

        val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(AiFeature.RESEARCH, profilesProvider())
        val exec = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, request)

        if (exec.success && exec.text.isNotBlank()) {
            try {
                val cleanJson = exec.text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").trim()
                val json = org.json.JSONObject(cleanJson)
                
                // Helper to extract value only if confidence is HIGH and source is verified
                val knownSourceUrls = researchRes.sources.map { it.url.lowercase() }
                val knownSourceTitles = researchRes.sources.map { it.title.lowercase() }

                fun extractValidatedField(key: String): String? {
                    if (!json.has(key) || json.isNull(key)) return null
                    val fieldObj = json.optJSONObject(key) ?: return null
                    val conf = fieldObj.optString("confidence", "LOW").uppercase()
                    if (conf != "HIGH") return null
                    val value = fieldObj.optString("value", "").trim()
                    if (value.isBlank()) return null
                    
                    val source = fieldObj.optString("source", "").trim().lowercase()
                    // Strict validation: candidate source must match actual ResearchResult sources
                    val sourceValid = if (researchRes.sources.isNotEmpty()) {
                        source.isNotBlank() && (
                            knownSourceUrls.any { it.contains(source) || source.contains(it) } ||
                            knownSourceTitles.any { it.contains(source) || source.contains(it) }
                        )
                    } else {
                        // If Web Search was not used or yielded 0 sources, reject candidate external claims
                        false
                    }

                    return if (sourceValid) value else null
                }

                val candidateEntity = com.example.database.GameEntity(
                    id = game.id,
                    title = game.title,
                    platform = game.platform,
                    romPath = game.romPath,
                    description = extractValidatedField("description"),
                    developer = extractValidatedField("developer"),
                    publisher = extractValidatedField("publisher"),
                    releaseDate = extractValidatedField("releaseDate"),
                    genre = extractValidatedField("genre")
                )
                
                // Save provenance JSON to permanent app filesDir for persistent auditing
                try {
                    val provenanceDir = java.io.File(context.filesDir, "provenance")
                    if (!provenanceDir.exists()) provenanceDir.mkdirs()
                    val provenanceFile = java.io.File(provenanceDir, "provenance_${game.id}.json")
                    provenanceFile.writeText(cleanJson)
                } catch (e: Exception) { e.printStackTrace() }

                return@withContext Pair(candidateEntity, cleanJson)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
        return@withContext null
    }

    suspend fun performResearch(
        gameTitle: String,
        platform: String,
        userQuery: String,
        searchMode: String = "SMART_SEARCH",
        searchProvider: String = "DUCKDUCKGO",
        searchApiKey: String? = null,
        searchCustomEndpoint: String? = null,
        localDescription: String? = null,
        appContext: com.example.agent.CuratorContext? = null
    ): ResearchResult = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val overallResult = kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull(30000L) {
            val (needSearch, decisionReason) = evaluateNeedWebSearch(userQuery, searchMode, !localDescription.isNullOrBlank())
            var sources = emptyList<ResearchSource>()
            var searchFailedNotice: String? = null
            val pageContents = mutableListOf<String>()

            if (needSearch) {
                // Build exact name & region search query (Section 16)
                val cleanTitle = gameTitle.substringBefore("(").trim()
                val regionTag = if (gameTitle.contains("(")) gameTitle.substringAfter("(").substringBefore(")").trim() else ""
                val exactSearchTarget = buildString {
                    if (cleanTitle.isNotBlank()) {
                        append("\"$cleanTitle\" ")
                        if (regionTag.isNotBlank()) append("\"$regionTag\" ")
                        if (platform.isNotBlank()) append("$platform ")
                    }
                    append(userQuery)
                }.trim()

                val searchRes = searchService.search(exactSearchTarget, searchProvider, searchApiKey, searchCustomEndpoint)

                if (searchRes.sources.isNotEmpty()) {
                    // Rank sources by Credibility Tier (Tier 1 official > Tier 2 database > Tier 3 forum > Tier 4)
                    sources = searchRes.sources.sortedBy { it.tier }
                    
                    // Fetch top 3 sources' main page text with a strict timeout limit per page (Staged Budgeting)
                    var accumulatedTextLength = 0
                    val maxTotalPageLength = 3500
                    for (src in sources.take(3)) {
                        if (accumulatedTextLength >= maxTotalPageLength) break
                        try {
                            val pageText = kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull(4000L) {
                                WebPageFetcher.fetchMainText(src.url)
                            }
                            if (!pageText.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                val budgetRemaining = maxTotalPageLength - accumulatedTextLength
                                val trimmedPageText = pageText.take(budgetRemaining)
                                accumulatedTextLength += trimmedPageText.length
                                pageContents.add("【網頁內容: ${src.title} (${src.domain} / Tier ${src.tier})】\n$trimmedPageText")
                            }
                        } catch (e: Exception) {
                            Log.w(TAG, "WebPageFetcher error on ${src.url}: ${e.message}")
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    searchFailedNotice = searchRes.error ?: "未檢索到網路相關資料"
                }
            }

            // Build prompt for AI synthesis (Section 5, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15)
            val promptBuilder = StringBuilder()
            if (gameTitle.isNotBlank()) promptBuilder.append("【主題作品】: $gameTitle ($platform)\n")
            if (!localDescription.isNullOrBlank()) {
                promptBuilder.append("【本機館藏說明 (CSV / 官方紀錄)】: $localDescription\n")
            }
            if (appContext != null) {
                if (!appContext.currentScreen.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    promptBuilder.append("【使用者目前情境】: ${appContext.currentScreen}")
                    if (!appContext.currentMuseumTab.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        promptBuilder.append(" (分頁: ${appContext.currentMuseumTab})")
                    }
                    if (!appContext.lastAction.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        promptBuilder.append("，最近操作: ${appContext.lastAction} (目標: ${appContext.lastActionTarget ?: "無"})")
                    }
                    promptBuilder.append("\n")
                }
            }
            promptBuilder.append("【使用者問題】: $userQuery\n\n")

            if (sources.isNotEmpty()) {
                promptBuilder.append("【即時 Web 檢索參考資料 (已按可信度分級排序)】:\n")
                sources.forEachIndexed { idx, src ->
                    promptBuilder.append("[${idx + 1}] ${src.title} (${src.domain} / 可信度分級 Tier ${src.tier})\nURL: ${src.url}\n摘要: ${src.snippet}\n\n")
                }
                if (pageContents.isNotEmpty()) {
                    promptBuilder.append("【高排名來源網頁內文】:\n")
                    pageContents.forEach { promptBuilder.append(it).append("\n\n") }
                }
                
                promptBuilder.append("""
                    【解答與引用原則】：
                    1. 請綜合以上 Web 搜尋資料進行分析歸納，整理成結構清晰、人類易讀的解答。
                    2. 多來源交叉比對：若不同來源的發售日、版本或資料存在衝突，請明確告知玩家（例如：「目前找到的資料有些許差異，較可靠的來源顯示……」）。
                    3. 可信度優先：優先採用 Tier 1 (官方網站/商店) 與 Tier 2 (維基/權威遊戲資料庫) 的資訊。
                    4. 嚴禁捏造任何網址！末尾必須根據實際檢索到的 sources 列出 3 至 5 個最重要的參考來源。格式如下：
                       🌐 參考資料
                       1. 標題
                          URL
                       2. 標題
                          URL
                """.trimIndent())
            } else {
                if (needSearch) {
                    if (searchMode.equals("SEARCH_OFF", ignoreCase = true)) {
                        promptBuilder.append("【系統提示】: 目前已關閉網路搜尋，請依現有 AI 知識解答，並於解答開頭親切說明「（目前已關閉網路搜尋，以下為 AI 現有知識回答）」。\n")
                    } else {
                        promptBuilder.append("【系統提示】: 網路搜尋暫時無法取得結果 ($searchFailedNotice)。請根據現有知識解答，並於解答開頭說明「⚠ 目前網路搜尋暫時無法連線，以下回答為根據 AI 現有知識產出。」\n")
                    }
                } else {
                    promptBuilder.append("【系統提示】: 本題不需發起網路搜尋。請根據 AI 館長專業知識與本機館藏說明解答。切勿附帶任何網路參考資料連結。\n")
                }
            }

            val request = AiRequest(
                prompt = promptBuilder.toString(),
                systemPrompt = "你是一位極其專業、客觀考據的電玩博覽館長與遊戲研究員。請以繁體中文客觀詳細解答。",
                temperature = 0.6f,
                maxTokens = com.example.agent.TokenBudgetManager.resolveOutputBudget("RESEARCH")
            )

            val targetProfiles = resolver.resolve(AiFeature.RESEARCH, profilesProvider())
            val exec = rotationManager.executeWithFailover(targetProfiles, request)

            var answerText = if (exec.success && exec.text.isNotBlank()) {
                exec.text.trim()
            } else {
                "⚠ 目前無法完成 Research 檢索 (${exec.toHumanReadableError()})"
            }

            // Post-process: ensure non-searched responses do not contain fake URLs
            if (sources.isEmpty()) {
                answerText = answerText.replace(Regex("🌐 參考資料[\\s\\S]*"), "").trim()
            }

            val result = ResearchResult(
                gameTitle = gameTitle,
                platform = platform,
                answerText = answerText,
                sources = sources,
                usedWebSearch = sources.isNotEmpty(),
                searchReason = decisionReason,
                providerUsed = exec.provider,
                modelUsed = exec.model
            )

            if (gameTitle.isNotBlank() && platform.isNotBlank()) {
                saveResearchToFile(platform, gameTitle, result)
            }

            result
        }

        overallResult ?: ResearchResult(
            gameTitle = gameTitle,
            platform = platform,
            answerText = "⚠ 考據研究請求逾時（超過 30 秒），已中止以保護系統資源。",
            sources = emptyList(),
            usedWebSearch = false,
            searchReason = "OVERALL_TIMEOUT"
        )
    }

    private fun saveResearchToFile(platform: String, gameTitle: String, result: ResearchResult) {
        try {
            val safePlatform = platform.trim().replace(Regex("[/\\\\:*?\"<>|]"), "_").ifBlank { "unknown_platform" }
            val safeGame = gameTitle.trim().replace(Regex("[/\\\\:*?\"<>|]"), "_").ifBlank { "unknown_game" }
            val dir = File(context.filesDir, "research/$safePlatform")
            if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs()

            val file = File(dir, "$safeGame.json")
            val obj = JSONObject().apply {
                put("gameTitle", result.gameTitle)
                put("platform", result.platform)
                put("answerText", result.answerText)
                put("usedWebSearch", result.usedWebSearch)
                put("searchReason", result.searchReason)
                put("providerUsed", result.providerUsed)
                put("modelUsed", result.modelUsed)
                put("timestampMs", result.timestampMs)

                val srcArr = JSONArray()
                result.sources.forEach { s ->
                    val sObj = JSONObject().apply {
                        put("title", s.title)
                        put("url", s.url)
                        put("snippet", s.snippet)
                        put("domain", s.domain)
                        put("tier", s.tier)
                        s.publishedAt?.let { put("publishedAt", it) }
                    }
                    srcArr.put(sObj)
                }
                put("sources", srcArr)
            }

            file.writeText(obj.toString(2), Charsets.UTF_8)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to save research citations JSON: ${e.message}")
        }
    }
}

================================================================================

    // Non-ROM companion/metadata/system file extensions that should NEVER be scanned as games
    val nonRomExtensions: Set<String> = setOf(
        "txt", "nfo", "log", "json", "xml", "ini", "db", "tmp", "cache", "dat",
        "doc", "docx", "pdf", "rtf", "url", "desktop", "html", "htm", "css", "js",
        "jpg", "jpeg", "webp", "gif", "bmp", "ico", "svg", "mp3", "flac", "ogg", "wav",
        "srm", "sav", "state", "cht", "chtz", "bps", "ips", "ups", "diff", "patch"
    )

    fun isRomExtensionAllowed(canonicalPlatform: String, ext: String): Boolean {
        val lowerExt = ext.lowercase().trim().removePrefix(".")
        if (lowerExt.isBlank()) return false
        val canonicalUpper = canonicalPlatform.uppercase()
        val allowed = platformExtensions[canonicalUpper] ?: platformExtensions[canonicalPlatform]
        if (allowed != null && allowed.contains(lowerExt)) {
            return true
        }
        if (nonRomExtensions.contains(lowerExt)) return false
        return allowed == null
    }

    fun normalizePlatform(folderOrPlatform: String): String {
        val clean = folderOrPlatform.trim().lowercase()
        // Exact match
        canonicalMap[clean]?.let { return it }

        // Fuzzy match: check if any key is contained as a distinct token
        val tokens = clean.split(Regex("[^a-z0-9]+"))
        for (token in tokens) {
            canonicalMap[token]?.let { return it }
        }

        // Substring check for composite names like "Nintendo - Game Boy Advance"
        for ((key, value) in canonicalMap) {
            if (clean.contains(key)) {
                return value
            }
        }
        return folderOrPlatform.trim()
    }

    fun getAllowedExtensions(platform: String): Set<String> {
        val canonical = normalizePlatform(platform)
        return platformExtensions[canonical.uppercase()] ?: platformExtensions[canonical] ?: setOf(
            "gba", "gbc", "gb", "nds", "3ds", "cia", "nes", "sfc", "smc", "n64", "z64",
            "nsp", "xci", "iso", "chd", "cso", "pbp", "rvz", "wbfs", "vpk", "md", "smd",
            "gen", "sms", "gg", "pce", "ws", "wsc", "a26", "zip", "7z", "cue", "bin", "rom"
        )
    }

    fun getPlatformCsvCandidateFilenames(platform: String): List<String> {
        val primary = getCanonicalPlatformCsvFilename(platform)
        val canonical = normalizePlatform(platform).lowercase()
        val candidates = mutableListOf(primary)
        when (canonical) {
            "snes" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("sfc.csv", "super_famicom.csv", "super_nintendo.csv"))
            "nes" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("fc.csv", "famicom.csv"))
            "genesis" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("megadrive.csv", "md.csv", "sega_genesis.csv", "mega_drive.csv"))
            "sega32x" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("32x.csv", "sega_32x.csv"))
            "gamecube" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("gc.csv", "ngc.csv"))
            "wii u", "wiiu" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("wii_u.csv", "wii u.csv"))
            "ps1" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("psx.csv", "playstation.csv", "playstation1.csv", "playstation 1.csv"))
            "ps2" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("playstation2.csv", "playstation 2.csv"))
            "ps3" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("playstation3.csv", "playstation 3.csv"))
            "psp" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("playstation portable.csv", "playstation_portable.csv"))
            "psvita" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("vita.csv", "ps_vita.csv"))
            "dreamcast" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("dc.csv", "sega_dreamcast.csv"))
            "saturn" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("ss.csv", "sega_saturn.csv"))
            "mastersystem" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("sms.csv", "master_system.csv"))
            "gamegear" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("gg.csv", "game_gear.csv"))
            "pcengine" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("pce.csv", "tg16.csv", "turbografx16.csv", "turbografx.csv"))
            "atari2600" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("a2600.csv", "atari_2600.csv", "atari 2600.csv"))
            "atari5200" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("a5200.csv", "atari_5200.csv"))
            "atari7800" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("a7800.csv", "atari_7800.csv"))
            "atarijaguar" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("jaguar.csv", "atari_jaguar.csv"))
            "atarilynx" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("lynx.csv", "atari_lynx.csv"))
            "arcade" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("mame.csv", "fba.csv", "fbneo.csv"))
            "neogeo" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("neogeo.csv", "neo_geo.csv"))
            "ngp" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("ngpc.csv", "neo_geo_pocket.csv"))
            "cdi" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("cd-i.csv", "philips_cdi.csv"))
            "cps1" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("cps.csv", "cps-1.csv"))
            "cps2" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("cps-2.csv"))
            "cps3" -> candidates.addAll(listOf("cps-3.csv"))
        }
        return candidates.distinct()
    }

    fun guessPlatformFromExtensionAndPath(ext: String, parentFolderNames: List<String>): String {
        val cleanExt = ext.lowercase().trim().removePrefix(".")

        // Early reject: Non-ROM companion / metadata / image / document extensions should NEVER be guessed as game platforms
        if (cleanExt.isBlank() || nonRomExtensions.contains(cleanExt)) {
            val isPico8Folder = parentFolderNames.any { 
                val norm = normalizePlatform(it)
                norm.equals("PICO8", ignoreCase = true)
            }
            if (cleanExt == "png" && isPico8Folder) {
                return "PICO8"
            }
            return "General"
        }

        // 1. Path hierarchy check FIRST: test folder names from immediate parent upwards
        for (folder in parentFolderNames.reversed()) {
            val trimmedFolder = folder.trim()
            val norm = normalizePlatform(trimmedFolder)
            val isKnown = canonicalMap.containsKey(trimmedFolder.lowercase()) ||
                          canonicalMap.values.any { it.equals(norm, ignoreCase = true) }
            if (isKnown) {
                if (isRomExtensionAllowed(norm, cleanExt)) {
                    return norm
                }
                // General container / multiplatform extensions inside a named platform folder
                if (cleanExt in setOf("zip", "7z", "bin", "iso", "cue", "chd", "img", "cso", "pbp", "m3u", "rom")) {
                    return norm
                }
                // Disallowed or non-matching extensions in this folder do NOT get matched to norm
            }
        }

        // 2. Unambiguous unique extension check (e.g. gba, nds, sfc, z64, nsp, xci, p8, vpk, etc.)
        extensionToPlatform[cleanExt]?.let { return it }

        // 3. Fallbacks by extension when no folder was matched
        return when (cleanExt) {
            "rvz", "gcm", "gcz" -> "GAMECUBE"
            "wbfs" -> "WII"
            "wux", "rpx", "wud" -> "WIIU"
            "cso" -> "PSP"
            "pbp" -> "PS1"
            "vpk" -> "PSVITA"
            "cdi" -> "CDI"
            "gdi" -> "DREAMCAST"
            "chd", "cue", "bin", "iso" -> "PS1"
            "zip", "7z" -> "ARCADE"
            else -> "General"
        }
    }
}

class RomScanner(private val context: Context) {

    /**
     * Discover platform subfolders within a ROM source root.
     */
    suspend fun discoverPlatforms(source: RomSource): List<String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val uri = Uri.parse(source.treeUri)
        val rootDoc = try {
            DocumentFile.fromTreeUri(context, uri)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        } ?: return@withContext emptyList()

        val platforms = mutableSetOf<String>()
        val children = rootDoc.listFiles()

        for (child in children) {
            val name = child.name ?: continue
            if (name.startsWith(".")) continue

            if (child.isDirectory) {
                platforms.add(PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(name))
            } else {
                // If files are directly in root, check their extensions
                val ext = name.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase()
                val isPico8Png = name.lowercase().endsWith(".p8.png") || (ext == "png" && rootDoc.name?.contains("pico", ignoreCase = true) == true)
                if (ext.isNotBlank() && (!PlatformRegistry.nonRomExtensions.contains(ext) || isPico8Png)) {
                    val effectiveExt = if (isPico8Png) "p8" else ext
                    val plat = PlatformRegistry.guessPlatformFromExtensionAndPath(effectiveExt, listOf(rootDoc.name.orEmpty()))
                    if (PlatformRegistry.isRomExtensionAllowed(plat, effectiveExt)) {
                        platforms.add(plat)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        platforms.filter { it.isNotBlank() }.sorted()
    }

    /**
     * Scan a ROM Source recursively (up to 4 levels deep), with intelligent platform deduction and duplicate track filtering.
     */
    suspend fun scanSource(
        source: RomSource,
        platformFilter: String? = null,
        onProgress: (current: Int, total: Int, item: String) -> Unit = { _, _, _ -> }
    ): List<GameEntity> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val uri = Uri.parse(source.treeUri)
        val rootDoc = try {
            DocumentFile.fromTreeUri(context, uri)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            null
        } ?: return@withContext emptyList()

        val discoveredFiles = mutableListOf<ScannedFileItem>()
        val rootFolderName = rootDoc.name ?: "ROMs"

        // Recursively traverse directory tree up to 4 levels deep
        traverseDirectory(rootDoc, listOf(rootFolderName), 0, 4, discoveredFiles)

        val results = mutableListOf<GameEntity>()
        val total = discoveredFiles.size

        // Group files by parent directory to de-duplicate CUE/BIN pairs and CD audio tracks
        val seenSignatures = mutableSetOf<String>()

        for ((index, item) in discoveredFiles.withIndex()) {
            val file = item.docFile
            val filename = file.name ?: continue
            val ext = filename.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase()
            val isPico8Png = filename.lowercase().endsWith(".p8.png") || (ext == "png" && item.pathSegments.any { it.contains("pico", ignoreCase = true) })

            // Skip hidden files, companion files, non-ROM extensions (allowing PICO-8 cartridges)
            if (filename.startsWith(".") || (PlatformRegistry.nonRomExtensions.contains(ext) && !isPico8Png) || ext.isBlank()) {
                continue
            }

            // Skip CD audio tracks (e.g. "Game (Track 2).bin", "Game_track02.bin") if cue/chd exists
            if (isAudioTrack(filename)) {
                continue
            }

            // Deduce platform
            val guessedPlat = if (isPico8Png) "PICO8" else PlatformRegistry.guessPlatformFromExtensionAndPath(ext, item.pathSegments)
            val canonicalPlatform = PlatformRegistry.normalizePlatform(guessedPlat)

            // Strict Platform-specific ROM extension whitelist verification
            if (!isPico8Png && !PlatformRegistry.isRomExtensionAllowed(canonicalPlatform, ext)) {
                continue
            }

            // If platform filter is active, filter accordingly
            if (platformFilter != null) {
                val matchesFilter = canonicalPlatform.equals(platformFilter, ignoreCase = true) ||
                        item.pathSegments.any { it.equals(platformFilter, ignoreCase = true) }
                if (!matchesFilter) continue
            }

            val originalTitle = filename.substringBeforeLast('.')
            val cleanTitle = cleanGameTitle(filename)
            val dedupeKey = "$canonicalPlatform::$originalTitle"

            if (seenSignatures.contains(dedupeKey)) {
                // If duplicate title in same platform (e.g. game.cue and game.bin), prefer .cue or .chd or .iso
                if (ext == "cue" || ext == "chd" || ext == "m3u" || ext == "rvz") {
                    results.removeAll { it.platform == canonicalPlatform && it.title.equals(originalTitle, ignoreCase = true) }
                } else {
                    continue
                }
            }
            seenSignatures.add(dedupeKey)

            onProgress(index + 1, total, originalTitle)

            results.add(
                GameEntity(
                    title = originalTitle,
                    platform = canonicalPlatform,
                    romPath = file.uri.toString(),
                    fileName = file.name,
                    filePath = file.uri.path,
                    aiDetectedTitle = cleanTitle,
                    sourceId = source.id
                )
            )
        }

        results
    }

    private fun traverseDirectory(
        directory: DocumentFile,
        currentPath: List<String>,
        currentDepth: Int,
        maxDepth: Int,
        outList: MutableList<ScannedFileItem>
    ) {
        if (currentDepth > maxDepth) return
        val children = try {
            directory.listFiles()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            emptyArray()
        }

        for (child in children) {
            val name = child.name ?: continue
            if (name.startsWith(".")) continue

            if (child.isDirectory) {
                traverseDirectory(child, currentPath + name, currentDepth + 1, maxDepth, outList)
            } else {
                outList.add(ScannedFileItem(child, currentPath))
            }
        }
    }

    private fun isAudioTrack(filename: String): Boolean {
        val lower = filename.lowercase()
        return lower.contains(Regex("""\(track\s*[2-9]\d*\)""")) ||
                lower.contains(Regex("""\[track\s*[2-9]\d*\]""")) ||
                lower.contains(Regex("""_track\s*[2-9]\d*""")) ||
                lower.contains(Regex("""\s+track\s+[2-9]\d*"""))
    }

    private fun cleanGameTitle(filename: String): String {
        val nameWithoutExt = filename.substringBeforeLast('.')
        // Remove standard release tags: (USA), [!], (v1.0), (Disc 1), (En,Ja), (Japan), (Beta), etc.
        val cleaned = nameWithoutExt
            .replace(Regex("\\(.*?\\)"), "")
            .replace(Regex("\\[.*?\\]"), "")
            .replace('_', ' ')
            .replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ")
            .trim()
        return if (cleaned.isNotBlank()) cleaned else nameWithoutExt
    }

    data class ScannedFileItem(
        val docFile: DocumentFile,
        val pathSegments: List<String>
    )
}

package com.example.settings

import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import com.example.UserProfile
import com.example.api.ApiKeyEntry
import com.example.api.ApiProfile
import com.example.scanner.RomSource
import com.example.ui.theme.FontSize
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

class SettingsRepository(context: Context) {
    private val prefs: SharedPreferences = context.getSharedPreferences("sylphora_settings_v1", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    // --- ROM Sources ---
    fun getRomSources(): List<RomSource> {
        val jsonStr = prefs.getString("rom_sources", null) ?: return emptyList()
        return try {
            val array = JSONArray(jsonStr)
            val list = mutableListOf<RomSource>()
            for (i in 0 until array.length()) {
                list.add(RomSource.fromJson(array.getJSONObject(i)))
            }
            list
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    fun saveRomSources(sources: List<RomSource>) {
        val array = JSONArray()
        sources.forEach { array.put(it.toJson()) }
        prefs.edit().putString("rom_sources", array.toString()).apply()
    }

    fun addRomSource(source: RomSource) {
        val current = getRomSources().toMutableList()
        current.removeAll { it.id == source.id || it.treeUri == source.treeUri }
        current.add(source)
        saveRomSources(current)
    }

    fun removeRomSource(sourceId: String) {
        val current = getRomSources().filterNot { it.id == sourceId }
        saveRomSources(current)
    }

    fun toggleRomSource(sourceId: String, enabled: Boolean) {
        val current = getRomSources().map {
            if (it.id == sourceId) it.copy(enabled = enabled) else it
        }
        saveRomSources(current)
    }

    // --- Asset & Description Root ---
    fun getAssetRootUri(): String? = prefs.getString("asset_root_uri", null)
    fun saveAssetRootUri(uri: String?) {
        prefs.edit().putString("asset_root_uri", uri).apply()
    }

    fun getDescriptionRootUri(): String? = prefs.getString("description_root_uri", null)
    fun saveDescriptionRootUri(uri: String?) {
        prefs.edit().putString("description_root_uri", uri).apply()
    }

    // --- Theme & Appearance ---
    fun getThemeFamilyIndex(): Int = prefs.getInt("theme_family_index", 0)
    fun isDarkTheme(): Boolean = prefs.getBoolean("is_dark_theme", true)
    fun getLayoutModeName(): String = prefs.getString("layout_mode", "PEGASUS_GRID") ?: "PEGASUS_GRID"
    fun getCustomWallpaperUri(themeFamilyIndex: Int = getThemeFamilyIndex()): String? {
        return prefs.getString("custom_wallpaper_uri_theme_$themeFamilyIndex", null)
            ?: prefs.getString("custom_wallpaper_uri", null) // Fallback to old global setting
    }
    fun getGlassOpacity(): Float = prefs.getFloat("glass_opacity", 0.85f)
    fun getCrtEnabled(): Boolean = prefs.getBoolean("crt_enabled", false)
    fun isCrtEnabled(): Boolean = getCrtEnabled()
    fun saveCrtEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean("crt_enabled", enabled).apply()
    }

    fun getFontSize(): FontSize = runCatching {
        FontSize.valueOf(prefs.getString("font_size", FontSize.MEDIUM.name) ?: FontSize.MEDIUM.name)
    }.getOrDefault(FontSize.MEDIUM)
    fun saveFontSize(size: FontSize) {
        prefs.edit().putString("font_size", size.name).apply()
    }

    fun saveThemePreferences(familyIndex: Int, isDark: Boolean, layoutModeName: String, wallpaperUri: String?, opacity: Float) {
        prefs.edit()
            .putInt("theme_family_index", familyIndex)
            .putBoolean("is_dark_theme", isDark)
            .putString("layout_mode", layoutModeName)
            .putString("custom_wallpaper_uri_theme_$familyIndex", wallpaperUri)
            .putFloat("glass_opacity", opacity)
            .apply()
    }

    // --- Audio Settings ---
    fun isSfxEnabled(): Boolean = prefs.getBoolean("is_sfx_enabled", true)
    fun isBgmEnabled(): Boolean = prefs.getBoolean("is_bgm_enabled", true)
    fun getBgmVolume(): Float = prefs.getFloat("bgm_volume", 0.8f)

    fun saveAudioSettings(sfx: Boolean, bgm: Boolean, volume: Float) {
        prefs.edit()
            .putBoolean("is_sfx_enabled", sfx)
            .putBoolean("is_bgm_enabled", bgm)
            .putFloat("bgm_volume", volume)
            .apply()
    }

    // --- Legacy API Keys / General Keys ---
    fun getSgdbKey(): String = prefs.getString("sgdb_key", "") ?: ""
    fun getGeminiKey(): String = prefs.getString("gemini_key", "") ?: ""
    fun getCuratorPersonality(): String = prefs.getString("curator_personality", "") ?: ""

    fun saveApiKeys(sgdbKey: String, geminiKey: String, personality: String) {
        prefs.edit()
            .putString("sgdb_key", sgdbKey)
            .putString("gemini_key", geminiKey)
            .putString("curator_personality", personality)
            .apply()
    }

    // --- User Profile ---
    fun getUserProfile(): UserProfile {
        return UserProfile(
            username = prefs.getString("user_username", "旅行者") ?: "旅行者",
            title = prefs.getString("user_title", "傳奇收藏家") ?: "傳奇收藏家",
            avatarType = prefs.getString("user_avatar_type", "PRESET_SYLPHORA") ?: "PRESET_SYLPHORA",
            customAvatarUri = prefs.getString("user_custom_avatar_uri", null)
        )
    }

    fun saveUserProfile(profile: UserProfile) {
        prefs.edit()
            .putString("user_username", profile.username)
            .putString("user_title", profile.title)
            .putString("user_avatar_type", profile.avatarType)
            .putString("user_custom_avatar_uri", profile.customAvatarUri)
            .apply()
    }

    // --- Custom Collections ---
    fun getCustomCollections(): List<String> {
        val jsonStr = prefs.getString("custom_collections", null)
        if (jsonStr == null) return listOf("我的最愛", "RPG經典", "動作熱血")
        return try {
            val array = JSONArray(jsonStr)
            val list = mutableListOf<String>()
            for (i in 0 until array.length()) list.add(array.getString(i))
            if (list.isEmpty()) listOf("我的最愛", "RPG經典", "動作熱血") else list
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            listOf("我的最愛", "RPG經典", "動作熱血")
        }
    }

    fun saveCustomCollections(collections: List<String>) {
        val array = JSONArray()
        collections.forEach { array.put(it) }
        prefs.edit().putString("custom_collections", array.toString()).apply()
    }

    // --- API Profiles ---
    fun getApiProfiles(): List<ApiProfile> {
        val jsonStr = prefs.getString("api_profiles", null)
        if (jsonStr == null) {
            return listOf(
                ApiProfile(id = "default_gemini", displayName = "Gemini (預設)", provider = "GEMINI", model = "")
            )
        }
        return try {
            val array = JSONArray(jsonStr)
            val list = mutableListOf<ApiProfile>()
            for (i in 0 until array.length()) {
                val obj = array.getJSONObject(i)
                val keyList = mutableListOf<ApiKeyEntry>()
                val keysArr = obj.optJSONArray("apiKeys")
                if (keysArr != null) {
                    for (k in 0 until keysArr.length()) {
                        val kObj = keysArr.getJSONObject(k)
                        val rawKey = kObj.optString("key", "")
                        keyList.add(
                            ApiKeyEntry(
                                id = kObj.optString("id", java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString()),
                                key = com.example.api.SecureKeyStoreHelper.decrypt(rawKey),
                                name = kObj.optString("name", "Key #${k + 1}"),
                                enabled = kObj.optBoolean("enabled", true),
                                priority = kObj.optInt("priority", 1)
                            )
                        )
                    }
                }
                val rawSingleKey = if (obj.has("apiKey") && !obj.isNull("apiKey")) obj.getString("apiKey") else null
                list.add(
                    ApiProfile(
                        id = obj.optString("id", java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString()),
                        provider = obj.optString("provider", "GEMINI"),
                        displayName = obj.optString("displayName", "AI Profile"),
                        model = obj.optString("model", ""),
                        baseUrl = if (obj.has("baseUrl") && !obj.isNull("baseUrl")) obj.getString("baseUrl") else null,
                        apiKey = rawSingleKey?.let { com.example.api.SecureKeyStoreHelper.decrypt(it) },
                        apiKeys = keyList,
                        accountId = if (obj.has("accountId") && !obj.isNull("accountId")) obj.getString("accountId") else null,
                        systemPrompt = if (obj.has("systemPrompt") && !obj.isNull("systemPrompt")) obj.getString("systemPrompt") else null,
                        enabled = obj.optBoolean("enabled", true),
                        priority = obj.optInt("priority", 1)
                    )
                )
            }
            if (list.isEmpty()) {
                listOf(ApiProfile(id = "default_gemini", displayName = "Gemini (預設)", provider = "GEMINI", model = ""))
            } else list
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            listOf(ApiProfile(id = "default_gemini", displayName = "Gemini (預設)", provider = "GEMINI", model = ""))
        }
    }

    fun saveApiProfiles(profiles: List<ApiProfile>) {
        val array = JSONArray()
        profiles.forEach { p ->
            val obj = JSONObject().apply {
                put("id", p.id)
                put("provider", p.provider)
                put("displayName", p.displayName)
                put("model", p.model)
                put("enabled", p.enabled)
                put("priority", p.priority)
                p.baseUrl?.let { put("baseUrl", it) }
                p.apiKey?.let { put("apiKey", com.example.api.SecureKeyStoreHelper.encrypt(it)) }
                p.accountId?.let { put("accountId", it) }
                p.systemPrompt?.let { put("systemPrompt", it) }

                if (p.apiKeys.isNotEmpty()) {
                    val kArr = JSONArray()
                    p.apiKeys.forEach { k ->
                        val kObj = JSONObject().apply {
                            put("id", k.id)
                            put("key", com.example.api.SecureKeyStoreHelper.encrypt(k.key))
                            put("name", k.name)
                            put("enabled", k.enabled)
                            put("priority", k.priority)
                        }
                        kArr.put(kObj)
                    }
                    put("apiKeys", kArr)
                }
            }
            array.put(obj)
        }
        prefs.edit().putString("api_profiles", array.toString()).apply()
    }

    fun getDefaultApiProfileId(): String? = prefs.getString("default_api_profile_id", "default_gemini")

    // --- Emulator & Core Overrides ---
    fun getEmulatorMode(platform: String): String {

    fun saveEmulatorMode(platform: String, mode: String) {

    }

    fun getEmulatorOverride(platform: String): String? {
        return prefs.getString("emu_override_${platform.uppercase()}", null)
    }

    fun saveEmulatorOverride(platform: String, launcherId: String?) {
        if (launcherId == null) {
            prefs.edit().remove("emu_override_${platform.uppercase()}").apply()
        } else {
            prefs.edit().putString("emu_override_${platform.uppercase()}", launcherId).apply()
        }
    }

    fun getCoreOverride(platform: String): String? {
        return prefs.getString("core_override_${platform.uppercase()}", null)
    }

    fun saveCoreOverride(platform: String, coreFileName: String?) {
        if (coreFileName == null) {
            prefs.edit().remove("core_override_${platform.uppercase()}").apply()
        } else {
            prefs.edit().putString("core_override_${platform.uppercase()}", coreFileName).apply()
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun CuratorScreen(viewModel: GameViewModel) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext.current
    var activeSubTab by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    val tabs = listOf("遊戲顧問", "隨機推薦", "情境推薦", "收藏庫統計")

    LaunchedEffect(activeSubTab) {
        viewModel.updateCuratorNavigation("CURATOR", tabs.getOrNull(activeSubTab))
    }

    Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        ThemeCuratorHeader(
            theme = theme,
            themeIndex = viewModel.currentThemeFamilyIndex.collectAsState().value
        )

        // Sub Navigation Capsules
        LazyRow(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            items(tabs.size, key = { "tab_${it}_${tabs[it]}" }) { index ->
                val isSelected = index == activeSubTab
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .background(if (isSelected) theme.accent else theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                        .border(1.dp, if (isSelected) theme.accent else theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.8f), RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .clickable { activeSubTab = index }
                        .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 10.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = tabs[index],
                        color = if (isSelected) theme.background else theme.textMain,
                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                        fontSize = 14.sp
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Active View
        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f)) {
            when (activeSubTab) {
                0 -> CuratorChatView(viewModel = viewModel)
                1 -> FateDrawView(viewModel = viewModel)
                2 -> MoodRecommendationView(viewModel = viewModel)
                3 -> ArchaeologyHealthView(viewModel = viewModel)
            }
        }
    }
}


enum class CuratorState {
    IDLE,
    THINKING,
    SPEAKING,
    ERROR
}

@Composable
fun SilphyetteAvatar(
    state: CuratorState,
    theme: com.example.ui.theme.PegasusTheme,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    isWhiteHair: Boolean = true
) {
    val infiniteTransition = androidx.compose.animation.core.rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "silphyette")
    val breathScale by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 1f,
        targetValue = 1.04f,
        animationSpec = androidx.compose.animation.core.infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = androidx.compose.animation.core.tween(1200, easing = androidx.compose.animation.core.FastOutLinearInEasing),
            repeatMode = androidx.compose.animation.core.RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "silphyette_breath"
    )
    val blink by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 1f,
        targetValue = 0f,
        animationSpec = androidx.compose.animation.core.infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = androidx.compose.animation.core.keyframes {
                durationMillis = 2500
                1f at 0
                1f at 2250
                0f at 2350
                1f at 2450
            }
        ),
        label = "silphyette_blink"
    )
    val mouthOpen by androidx.compose.animation.core.animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (state == CuratorState.SPEAKING) 1f else 0f,
        animationSpec = androidx.compose.animation.core.tween(200, easing = androidx.compose.animation.core.FastOutLinearInEasing),
        label = "silphyette_mouth"
    )
    val thinkingLook by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = -1f,
        targetValue = 1f,
        animationSpec = androidx.compose.animation.core.infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = androidx.compose.animation.core.tween(800, easing = androidx.compose.animation.core.FastOutLinearInEasing),
            repeatMode = androidx.compose.animation.core.RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "silphyette_thinking_look"
    )

    Canvas(modifier = modifier.size(80.dp).scale(breathScale)) {
        val w = size.width
        val h = size.height
        val cx = w / 2f
        val cy = h / 2f
        val face = w * 0.55f
        val hair = if (isWhiteHair) Color(0xFFF0F4F8) else Color(0xFF8BA88A)
        val eye = Color(0xFFE63E2E)
        drawCircle(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.35f), radius = w / 2f)
        drawCircle(
            color = when (state) {
                CuratorState.THINKING -> theme.tertiary
                CuratorState.ERROR -> Color(0xFFEF4444)
                else -> theme.accent
            },
            radius = w / 2f - 1.dp.toPx(),
            style = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.drawscope.Stroke(width = 2.dp.toPx())
        )
        drawRoundRect(
            color = Color(0xFFFFE0BD),
            topLeft = Offset(cx - face / 2f, cy - face / 2f * 0.9f),
            size = androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Size(face, face * 0.85f),
            cornerRadius = androidx.compose.ui.geometry.CornerRadius(face * 0.3f)
        )
        val ear = face * 0.2f
        drawRoundRect(Color(0xFFFFDAB9), Offset(cx - face * 0.6f, cy - face * 0.2f), androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Size(ear, ear * 1.8f), androidx.compose.ui.geometry.CornerRadius(ear * 0.5f))
        drawRoundRect(Color(0xFFFFDAB9), Offset(cx + face * 0.4f, cy - face * 0.2f), androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Size(ear, ear * 1.8f), androidx.compose.ui.geometry.CornerRadius(ear * 0.5f))
        val hairPath = Path().apply {
            moveTo(cx - face * 0.6f, cy - face * 0.3f)
            cubicTo(cx - face * 0.4f, cy - face * 0.9f, cx + face * 0.4f, cy - face * 0.9f, cx + face * 0.6f, cy - face * 0.3f)
            lineTo(cx + face * 0.5f, cy - face * 0.05f)
            lineTo(cx - face * 0.5f, cy - face * 0.05f)
            close()
        }
        drawPath(hairPath, hair)
        drawLine(hair, Offset(cx, cy - face * 0.45f), Offset(cx + face * 0.15f, cy - face * 0.7f), strokeWidth = 4f)
        val eyeY = cy - face * 0.05f + if (state == CuratorState.THINKING) thinkingLook * face * 0.04f else 0f
        val spacing = face * 0.25f
        val eyeSize = face * 0.12f
        if (blink > 0.5f) {
            listOf(-spacing, spacing).forEach { dx ->
                drawCircle(Color.White, eyeSize, Offset(cx + dx, eyeY))
                drawCircle(eye, eyeSize * 0.5f, Offset(cx + dx, eyeY))
                drawCircle(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.7f), eyeSize * 0.15f, Offset(cx + dx - eyeSize * 0.2f, eyeY - eyeSize * 0.2f))
            }
        } else {
            listOf(-spacing, spacing).forEach { dx -> drawLine(eye, Offset(cx + dx - eyeSize, eyeY), Offset(cx + dx + eyeSize, eyeY), strokeWidth = 2.5f) }
        }
        val mouthY = cy + face * 0.2f
        val mouthWidth = face * 0.2f
        if (mouthOpen > 0.1f) {
            drawOval(Color(0xFFD4735E), Offset(cx - mouthWidth / 2f, mouthY - mouthWidth * 0.3f * mouthOpen), androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Size(mouthWidth, mouthWidth * 0.6f * mouthOpen))
        } else {
            drawArc(Color(0xFFD4735E), 180f, 180f, false, Offset(cx - mouthWidth / 2f, mouthY - mouthWidth * 0.1f), androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Size(mouthWidth, mouthWidth * 0.3f), style = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.drawscope.Stroke(width = 2f))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun CuratorChatView(viewModel: GameViewModel) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val fontScale by viewModel.fontSize.collectAsState()
    val chatMessages by viewModel.chatMessages.collectAsState()
    val isAgentTyping by viewModel.isAgentTyping.collectAsState()
    var curatorState by remember { mutableStateOf(CuratorState.IDLE) }
    var inputMessage by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    val listState = rememberLazyListState()
    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val density = LocalDensity.current
    val imeBottom = with(density) { WindowInsets.ime.getBottom(this).toDp() }
    val isImeVisible = WindowInsets.ime.isImeVisible

    LaunchedEffect(chatMessages.size, isAgentTyping) {
        curatorState = if (isAgentTyping) CuratorState.THINKING else if (chatMessages.lastOrNull()?.sender == "curator") CuratorState.SPEAKING else CuratorState.IDLE
        if (!isAgentTyping && chatMessages.lastOrNull()?.sender == "curator") {
            delay(600)
            curatorState = CuratorState.IDLE
        }
        if (chatMessages.isNotEmpty()) {
            listState.animateScrollToItem(chatMessages.lastIndex)
        }
    }

    val lastCuratorMessage = chatMessages.lastOrNull { it.sender == "curator" }?.content ?: ""
    val isAskingToFill = lastCuratorMessage.contains("需要我為您考據") || lastCuratorMessage.contains("需要我為您補充")
    
    val quickPrompts = if (isAskingToFill) {
        listOf("✅ 開始考據", "❌ 先不要")
    } else {
        listOf(
            "📊 分析我目前的遊戲庫健康度",
            "☕ 推薦一款 20 分鐘能通關/暢玩的遊戲",
            "🎮 今晚適合玩什麼？幫我挑一款經典！",
            "🔍 幫我列出缺少簡介的遊戲清單"
        )
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
            .padding(bottom = if (isImeVisible) imeBottom else 0.dp)
    ) {
        // Chat History
        LazyColumn(
            state = listState,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).fillMaxWidth(),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 12.dp)
        ) {
            items(chatMessages, key = { "${it.sender}_${it.timestamp}_${it.content.hashCode()}" }) { msg ->
                val isUser = msg.sender == "user"
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = if (isUser) Arrangement.End else Arrangement.Start
                ) {
                    if (!isUser) {
                        val avatarSize = if (LocalConfiguration.current.screenWidthDp > 600) 100.dp else 80.dp
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier.size(avatarSize),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            SilphyetteAvatar(
                                state = curatorState,
                                theme = theme,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(80.dp).then(
                                    if (LocalConfiguration.current.screenWidthDp > 600) Modifier.size(100.dp) else Modifier
                                )
                            )
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                    }

                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .widthIn(max = 720.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                            .background(if (isUser) theme.accent else theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                            .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f), RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                            .padding(18.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = msg.content,
                            color = if (isUser) theme.background else theme.textMain,
                            fontSize = 16.sp * fontScale.scale,
                            lineHeight = 24.sp * fontScale.scale
                        )
                    }

                    if (isUser) {
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(36.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(theme.surface),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.Person, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.textMain, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if (isAgentTyping) {
                item {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                        Text("正在思考...", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp * fontScale.scale)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Quick Suggestion Chips
        LazyRow(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 8.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            items(quickPrompts, key = { it }) { prompt ->
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.4f))
                        .border(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.3f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .clickable {
                            viewModel.sendCuratorChat(prompt)
                            coroutineScope.launch {
                                listState.animateScrollToItem(chatMessages.size)
                            }
                        }
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(prompt, color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
                }
            }
        }

        // Input Box
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(bottom = 8.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
        ) {
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = inputMessage,
                onValueChange = { inputMessage = it },
                placeholder = { Text("搜尋或詢問任何遊戲相關內容...", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 14.sp * fontScale.scale) },
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
                colors = TextFieldDefaults.colors(
                    focusedTextColor = theme.textMain,
                    unfocusedTextColor = theme.textMain,
                    focusedContainerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
                    unfocusedContainerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.4f),
                    focusedIndicatorColor = theme.accent,
                    unfocusedIndicatorColor = Color.Transparent
                ),
                singleLine = true
            )

            SylphoraIconButton(
                onClick = {
                    if (inputMessage.isNotBlank()) {
                        val txt = inputMessage
                        inputMessage = ""
                        viewModel.sendCuratorChat(txt)
                        coroutineScope.launch {
                            listState.animateScrollToItem(chatMessages.size)
                        }
                    }
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(48.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(theme.accent)
            ) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.Send, contentDescription = "發送", tint = theme.background)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun FateDrawView(viewModel: GameViewModel) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val fateGame by viewModel.fateGame.collectAsState()

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(24.dp),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Column(
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            modifier = Modifier
                .widthIn(max = 500.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
                .background(Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(theme.surface, theme.background)))
                .border(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
                .padding(32.dp)
        ) {
            Text("隨機推薦 · 今晚玩什麼", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
            Text("隨機為你挑選今晚推薦遊玩的經典作品", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))

            val currentFate = fateGame
            if (currentFate != null) {
                val game = currentFate
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .background(theme.cardStart)
                        .padding(20.dp),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                        Text("🎮", fontSize = 48.sp)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        Text(game.title, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                                .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(game.platform, color = theme.accent, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = game.description ?: "經典之選，立刻開啟你的冒險！",
                            color = theme.textSecondary,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            maxLines = 3
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                        SylphoraButton(
                            onClick = { viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context) },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayArrow, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.background)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text("立刻啟動遊戲", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .height(180.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                        .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Text("點擊下方按鈕開始抽取推薦遊戲", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 15.sp)
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))

            SylphoraButton(
                onClick = { viewModel.drawFate() },
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(50.dp),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(25.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.Shuffle, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.background)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Text(if (fateGame == null) "隨機推薦一款遊戲" else "再抽一次", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 16.sp)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun MoodRecommendationView(viewModel: GameViewModel) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var selectedMood by remember { mutableStateOf("relax") }
    val moods = listOf(
        "relax" to "☕ 輕鬆休閒",
        "action" to "🔥 熱血動作",
        "story" to "📖 深度劇情",
        "arcade" to "🕹️ 經典街機"
    )

    val recommendedGames = remember(selectedMood, viewModel.allGames.collectAsState().value) {
        viewModel.recommendByMood(selectedMood)
    }

    Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(24.dp)) {
        Text("依心情與情境推薦", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
            moods.forEach { (key, label) ->
                val isSelected = key == selectedMood
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(if (isSelected) theme.accent else theme.surface)
                        .clickable { selectedMood = key }
                        .padding(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 10.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = label,
                        color = if (isSelected) theme.background else theme.textMain,
                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                        fontSize = 14.sp
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(20.dp))

        LazyColumn(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
            items(recommendedGames, key = { "${it.id}_${it.romPath}" }) { game ->
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                        .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .padding(16.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
                ) {
                    Text("🎮", fontSize = 28.sp)
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(game.title, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 16.sp)
                        Text("平台: ${game.platform}", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 12.sp)
                    }
                    SylphoraButton(
                        onClick = { viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context) },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text("玩這款", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun ArchaeologyHealthView(viewModel: GameViewModel) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val health = viewModel.calculateHealth()

    LazyColumn(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(24.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp)
    ) {
        item {
            // Health Score Banner
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                    .background(Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(theme.surface, theme.cardStart)))
                    .border(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.3f), RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                    .padding(24.dp)
            ) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(90.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                            .border(3.dp, theme.accent, CircleShape),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Text("${health.score.toInt()}%", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(24.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text("遊戲庫健康度指數", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                        Text("總計收錄 ${health.totalGames} 款遊戲，缺少簡介: ${health.missingDesc} 款", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        item {
            Text("📊 各平台遊戲庫存分佈", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                health.platformCounts.forEach { (platform, count) ->
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                            .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                    ) {
                        Text(platform, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 15.sp)
                        Text("$count 款", color = theme.accent, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 14.sp)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.example.ui.screens

import android.content.Intent
