@Composable
fun LibraryScreen(
    viewModel: GameViewModel,
    installedApps: List<AppInfo> = emptyList(),
    onLaunchApp: (String) -> Unit = {}
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val allGames by viewModel.allGames.collectAsState()
    val isBatchScraping by viewModel.isBatchScraping.collectAsState()
    val batchScrapeProgress by viewModel.batchScrapeProgress.collectAsState()
    val layoutMode by viewModel.currentLayoutMode.collectAsState()
    val customCollections by viewModel.customCollectionsList.collectAsState()
    val selectedCollection by viewModel.selectedCollectionFilter.collectAsState()
    val selectedPlayStatus by viewModel.selectedPlayStatusFilter.collectAsState()
    val themeIndex by viewModel.currentThemeFamilyIndex.collectAsState()
    val isCrtEnabled by viewModel.isCrtEnabled.collectAsState()
    val fontScale by viewModel.fontSize.collectAsState()

    var searchQuery by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var debouncedQuery by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        snapshotFlow { searchQuery }
            .collectLatest { q ->
                delay(250)
                debouncedQuery = q
            }
    }
    var selectedPlatform by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var selectedGameForDetail by remember { mutableStateOf<GameEntity?>(null) }
    var showApplications by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showScrapePicker by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val platforms = remember(allGames) {
        listOf("全部") + allGames.map { it.platform }.distinct().sorted()
    }

    LaunchedEffect(showApplications, selectedPlatform) {
        if (!showApplications) {
            viewModel.updateCuratorNavigation("LIBRARY", selectedPlatform ?: "全部")
        }
    }

    val playStatusOptions = listOf("全部", "PLAYING", "COMPLETED", "MASTERED", "UNPLAYED")

    val filteredGames = remember(allGames, debouncedQuery, selectedPlatform, selectedCollection, selectedPlayStatus) {
        allGames.filter { game ->
            val matchPlat = selectedPlatform == null || selectedPlatform == "全部" || game.platform.equals(selectedPlatform, ignoreCase = true)
            val matchQuery = debouncedQuery.isBlank() || game.title.contains(debouncedQuery, ignoreCase = true) || game.platform.contains(debouncedQuery, ignoreCase = true)
            val matchStatus = selectedPlayStatus == null || selectedPlayStatus == "全部" || game.playStatus.equals(selectedPlayStatus, ignoreCase = true)
            val matchCollection = selectedCollection == null || game.customCollections.split(",").contains(selectedCollection)
            matchPlat && matchQuery && matchStatus && matchCollection
        }
    }

    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        ThemeLibraryHeader(
            theme = theme,
            themeIndex = viewModel.currentThemeFamilyIndex.collectAsState().value,
            gameCount = allGames.size,
            appMode = showApplications,
            onToggleApps = { showApplications = !showApplications }
        )

        // P1 — Applications live inside the Library, never as a parallel top-level route.
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            FilterChip(
                selected = !showApplications,
                onClick = { showApplications = false },
                label = { Text("遊戲") },
                leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Filled.SportsEsports, contentDescription = null) }
            )
            FilterChip(
                selected = showApplications,
                onClick = { showApplications = true },
                label = { Text("應用程式") },
                leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Filled.Apps, contentDescription = null) }
            )
        }

        if (showApplications) {
            AppsScreen(
                apps = installedApps,
                onLaunchApp = onLaunchApp,
                viewModel = viewModel,
                fontScale = fontScale.scale
            )
            return@Column
        }

        // P2 V3 — non-Sylphora themes own the complete Library composition.
        // Sylphora (index 0) continues through the original Library layouts below.
        if (themeIndex != 0) {
            var themedSelectedGame by remember { mutableStateOf<GameEntity?>(null) }
            ThemeExperienceLibrary(
                theme = theme,
                themeIndex = themeIndex,
                games = filteredGames,
                selectedGame = themedSelectedGame,
                searchQuery = searchQuery,
                onSearchQueryChange = { searchQuery = it },
                selectedPlatform = selectedPlatform,
                platforms = platforms,
                onPlatformChange = { selectedPlatform = it },
                selectedCollection = selectedCollection,
                collections = customCollections,
                onCollectionChange = { viewModel.selectedCollectionFilter.value = it },
                onGameSelected = { themedSelectedGame = it; selectedGameForDetail = it },
                onLaunch = { game ->
                    SoundManager.playLaunchSound(context)
                    viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context)
                    
                }
            )
            return@Column
        }

        // Top Filter Bar & Search
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            // Search Input
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = searchQuery,
                onValueChange = { searchQuery = it },
                placeholder = { Text("搜尋遊戲標題或平台...", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 14.sp * fontScale.scale) },
                leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Filled.Search, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent) },
                trailingIcon = {
                    if (searchQuery.isNotEmpty()) {
                        SylphoraIconButton(onClick = { searchQuery = "" }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.textSecondary)
                        }
                    }
                },
                singleLine = true,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .height(52.dp),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                colors = TextFieldDefaults.colors(
                    focusedTextColor = theme.textMain,
                    unfocusedTextColor = theme.textMain,
                    focusedContainerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                    unfocusedContainerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.3f),
                    focusedIndicatorColor = theme.accent,
                    unfocusedIndicatorColor = Color.Transparent
                )
            )

            // Batch Media Scrape Button (Secondary Utility Action)
            SylphoraButton(
                onClick = {
                    SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                    showScrapePicker = true
                },
                enabled = !isBatchScraping,
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.8f)),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.height(44.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.CloudDownload, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                Text(if (isBatchScraping) "下載圖檔中..." else "下載遊戲圖片 (封面/背景)", color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 12.sp * fontScale.scale)
            }

            // Layout Mode Switcher Pills
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                    .padding(4.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
            ) {
                SylphoraIconButton(
                    onClick = {
                        SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                        viewModel.currentLayoutMode.value = LauncherLayoutMode.PEGASUS_GRID
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(36.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(if (layoutMode == LauncherLayoutMode.PEGASUS_GRID) theme.accent else Color.Transparent)
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.GridView, contentDescription = "網格", tint = if (layoutMode == LauncherLayoutMode.PEGASUS_GRID) theme.background else theme.textSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                }
                SylphoraIconButton(
                    onClick = {
                        SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                        viewModel.currentLayoutMode.value = LauncherLayoutMode.ESDE_WHEEL_LIST
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(36.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(if (layoutMode == LauncherLayoutMode.ESDE_WHEEL_LIST) theme.accent else Color.Transparent)
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.ViewList, contentDescription = "列表", tint = if (layoutMode == LauncherLayoutMode.ESDE_WHEEL_LIST) theme.background else theme.textSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                }
                SylphoraIconButton(
                    onClick = {
                        SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                        viewModel.currentLayoutMode.value = LauncherLayoutMode.NIGHT_MUSEUM
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(36.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(if (layoutMode == LauncherLayoutMode.NIGHT_MUSEUM) theme.accent else Color.Transparent)
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.Museum, contentDescription = "資料館", tint = if (layoutMode == LauncherLayoutMode.NIGHT_MUSEUM) theme.background else theme.textSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                }
            }
        }

        // Batch Scraping Live Progress Banner
        if (isBatchScraping || batchScrapeProgress.isNotBlank()) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.15f))
                    .border(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.3f), RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
            ) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    if (isBatchScraping) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp), color = theme.accent, strokeWidth = 2.dp)
                    } else {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.CheckCircle, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                    }
                    Text(batchScrapeProgress, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 12.sp * fontScale.scale, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                }
            }
        }

        // Platform & Filter Chips Row
        LazyRow(
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            // Platforms
            items(platforms, key = { it }) { platform ->
                val isSelected = (selectedPlatform == platform) || (selectedPlatform == null && platform == "全部")
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(if (isSelected) theme.accent else theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                        .border(1.dp, if (isSelected) theme.accent else theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.8f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .clickable {
                            SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                            selectedPlatform = if (platform == "全部") null else platform
                        }
                        .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = platform,
                        color = if (isSelected) theme.background else theme.textMain,
                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                        fontSize = 12.sp * fontScale.scale
                    )
                }
            }

            item {
                Box(modifier = Modifier.width(1.dp).height(20.dp).background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f)))
            }

            // Play Status Chips
            items(playStatusOptions, key = { it }) { status ->
                val label = when (status) {
                    "PLAYING" -> "遊玩中"
                    "COMPLETED" -> "已通關"
                    "MASTERED" -> "已精通"
                    "UNPLAYED" -> "未遊玩"
                    else -> "全部狀態"
                }
                val isSelected = (selectedPlayStatus == status) || (selectedPlayStatus == null && status == "全部")
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(if (isSelected) theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.85f) else theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.4f))
                        .border(1.dp, if (isSelected) theme.accent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .clickable {
                            SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                            viewModel.selectedPlayStatusFilter.value = if (status == "全部") null else status
                        }
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = label,
                        color = if (isSelected) theme.background else theme.textSecondary,
                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                        fontSize = 12.sp * fontScale.scale
                    )
                }
            }

            item {
                Box(modifier = Modifier.width(1.dp).height(20.dp).background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f)))
            }

            // Custom Collection Chips
            items(customCollections, key = { it }) { col ->
                val isSelected = selectedCollection == col
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(if (isSelected) Color(0xFFEAB308) else theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.4f))
                        .border(1.dp, if (isSelected) Color(0xFFEAB308) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .clickable {
                            SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                            viewModel.selectedCollectionFilter.value = if (isSelected) null else col
                        }
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "🏷️ $col",
                        color = if (isSelected) Color.Black else theme.textSecondary,
                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                        fontSize = 12.sp * fontScale.scale
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))

        // Dynamic View by Layout Mode
        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f)) {
            if (filteredGames.isEmpty()) {
                Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                    Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.SearchOff, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.textSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp))
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                        Text("未找到符合條件的遊戲", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 16.sp * fontScale.scale)
                    }
                }
            } else {
                when (layoutMode) {
                    LauncherLayoutMode.II_SU_CAROUSEL -> {
                        IisuCarouselView(
                            games = filteredGames,
                            onGameClick = {
                                SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                                selectedGameForDetail = it
                            },
                            onLaunchDirect = { game ->
                                SoundManager.playLaunchSound(context)
                                viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context)
                                
                            }
                        )
                    }
                    LauncherLayoutMode.SWITCH_TILES -> {
                        SwitchTilesView(
                            games = filteredGames,
                            onGameClick = {
                                SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                                selectedGameForDetail = it
                            },
                            onLaunchDirect = { game ->
                                SoundManager.playLaunchSound(context)
                                viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context)
                                
                            }
                        )
                    }
                    LauncherLayoutMode.ESDE_WHEEL_LIST -> {
                        EsdeListView(
                            games = filteredGames,
                            onGameClick = {
                                SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                                selectedGameForDetail = it
                            },
                            onLaunchDirect = { game ->
                                SoundManager.playLaunchSound(context)
                                viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context)
                                
                            }
                        )
                    }
                    LauncherLayoutMode.NIGHT_MUSEUM -> {
                        NightMuseumExhibitionView(
                            games = filteredGames,
                            onGameClick = {
                                SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                                selectedGameForDetail = it
                            },
                            onLaunchDirect = { game ->
                                SoundManager.playLaunchSound(context)
                                viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context)
                                
                            }
                        )
                    }
                    LauncherLayoutMode.PEGASUS_GRID -> {
                        PegasusGridView(
                            games = filteredGames,
                            onGameClick = {
                                SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                                selectedGameForDetail = it
                            },
                            onLaunchDirect = { game ->
                                SoundManager.playLaunchSound(context)
                                viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context)
                                
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
            CrtOverlay(enabled = isCrtEnabled)
        }
    }
    }
    if (selectedGameForDetail != null) {
        GameMuseumDetailDialog(
            game = selectedGameForDetail!!,
            onDismiss = { selectedGameForDetail = null },
            viewModel = viewModel
        )
    }

    if (showScrapePicker) {
        ScrapeAssetPickerDialog(
            title = "選擇要刮削的資材",
            confirmLabel = "開始批次刮削",
            onDismiss = { showScrapePicker = false },
            onConfirm = { kinds ->
                showScrapePicker = false
                viewModel.startBatchSteamGridScraping(kinds)
            }
        )
    }

    val activeEmulatorPicker by viewModel.activeEmulatorPicker.collectAsState()
    val activeNoEmulatorDialog by viewModel.activeNoEmulatorDialog.collectAsState()

    if (activeEmulatorPicker != null) {
        EmulatorSelectionDialog(
            state = activeEmulatorPicker!!,
            onDismiss = { viewModel.activeEmulatorPicker.value = null },
            onSelectLauncher = { launcherId, setAsDefault ->
                viewModel.launchGameWithLauncher(context, activeEmulatorPicker!!.game, launcherId, setAsDefault)
            }
        )
    }

    if (activeNoEmulatorDialog != null) {
        NoEmulatorDialog(
            game = activeNoEmulatorDialog!!,
            onDismiss = { viewModel.activeNoEmulatorDialog.value = null }
        )
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. iiSU 流體輪播佈局 (iiSU Fluid Carousel View)
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun IisuCarouselView(
    games: List<GameEntity>,
    onGameClick: (GameEntity) -> Unit,
    onLaunchDirect: (GameEntity) -> Unit
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    var currentIndex by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    val activeGame = games.getOrNull(currentIndex) ?: games.firstOrNull()

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(vertical = 12.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
    ) {
        // Hero Carousel Row
        LazyRow(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(24.dp),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 48.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            items(games.size, key = { games[it].id }) { index ->
                val game = games[index]
                val isSelected = index == currentIndex

                BoxWithConstraints {
                    val maxH = maxHeight
                    val baseH = if (maxH > 0.dp) (maxH * 0.72f).coerceIn(160.dp, 360.dp) else 360.dp
                    val cardHeight = if (isSelected) baseH else baseH * (300f / 360f)
                    val cardWidth = cardHeight * (280f / 360f)

                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .width(cardWidth)
                            .height(cardHeight)
                            .clickable { currentIndex = index }
                            .border(
                                width = if (isSelected) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                                color = if (isSelected) theme.accent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
                            ),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = if (isSelected) 0.85f else 0.45f)),
                        elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = if (isSelected) 16.dp else 4.dp)
                    ) {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                        val displayImage = game.coverPath ?: game.backgroundPath ?: game.iconPath ?: game.screenshotPath
                        if (!displayImage.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            AsyncImage(
                                model = displayImage,
                                contentDescription = game.title,
                                contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                            )
                        } else {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(theme.cardStart, theme.cardEnd))),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                            ) {
                                Text(game.platform, color = theme.textSecondary, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 28.sp)
                            }
                        }

                        // Gradient Shade
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxSize()
                                .background(Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(Color.Transparent, Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.8f))))
                        )

                        // Top Badges
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp).align(Alignment.TopStart),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                        ) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                    .background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.9f))
                                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                            ) {
                                Text(game.platform, color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 11.sp)
                            }

                            if (game.rating > 0) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                        .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                                        .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Text("★ ${String.format("%.1f", game.rating)}", color = Color(0xFFFFD700), fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Bottom Title info
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomStart).padding(16.dp)) {
                            Text(game.title, color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = if (isSelected) 18.sp else 15.sp, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                            if (!game.developer.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                Text(game.developer, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.7f), fontSize = 12.sp, maxLines = 1)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            }
        }

        // Active Game Command Hub
        if (activeGame != null) {
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 32.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.8f)),
                border = BorderStroke(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f))
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 16.dp)) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Text(activeGame.title, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 18.sp, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            if (!activeGame.releaseDate.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                Text("(${activeGame.releaseDate})", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
                            }
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = activeGame.description ?: "尚無遊戲簡介記錄，點擊開啟歷史博物館考據與金手指資料庫。",
                            color = theme.textSecondary,
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                        )
                    }

                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                        SylphoraOutlinedButton(
                            onClick = { onGameClick(activeGame) },
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                            border = BorderStroke(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.Museum, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text("資料館", color = theme.accent, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 13.sp)
                        }

                        SylphoraButton(
                            onClick = { onLaunchDirect(activeGame) },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayArrow, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.background, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text("啟動遊戲", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 13.sp)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. Switch 方塊平鋪佈局 (Switch Tiles Horizontal View)
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun SwitchTilesView(
    games: List<GameEntity>,
    onGameClick: (GameEntity) -> Unit,
    onLaunchDirect: (GameEntity) -> Unit
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    var selectedIndex by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    val activeGame = games.getOrNull(selectedIndex) ?: games.firstOrNull()

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
    ) {
        // Active Game Banner Header
        if (activeGame != null) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 8.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
            ) {
                Column {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .background(theme.accent)
                                .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(activeGame.platform, color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 12.sp)
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                        Text(activeGame.title, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 22.sp, maxLines = 1)
                    }
                    if (!activeGame.developer.isNullOrBlank() || !activeGame.genre.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        Text("${activeGame.developer ?: "Unknown"} · ${activeGame.genre ?: "Classic"}", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    }
                }

                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    SylphoraButton(
                        onClick = { onLaunchDirect(activeGame) },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayArrow, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.background)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                        Text("開始", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                    SylphoraOutlinedButton(
                        onClick = { onGameClick(activeGame) },
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, theme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.4f))
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.Info, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.textMain)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Horizontal Square Tile Rail
        LazyRow(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 16.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            items(games.size, key = { games[it].id }) { index ->
                val game = games[index]
                val isSelected = index == selectedIndex

                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(if (isSelected) 220.dp else 180.dp)
                        .clickable {
                            selectedIndex = index
                        }
                        .border(
                            width = if (isSelected) 3.dp else 1.dp,
                            color = if (isSelected) theme.accent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)
                        ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.6f)),
                    elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = if (isSelected) 12.dp else 2.dp)
                ) {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                        val displayImage = game.coverPath ?: game.backgroundPath ?: game.iconPath ?: game.screenshotPath
                        if (!displayImage.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            AsyncImage(
                                model = displayImage,
                                contentDescription = game.title,
                                contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                            )
                        } else {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(theme.cardStart, theme.cardEnd))),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                            ) {
                                Text(game.platform, color = theme.textSecondary, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 20.sp)
                            }
                        }

                        // Play time badge
                        if (game.playTimeMinutes > 0) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .align(Alignment.BottomEnd)
                                    .padding(8.dp)
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                                    .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                                    .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                            ) {
                                Text("⏱️ ${game.playTimeMinutes}m", color = Color.White, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Bottom Console Hint
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(bottom = 8.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text("點選卡片切換焦點與簡介，點擊「開始」按鈕進入模擬器", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)) {
                Text("🅰️ 進入遊戲", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Text("➕ 詳細資料館", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
            }
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. 網格海報佈局 (Pegasus Grid View)
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun PegasusGridView(
    games: List<GameEntity>,
    onGameClick: (GameEntity) -> Unit,
    onLaunchDirect: (GameEntity) -> Unit = {}
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    LazyVerticalGrid(
        columns = GridCells.Adaptive(minSize = 160.dp),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(24.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp),
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
    ) {
        items(games, key = { it.id }) { game ->
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(250.dp)
                    .clickable { onGameClick(game) },
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.6f)),
                border = BorderStroke(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f))
            ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .weight(1f)
                            .background(Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(theme.cardStart, theme.cardEnd))),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        com.example.ui.theme.UniversalGameArt(
                            game = game,
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                        )

                        // Top Badges
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(8.dp)
                                .align(Alignment.TopStart),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                        ) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                    .background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.85f))
                                    .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                            ) {
                                Text(game.platform, color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 10.sp)
                            }

                            if (game.playTimeMinutes > 0) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                        .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                                        .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                ) {
                                    val timeStr = if (game.playTimeMinutes >= 60) "${game.playTimeMinutes / 60}h" else "${game.playTimeMinutes}m"
                                    Text("⏱️ $timeStr", color = Color.White, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Quick launch button on cover
                        SylphoraIconButton(
                            onClick = { onLaunchDirect(game) },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.BottomEnd)
                                .padding(6.dp)
                                .size(32.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.9f))
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayArrow, contentDescription = "Launch", tint = theme.background, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                        }
                    }

                    Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp)) {
                        Text(
                            text = game.title,
                            color = theme.textMain,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = when (game.playStatus) {
                                "PLAYING" -> "🟢 遊玩中"
                                "COMPLETED" -> "⭐ 已通關"
                                "MASTERED" -> "👑 白金"
                                else -> "🆕 未遊玩"
                            },
                            color = theme.textSecondary,
                            fontSize = 11.sp
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// 4. ES-DE 經典清單佈局 (ESDE List View)
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun EsdeListView(
    games: List<GameEntity>,
    onGameClick: (GameEntity) -> Unit,
    onLaunchDirect: (GameEntity) -> Unit = {}
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    var selectedIndex by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    val activeGame = games.getOrNull(selectedIndex) ?: games.firstOrNull()

    Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 8.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(24.dp)) {
        // Left Wheel List
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1.2f).fillMaxHeight(),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(vertical = 12.dp)
        ) {
            items(games.size, key = { games[it].id }) { index ->
                val game = games[index]
                val isSelected = index == selectedIndex
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(if (isSelected) theme.accent else theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.4f))
                        .border(1.dp, if (isSelected) theme.accent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.05f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .clickable {
                            selectedIndex = index
                        }
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                ) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            text = game.title,
                            color = if (isSelected) theme.background else theme.textMain,
                            fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                            fontSize = 15.sp,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                        )
                    }
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                            .background(if (isSelected) theme.background.copy(alpha = 0.2f) else theme.surface)
                            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(game.platform, color = if (isSelected) theme.background else theme.accent, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Right Detail Preview Panel
        if (activeGame != null) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .fillMaxHeight()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                    .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f), RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                    .padding(20.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
            ) {
                Column {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(180.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            .background(Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(theme.cardStart, theme.cardEnd))),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        com.example.ui.theme.UniversalGameArt(
                            game = activeGame,
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    Text(activeGame.title, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 20.sp, maxLines = 2)
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = activeGame.description ?: "尚未有簡介，可點擊下方按鈕獲取遊戲歷史與特徵...",
                        color = theme.textSecondary,
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        maxLines = 3,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                }

                Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    SylphoraOutlinedButton(
                        onClick = { onGameClick(activeGame) },
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).height(48.dp),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.Museum, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                        Text("考據館", color = theme.accent, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    }

                    SylphoraButton(
                        onClick = { onLaunchDirect(activeGame) },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1.2f).height(48.dp),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayArrow, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.background)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                        Text("啟動遊戲", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// 5. 夜間博物館特展佈局 (Night Museum Exhibition View)
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun NightMuseumExhibitionView(
    games: List<GameEntity>,

) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    LazyColumn(
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(24.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
    ) {
        item {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))

                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
                    Column {

                        Text("作品歷史迴廊，點擊卡片瀏覽考據資料館、特徵光譜、金手指攻略與成就", color = Color(0xFF94A3B8), fontSize = 12.sp)

        items(games, key = { it.id }) { game ->
            Row(
