                    .padding(16.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(100.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(theme.cardStart),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    com.example.ui.theme.UniversalGameArt(
                        game = game,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                    )
                }
                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Text(game.title, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 17.sp, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), maxLines = 1)
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp)).background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.2f)).padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 2.dp)) {
                            Text(game.platform, color = theme.accent, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        }
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = game.description ?: "點擊載入遊戲詳細簡介與歷史資料...",
                        color = theme.textSecondary,
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        maxLines = 2,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                }

                SylphoraIconButton(
                    onClick = { onLaunchDirect(game) },
                    modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(theme.accent)
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayArrow, contentDescription = "Play", tint = theme.background)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// MUSEUM MODE 2.0 深度遊戲資料館對話框 (Dialog)
// 包含 5 大分頁：【🏛️ 歷史考據】、【🧬 Game DNA】、【💡 AI 金手指與攻略】、【🏆 成就】、【⏱️ 遊玩管理】
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun GameMuseumDetailDialog(game: GameEntity, onDismiss: () -> Unit, viewModel: GameViewModel) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val themeIndex by viewModel.currentThemeFamilyIndex.collectAsState()
    val clipboardManager = LocalClipboardManager.current
    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val moshi = remember { Moshi.Builder().add(KotlinJsonAdapterFactory()).build() }

    var selectedTab by remember { mutableStateOf(0) } // 0: 歷史考據, 1: Game DNA, 2: 作品演化, 3: 金手指與攻略, 4: 遊玩管理
    val tabs = listOf("歷史考據", "Game DNA", "作品演化", "金手指/攻略", "遊玩管理")

    // Cheats state
    var cheatsData by remember { mutableStateOf<CheatGuideData?>(null) }
    var isLoadingCheats by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    // Evolution state (Phase P1 — was backend-only before)
    var evolutionData by remember { mutableStateOf<com.example.agent.EvolutionData?>(null) }
    var isLoadingEvolution by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val customCollections by viewModel.customCollectionsList.collectAsState()

    // Museum state
    var dynamicMuseumFields by remember { mutableStateOf<MuseumFields?>(null) }
    var isLoadingMuseum by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    // DNA state
    var dynamicGameDna by remember { mutableStateOf<GameDna?>(null) }
    var isLoadingDna by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val initialMuseumFields = remember(game.museumJson) {
        if (!game.museumJson.isNullOrBlank()) {
            try {
                moshi.adapter(MuseumFields::class.java).fromJson(game.museumJson)
            } catch (e: Exception) { null }
        } else null
    }

    val initialGameDna = remember(game.dnaJson) {
        if (!game.dnaJson.isNullOrBlank()) {
            try {
                moshi.adapter(GameDna::class.java).fromJson(game.dnaJson)
            } catch (e: Exception) { null }
        } else null
    }

    val currentMuseumFields = dynamicMuseumFields ?: initialMuseumFields
    val currentGameDna = dynamicGameDna ?: initialGameDna

    LaunchedEffect(selectedTab) {
        if (selectedTab == 2 && evolutionData == null && !isLoadingEvolution) {
            isLoadingEvolution = true
            evolutionData = viewModel.fetchOrGenerateEvolution(game)
            isLoadingEvolution = false
        } else if (selectedTab == 3 && cheatsData == null && !isLoadingCheats) {
            isLoadingCheats = true
            cheatsData = viewModel.fetchOrGenerateCheats(game)
            isLoadingCheats = false
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(selectedTab, game) {
        viewModel.updateCuratorNavigation("GAME_MUSEUM", tabs.getOrNull(selectedTab))
        viewModel.selectGame(game)
    }

    val detailShape = when (themeIndex) {
        1 -> RoundedCornerShape(32.dp)
        2 -> RoundedCornerShape(6.dp)
        3 -> RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)
        4 -> RoundedCornerShape(22.dp)
        5 -> RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)
        6 -> RoundedCornerShape(30.dp)
        7 -> RoundedCornerShape(2.dp)
        else -> RoundedCornerShape(28.dp)
    }
    val detailBorder = when (themeIndex) {
        2 -> 2.dp
        5, 7 -> 3.dp
        else -> 1.dp
    }

    Dialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        properties = androidx.compose.ui.window.DialogProperties(usePlatformDefaultWidth = false)
    ) {
        BoxWithConstraints(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            val dialogWidth = (maxWidth * 0.95f).coerceIn(280.dp, 1000.dp)
            val dialogHeight = (maxHeight * 0.92f).coerceIn(240.dp, 750.dp)
            val isShortDialog = dialogHeight < 420.dp
            val dialogPad = if (isShortDialog) 10.dp else 20.dp

            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .width(dialogWidth)
                    .height(dialogHeight)
                    .clip(detailShape)
                    .background(
                        if (themeIndex == 1) Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(theme.surface, theme.background))
                        else Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(theme.background, theme.background))
                    )
                    .border(detailBorder, theme.accent.copy(alpha = if (themeIndex == 5 || themeIndex == 7) 0.9f else 0.35f), detailShape)
            ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(dialogPad)) {
                // Header (Cover Thumbnail + Title/Platform + Close)
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    val cover = game.coverPath ?: game.iconPath ?: game.screenshotPath
                    if (!cover.isNullOrBlank() && !isShortDialog) {
                        AsyncImage(
                            model = cover,
                            contentDescription = game.title,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(width = 52.dp, height = 66.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .background(theme.surface),
                            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                    }
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                            Box(modifier = Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)).background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.2f)).padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 2.dp)) {
                                Text(game.platform, color = theme.accent, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            }
                            if (game.playTimeMinutes > 0) {
                                Text("${game.playTimeMinutes} 分鐘", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
                            }
                        }
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = when (themeIndex) {
                                2 -> "OBJECT · ${game.title.uppercase()}"
                                5 -> "// ${game.title.uppercase()}"
                                7 -> game.title.uppercase()
                                else -> game.title
                            },
                            color = theme.textMain,
                            fontSize = if (isShortDialog) 18.sp else 22.sp,
                            fontWeight = if (themeIndex == 2) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Bold,
                            fontFamily = if (themeIndex == 5 || themeIndex == 7) FontFamily.Monospace else FontFamily.Default,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                        )
                    }
                    SylphoraIconButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = "關閉", tint = theme.textMain)
                    }
                }

                if (themeIndex == 5) {
                    Text(
                        text = "STATUS :: ARCHIVE LINKED  //  MEDIA :: ${if (game.coverPath.isNullOrBlank()) "MISSING" else "READY"}",
                        color = theme.accent,
                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                        fontSize = 9.sp,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 8.dp)
                    )
                } else if (themeIndex == 7) {
                    Text(
                        text = "[${game.platform.uppercase()}]  •  SAVE SLOT READY",
                        color = theme.textSecondary,
                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                        fontSize = 9.sp,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 8.dp)
                    )
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                // Launch & Favorite Action Row
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                    SylphoraButton(
                        onClick = {
                            viewModel.recordGameLaunch(game, context)
                            
                        },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).height(44.dp),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayArrow, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.background)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text("啟動遊戲 (Launch)", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 14.sp)
                    }

                    SylphoraIconButton(
                        onClick = { viewModel.toggleFavorite(game) },
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(44.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                            .background(theme.surface)
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            if (game.isFavorite) Icons.Filled.Favorite else Icons.Filled.FavoriteBorder,
                            contentDescription = "收藏",
                            tint = if (game.isFavorite) Color.Red else theme.textMain
                        )
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))

                // Navigation Tabs
                ScrollableTabRow(
                    selectedTabIndex = selectedTab,
                    containerColor = Color.Transparent,
                    contentColor = theme.accent,
                    edgePadding = 0.dp,
                    divider = {}
                ) {
                    tabs.forEachIndexed { index, title ->
                        Tab(
                            selected = selectedTab == index,
                            onClick = {
                                SoundManager.playSelectSound(context)
                                selectedTab = index
                            },
                            text = {
                                Text(
                                    title,
                                    color = if (selectedTab == index) theme.accent else theme.textSecondary,
                                    fontWeight = if (selectedTab == index) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                    fontSize = 13.sp
                                )
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                // Content Panel by Selected Tab
                Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f)) {
                    when (selectedTab) {
                        0 -> MuseumHistoryTab(
                            game = game,
                            museumFields = currentMuseumFields,
                            isLoading = isLoadingMuseum,
                            onGenerate = {
                                if (!isLoadingMuseum) {
                                    coroutineScope.launch {
                                        isLoadingMuseum = true
                                        try {
                                            val generated = viewModel.fetchOrGenerateMuseum(game)
                                            if (generated != null) {
                                                dynamicMuseumFields = generated
                                            } else {
                                                Toast.makeText(context, "目前無法取得歷史考據，請檢查網路或稍後重試", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                            }
                                        } catch (e: Exception) {
                                            Toast.makeText(context, "目前無法取得歷史考據：${e.localizedMessage ?: "未知錯誤"}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                        } finally {
                                            isLoadingMuseum = false
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        )
                        1 -> GameDnaTab(
                            gameDna = currentGameDna,
                            isLoading = isLoadingDna,
                            onGenerate = {
                                if (!isLoadingDna) {
                                    coroutineScope.launch {
                                        isLoadingDna = true
                                        try {
                                            val generated = viewModel.fetchOrGenerateDna(game)
                                            if (generated != null) {
                                                dynamicGameDna = generated
                                            } else {
                                                Toast.makeText(context, "目前無法取得 Game DNA，請檢查網路或稍後重試", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                            }
                                        } catch (e: Exception) {
                                            Toast.makeText(context, "目前無法取得 Game DNA：${e.localizedMessage ?: "未知錯誤"}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                        } finally {
                                            isLoadingDna = false
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        )
                        2 -> EvolutionTab(
                            evolutionData = evolutionData,
                            isLoading = isLoadingEvolution,
                            findLibraryMatch = { title -> viewModel.findLibraryMatchForTitle(title) }
                        )
                        3 -> CheatsAndGuideTab(
                            cheatsData = cheatsData,
                            isLoading = isLoadingCheats,
                            onCopyCode = { code ->
                                clipboardManager.setText(AnnotatedString(code))
                                Toast.makeText(context, "已複製金手指代碼: $code", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            }
                        )
                        4 -> PlayManagementTab(
                            game = game,
                            customCollections = customCollections,
                            onStatusChange = { newStatus -> viewModel.updatePlayStatus(game, newStatus) },
                            onAddPlayTime = { mins -> viewModel.addPlayTime(game, mins) },
                            onToggleCollection = { col -> viewModel.toggleGameCollection(game, col) },
                            onUpdateGame = { updated -> viewModel.updateGame(updated) },
                            onDeleteGame = {
                                viewModel.deleteGame(game)
                                onDismiss()
                            },
                            onScrapeSingleGame = { kinds ->
                                viewModel.scrapeSingleGame(game, kinds)
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
}

@Composable
private fun ScrapeAssetPickerDialog(
    title: String,
    confirmLabel: String,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onConfirm: (Set<AssetKind>) -> Unit
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    var selected by remember {
        mutableStateOf(setOf(AssetKind.BOX_FRONT, AssetKind.HERO, AssetKind.TITLE))
    }
    val labels = mapOf(
        AssetKind.BOX_FRONT to "封面 / Box Front",
        AssetKind.ICON to "圖示 / Icon",
        AssetKind.TITLE to "Logo / Title",
        AssetKind.HERO to "背景 / Hero",
        AssetKind.SLIDE to "截圖 / Screenshot"
    )
    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = { Text(title, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) },
        text = {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                Text("選擇要從 provider 搜尋並下載的資材類型。只會處理實際選取的種類。", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
                Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                AssetKind.values().forEach { kind ->
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable {
                            selected = if (selected.contains(kind)) selected - kind else selected + kind
                        },
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Checkbox(
                            checked = selected.contains(kind),
                            onCheckedChange = { checked ->
                                selected = if (checked) selected + kind else selected - kind
                            },
                            colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = theme.accent)
                        )
                        Text(labels[kind] ?: kind.canonical, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    }
                }
                Text("模式：只補缺失的本機檔案；已存在的資材不會重抓。", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 11.sp)
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            TextButton(
                onClick = { if (selected.isNotEmpty()) onConfirm(selected) },
                enabled = selected.isNotEmpty()
            ) { Text(confirmLabel, color = theme.accent, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) { Text("取消", color = theme.textSecondary) }
        },
        containerColor = theme.surface
    )
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Tab 1: 🏛️ 歷史考據
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun MuseumHistoryTab(
    game: GameEntity,
    museumFields: MuseumFields?,
    isLoading: Boolean = false,
    onGenerate: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val provenanceMap = remember(game.id) {
        try {
            val permanentFile = java.io.File(java.io.File(context.filesDir, "provenance"), "provenance_${game.id}.json")
            val cacheFile = java.io.File(context.cacheDir, "provenance_${game.id}.json")
            val file = if (permanentFile.exists()) permanentFile else cacheFile
            if (file.exists()) {
                val json = org.json.JSONObject(file.readText())
                val list = mutableListOf<Triple<String, String, String>>()
                val keyLabels = listOf(
                    "developer" to "開發商 (Developer)",
                    "publisher" to "發行商 (Publisher)",
                    "releaseDate" to "發售日期 (Release Date)",
                    "genre" to "遊戲類型 (Genre)",
                    "description" to "遊戲介紹 (Description)"
                )
                keyLabels.forEach { (k, label) ->
                    if (json.has(k) && !json.isNull(k)) {
                        val obj = json.optJSONObject(k)
                        if (obj != null) {
                            val v = obj.optString("value", "")
                            val src = obj.optString("source", "Web")
                            val conf = obj.optString("confidence", "HIGH")
                            if (v.isNotBlank()) {
                                list.add(Triple(label, v, "$src • 信心度 $conf"))
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                list
            } else emptyList()
        } catch (e: Exception) { emptyList() }
    }

    LazyColumn(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        // 📖 Dedicated 遊戲簡介 (Game Description) Card
        item {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                    .border(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.25f), RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                    .padding(16.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
            ) {
                Text("📖 遊戲簡介", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                
                if (!game.description.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    Text(
                        text = game.description,
                        color = theme.textMain,
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        lineHeight = 19.sp
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "資料來源：${if (game.descriptionCsvSynced == true) "Description CSV (/storage/emulated/0/description/${game.platform.lowercase()}.csv)" else "本機資材檔 / 本地資料庫"}",
                        color = theme.textSecondary,
                        fontSize = 11.sp
                    )
                } else {
                    Text(
                        text = "目前沒有遊戲簡介",
                        color = theme.textSecondary,
                        fontSize = 13.sp
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "提示：可在 /storage/emulated/0/description/ 放置 ${game.platform.lowercase()}.csv 或進行全庫 Asset 比對即可自動匯入。",
                        color = theme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.8f),
                        fontSize = 11.sp
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        if (provenanceMap.isNotEmpty()) {
            item {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                        .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.55f))
                        .border(1.dp, theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.35f), RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                        .padding(14.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.Verified, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text("🔍 AI 考據來源與欄位溯源 (Provenance)", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                    provenanceMap.forEach { (label, value, sourceMeta) ->
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 2.dp)) {
                            Text(label, color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 11.sp)
                            Text(value, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
                            Text("來源: $sourceMeta", color = theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.85f), fontSize = 10.sp)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        item {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.05f), RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                    .padding(14.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text("🏛️ 歷史資料考據 (7 大維度)", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    if (museumFields != null && !isLoading) {
                        TextButton(onClick = onGenerate, contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 2.dp)) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Icon(Icons.Filled.Refresh, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Text("重新生成", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 12.sp)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (isLoading) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 20.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                        Text("AI 館長深度考據中，請稍候...", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    }
                } else if (museumFields == null) {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 12.dp),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text("尚未建立歷史考據資料", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
                        Button(
                            onClick = onGenerate,
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                        ) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Icon(Icons.Filled.AutoAwesome, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.background, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                Text("✨ 生成歷史考據", color = theme.background, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    MuseumFieldRow("背景故事", museumFields.background ?: game.description ?: "尚無背景故事考據資料")
                    MuseumFieldRow("歷史地位", museumFields.historical_status ?: "尚無歷史地位考據資料")
                    MuseumFieldRow("配樂特色", museumFields.music_highlights ?: "尚無配樂特色考據資料")
                    MuseumFieldRow("開發幕後", museumFields.dev_story ?: "尚無開發幕後考據資料")
                    MuseumFieldRow("遊玩建議", museumFields.play_order ?: "尚無遊玩建議考據資料")
                    MuseumFieldRow("相似推薦", museumFields.similar_games ?: "尚無相似推薦資料")
                    MuseumFieldRow("系列作品", museumFields.series_works ?: "尚無系列作品資料")
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Tab 2: 🧬 Game DNA
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun GameDnaTab(
    gameDna: GameDna?,
    isLoading: Boolean = false,
    onGenerate: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    LazyColumn(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        item {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.05f), RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                    .padding(16.dp)
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text("🧬 Game DNA 特徵光譜", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    if (gameDna != null && !isLoading) {
                        TextButton(onClick = onGenerate, contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 2.dp)) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Icon(Icons.Filled.Refresh, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Text("重新生成", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 12.sp)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                if (isLoading) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 20.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                        Text("正在解析遊戲 DNA 特徵光譜...", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    }
                } else if (gameDna != null && gameDna.axes.isNotEmpty()) {
                    gameDna.axes.forEach { axis ->
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 4.dp)) {
                            Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween) {
                                Text(axis.label, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 13.sp)
                                Text("${axis.score}%", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            }
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                            LinearProgressIndicator(
                                progress = { axis.score / 100f },
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(6.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)),
                                color = theme.accent,
                                trackColor = theme.background
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                    Text("核心特徵: ${gameDna.summary}", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
                } else {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 12.dp),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text("尚未建立 Game DNA 特徵光譜", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
                        Button(
                            onClick = onGenerate,
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                        ) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Icon(Icons.Filled.AutoAwesome, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.background, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                Text("✨ 生成 Game DNA", color = theme.background, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Tab: 🌳 作品演化 (Evolution) — Phase P1, backend existed, UI newly wired
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun EvolutionTab(
    evolutionData: com.example.agent.EvolutionData?,
    isLoading: Boolean,
    findLibraryMatch: (String) -> GameEntity?
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current

    fun relationLabel(raw: String): String = when (raw.trim().lowercase()) {
        "original" -> "🌱 原作"
        "sequel" -> "➡️ 續作"
        "prequel" -> "⬅️ 前傳"
        "remake" -> "🔨 重製版"
        "remaster" -> "✨ 重新整理版"
        "reboot" -> "🔄 重啟作品"
        "spinoff", "spin-off" -> "🌿 衍生作品"
        else -> raw
    }

    if (isLoading) {
        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                CircularProgressIndicator(color = theme.accent)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                Text("正在檢索作品演化關係...", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
            }
        }
        return
    }

    if (evolutionData == null || !evolutionData.known || evolutionData.links.isEmpty()) {
        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Text("尚未找到已知的演化關係（原作／續作／重製版等）。", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
        }
        return
    }

    LazyColumn(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        items(evolutionData.links, key = { "${it.title}_${it.relation}" }) { link ->
            val libraryMatch = findLibraryMatch(link.title)
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.05f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                    .padding(14.dp)
            ) {
                Text(relationLabel(link.relation), color = theme.accent, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                Text(link.title, color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                if (!link.note.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    Text(link.note, color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                if (libraryMatch != null) {
                    Text("✅ 已收藏於遊戲庫（${libraryMatch.platform}）", color = Color(0xFF34D399), fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                } else {
                    Text("尚未收藏於遊戲庫", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 11.sp)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Tab 3: 💡 AI 金手指與卡關攻略
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun CheatsAndGuideTab(
    cheatsData: CheatGuideData?,
    isLoading: Boolean,
    onCopyCode: (String) -> Unit
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    if (isLoading) {
        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                CircularProgressIndicator(color = theme.accent)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                Text("正在檢索經典金手指與攻略...", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
            }
        }
    } else if (cheatsData == null || (cheatsData.cheats.isEmpty() && cheatsData.quickTips.isEmpty())) {
        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Text("暫無金手指資料，請檢查 API Key 設定。", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp)
        }
    } else {
        LazyColumn(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            if (cheatsData.quickTips.isNotEmpty()) {
                item {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            .background(Color(0xFF3B1D2A))
                            .border(1.dp, Color(0xFFF43F5E).copy(alpha = 0.3f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            .padding(14.dp)
                    ) {
                        Column {
                            Text("通關與 Boss 戰重點秘訣", color = Color(0xFFF43F5E), fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 13.sp)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                            cheatsData.quickTips.forEach { tip ->
                                Row(modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 2.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                                    Text("•", color = Color(0xFFF43F5E), fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                    Text(tip, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.9f), fontSize = 12.sp, lineHeight = 17.sp)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            item {
                Text("經典秘技與金手指代碼 (Action Replay / GameShark / RAW)", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }

            items(cheatsData.cheats, key = { "${it.title}_${it.type}_${it.code}" }) { item ->
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.6f)),
                    border = BorderStroke(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f))
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(14.dp)) {
                        Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp), modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                                        .background(theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                                        .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Text(item.type, color = theme.accent, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                }
                                Text(item.title, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 14.sp, maxLines = 1)
                            }
                            if (!item.code.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                SylphoraButton(
                                    onClick = { onCopyCode(item.code) },
                                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.2f)),
                                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                                    modifier = Modifier.height(30.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Filled.ContentCopy, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp))
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                    Text("複製代碼", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        if (!item.code.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                    .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                                    .padding(8.dp)
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = item.code,
                                    color = Color(0xFF34D399),
                                    fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        if (item.content.isNotBlank()) {
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                            Text(item.content, color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp, lineHeight = 17.sp)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Tab 5: ⏱️ 遊玩管理與自訂分類
// ------------------------------------------------------------
@Composable
fun PlayManagementTab(
    game: GameEntity,
    customCollections: List<String>,
    onStatusChange: (String) -> Unit,
    onAddPlayTime: (Long) -> Unit,
    onToggleCollection: (String) -> Unit,
    onUpdateGame: ((GameEntity) -> Unit)? = null,
    onDeleteGame: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    onScrapeSingleGame: ((Set<AssetKind>) -> Unit)? = null
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val gameCollections = remember(game.customCollections) {
        game.customCollections.split(",").filter { it.isNotBlank() }.toSet()
    }

    var isEditing by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var editTitle by remember { mutableStateOf(game.title) }
    var editPlatform by remember { mutableStateOf(game.platform) }
    var editDeveloper by remember { mutableStateOf(game.developer ?: "") }
    var editReleaseDate by remember { mutableStateOf(game.releaseDate ?: "") }
    var editCoverPath by remember { mutableStateOf(game.coverPath ?: "") }
    var editDescription by remember { mutableStateOf(game.description ?: "") }

    var showDeleteConfirm by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showSingleScrapePicker by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LazyColumn(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        // Status Selector
        item {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    .padding(16.dp)
            ) {
                Text("📌 遊玩狀態標記", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    listOf(
                        "UNPLAYED" to "🆕 未遊玩",
                        "PLAYING" to "🟢 遊玩中",
                        "COMPLETED" to "⭐ 已通關",
                        "MASTERED" to "👑 白金"
                    ).forEach { (status, label) ->
                        val isSelected = game.playStatus == status
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                .background(if (isSelected) theme.accent else theme.background)
                                .border(1.dp, if (isSelected) theme.accent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f), RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                .clickable { onStatusChange(status) }
                                .padding(vertical = 8.dp),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = label,
                                color = if (isSelected) theme.background else theme.textMain,
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Playtime Modifier
        item {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    .padding(16.dp)
            ) {
                Text("⏱️ 累計遊玩時間: ${game.playTimeMinutes} 分鐘 (${game.playTimeMinutes / 60} 小時 ${game.playTimeMinutes % 60} 分)", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    SylphoraButton(
                        onClick = { onAddPlayTime(30L) },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.surface),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    ) {
                        Text("+30 分鐘", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 12.sp)
                    }
                    SylphoraButton(
                        onClick = { onAddPlayTime(60L) },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.surface),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    ) {
                        Text("+1 小時", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 12.sp)
                    }
                    SylphoraButton(
                        onClick = { onAddPlayTime(-30L) },
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.surface),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    ) {
                        Text("-30 分鐘", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 12.sp)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Custom Collections Tagging
        item {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    .padding(16.dp)
            ) {
                Text("🏷️ 自訂收藏集與分類標籤 (點擊加入/移除)", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                if (customCollections.isEmpty()) {
                    Text("尚未建立任何自訂標籤，可前往設定頁新增！", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
                } else {
                    LazyRow(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        items(customCollections, key = { it }) { col ->
                            val isChecked = gameCollections.contains(col)
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .background(if (isChecked) Color(0xFFEAB308) else theme.background)
                                    .border(1.dp, if (isChecked) Color(0xFFEAB308) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f), RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .clickable { onToggleCollection(col) }
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = (if (isChecked) "✓ " else "+ ") + col,
                                    color = if (isChecked) Color.Black else theme.textSecondary,
                                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                                    fontWeight = if (isChecked) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Metadata Edit & Maintenance Card
        item {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                    .background(theme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    .padding(16.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text("⚙️ 遊戲資訊管理與維護", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    if (!isEditing) {
                        SylphoraTextButton(onClick = { isEditing = true }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.Edit, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.accent, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text("編輯資訊", color = theme.accent, fontSize = 12.sp)
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (isEditing) {
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = editTitle,
                        onValueChange = { editTitle = it },
                        label = { Text("遊戲名稱") },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        singleLine = true
                    )
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = editPlatform,
                        onValueChange = { editPlatform = it },
                        label = { Text("主機平台 (例如 GBA, SNES, PS1)") },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        singleLine = true
                    )
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = editDeveloper,
                        onValueChange = { editDeveloper = it },
                        label = { Text("開發商 (Developer)") },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        singleLine = true
                    )
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = editReleaseDate,
                        onValueChange = { editReleaseDate = it },
                        label = { Text("發行年份 (Release Year/Date)") },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        singleLine = true
                    )
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = editCoverPath,
                        onValueChange = { editCoverPath = it },
                        label = { Text("封面圖路徑/URL (Cover Art URL)") },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        singleLine = true
                    )
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = editDescription,
                        onValueChange = { editDescription = it },
                        label = { Text("遊戲簡介描述") },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        maxLines = 4
                    )

                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                        SylphoraButton(
                            onClick = {
                                val updated = game.copy(
                                    title = editTitle,
                                    platform = editPlatform,
                                    developer = editDeveloper.ifBlank { null },
                                    releaseDate = editReleaseDate.ifBlank { null },
                                    coverPath = editCoverPath.ifBlank { null },
                                    description = editDescription.ifBlank { null }
                                )
                                onUpdateGame?.invoke(updated)
                                isEditing = false
                                Toast.makeText(context, "已更新遊戲資訊", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        ) {
                            Text("儲存修改", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        }
                        SylphoraOutlinedButton(
                            onClick = { isEditing = false },
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        ) {
                            Text("取消", color = theme.textMain)
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                        if (onScrapeSingleGame != null) {
                            SylphoraButton(
                                onClick = {
                                    showSingleScrapePicker = true
                                },
                                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.surface),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Filled.ImageSearch, contentDescription = null, tint = theme.textMain, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                Text("下載網路圖檔", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 12.sp)
                            }
                        }

                        if (onDeleteGame != null) {
                            SylphoraButton(
                                onClick = { showDeleteConfirm = true },
                                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFFEF4444).copy(alpha = 0.2f)),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Filled.Delete, contentDescription = null, tint = Color(0xFFEF4444), modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                Text("從遊戲庫移除", color = Color(0xFFEF4444), fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showDeleteConfirm) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showDeleteConfirm = false },
            title = { Text("確認移除遊戲？", color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) },
            text = { Text("確定要將《${game.title}》從前端遊戲庫中移除嗎？(不會刪除裝置上的 ROM 實體檔案)", color = theme.textSecondary) },
            confirmButton = {
                SylphoraButton(
                    onClick = {
                        showDeleteConfirm = false
                        onDeleteGame?.invoke()
                    },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFFEF4444))
                ) {
                    Text("確定移除", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                SylphoraTextButton(onClick = { showDeleteConfirm = false }) {
                    Text("取消", color = theme.textMain)
                }
            },
            containerColor = theme.background,
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)
        )
    }

    if (showSingleScrapePicker && onScrapeSingleGame != null) {
        ScrapeAssetPickerDialog(
            title = "《${game.title}》選擇資材",
            confirmLabel = "開始刮削",
            onDismiss = { showSingleScrapePicker = false },
            onConfirm = { kinds ->
                showSingleScrapePicker = false
                onScrapeSingleGame(kinds)
                Toast.makeText(context, "已啟動《${game.title}》資材刮削", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun MuseumFieldRow(title: String, content: String) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    Column {
        Text(title, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 14.sp)
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
        Text(content, color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 13.sp, lineHeight = 18.sp)
    }
}

@Composable
fun EmulatorSelectionDialog(
    state: GameViewModel.EmulatorPickerState,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onSelectLauncher: (String, Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    var setAsDefault by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var selectedLauncherId by remember { mutableStateOf(state.installedLaunchers.firstOrNull()?.id ?: "") }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text("選擇《${state.game.title}》的啟動模擬器", color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 16.sp)
        },
        text = {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                Text("偵測到系統中有多個支援 ${state.game.platform} 的模擬器：", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
                
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                    state.installedLaunchers.forEach { launcher ->
                        val isSelected = launcher.id == selectedLauncherId
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                .background(if (isSelected) theme.accent.copy(alpha = 0.2f) else theme.surface)
                                .border(1.dp, if (isSelected) theme.accent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f), RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                .clickable { selectedLauncherId = launcher.id }
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            RadioButton(
                                selected = isSelected,
                                onClick = { selectedLauncherId = launcher.id },
                                colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(selectedColor = theme.accent)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Column {
                                Text(launcher.label, color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 13.sp)
                                Text(launcher.packageName, color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 11.sp)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    modifier = Modifier.clickable { setAsDefault = !setAsDefault }
                ) {
                    Checkbox(
                        checked = setAsDefault,
                        onCheckedChange = { setAsDefault = it },
                        colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = theme.accent)
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    Text("設為 ${state.game.platform} 平台的預設模擬器", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 12.sp)
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            SylphoraButton(
                onClick = {
                    if (selectedLauncherId.isNotBlank()) {
                        onSelectLauncher(selectedLauncherId, setAsDefault)
                    }
                },
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent)
            ) {
                Text("啟動遊戲", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            SylphoraTextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("取消", color = theme.textSecondary)
            }
        },
        containerColor = theme.surface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
fun NoEmulatorDialog(
    game: GameEntity,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val theme = LocalPegasusTheme.current
    val context = LocalContext.current

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text("未安裝 ${game.platform} 模擬器", color = theme.textMain, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 16.sp)
        },
        text = {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                Text("系統中未偵測到能夠執行 ${game.platform} 遊戲的模擬器應用程式。", color = theme.textMain, fontSize = 13.sp)
                Text("您可以前往 Google Play 搜尋安裝對應模擬器 (如 RetroArch 或獨立模擬器)，或至設定頁關聯外部 App。", color = theme.textSecondary, fontSize = 12.sp)
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            SylphoraButton(
                onClick = {
                    onDismiss()
                    try {
                        val playStoreIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("market://search?q=${game.platform}+emulator"))
                        playStoreIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                        context.startActivity(playStoreIntent)
                    } catch (_: Exception) {
                        val webIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("https://play.google.com/store/search?q=${game.platform}+emulator"))
                        webIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                        context.startActivity(webIntent)
                    }
                },
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = theme.accent)
            ) {
                Text("前往 Play 商店搜尋", color = theme.background, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            SylphoraTextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("關閉", color = theme.textSecondary)
            }
        },
        containerColor = theme.surface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)
    )
}

================================================================================
package com.example.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.runtime.staticCompositionLocalOf
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color

enum class FontSize(val scale: Float) {
    SMALL(0.85f),
    MEDIUM(1.0f),
    LARGE(1.15f)
}

enum class LauncherLayoutMode(val displayName: String, val desc: String) {
    II_SU_CAROUSEL("iiSU 流體輪播", "大焦點高光毛玻璃卡片輪播"),
    PEGASUS_GRID("天馬海報網格", "多欄海報牆網格佈局"),
    ESDE_WHEEL_LIST("ES-DE 輪盤列表", "左側遊戲清單，右側大尺寸封面與資訊"),
    SWITCH_TILES("Switch 平鋪方塊", "極簡單列方塊橫向滑動"),
    NIGHT_MUSEUM("夜間博物館", "沉浸式暗黑展品展覽視圖")
}

data class PegasusTheme(
    val id: String,
    val name: String,
    val isDark: Boolean,
    val background: Color,
    val surface: Color,
    val accent: Color,
    val textMain: Color,
    val textSecondary: Color,
    val cardStart: Color,
    val cardEnd: Color,
    val customWallpaperUri: String? = null,
    val glassmorphismAlpha: Float = 0.65f,
    val tertiary: Color = accent,
    val outline: Color = textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.35f)
)

data class ThemeFamily(
    val name: String,
    val dark: PegasusTheme,
    val light: PegasusTheme
)

val LocalPegasusTheme = staticCompositionLocalOf { SylphoraDark }

fun PegasusTheme.toMaterialColorScheme(): androidx.compose.material3.ColorScheme {
    return if (isDark) {
        androidx.compose.material3.darkColorScheme(
            primary = accent,
            onPrimary = if (accent == RetroMuseumDarkPrimary) RetroMuseumDarkOnPrimary else Color.White,
            primaryContainer = cardStart,
            onPrimaryContainer = textMain,
            secondary = accent,
            onSecondary = Color.White,
            secondaryContainer = cardEnd,
            onSecondaryContainer = textSecondary,
            tertiary = tertiary,
            onTertiary = RetroMuseumDarkOnTertiary,
            tertiaryContainer = RetroMuseumDarkTertiaryContainer,
            onTertiaryContainer = RetroMuseumDarkOnTertiaryContainer,
            background = background,
            onBackground = textMain,
            surface = surface,
            onSurface = textMain,
            surfaceVariant = cardStart,
            onSurfaceVariant = textSecondary,
            outline = outline,
            outlineVariant = RetroMuseumDarkOutlineVariant
        )
    } else {
        androidx.compose.material3.lightColorScheme(
            primary = accent,
            onPrimary = Color.White,
            primaryContainer = cardStart,
            onPrimaryContainer = textMain,
            secondary = accent,
            onSecondary = Color.White,
            secondaryContainer = cardEnd,
            onSecondaryContainer = textSecondary,
            tertiary = tertiary,
            onTertiary = RetroMuseumLightOnTertiary,
            tertiaryContainer = RetroMuseumLightTertiaryContainer,
            onTertiaryContainer = RetroMuseumLightOnTertiaryContainer,
            background = background,
            onBackground = textMain,
            surface = surface,
            onSurface = textMain,
            surfaceVariant = cardStart,
            onSurfaceVariant = textSecondary,
            outline = outline,
            outlineVariant = RetroMuseumLightOutlineVariant
        )
    }
}
